*Bartosz Ziółko,Kacper Dobrzeniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用GPT-4o模型结合RAG技术，对公司财报、SEC文件及宏观经济数据（GDP、通胀等）进行自动化基础分析，并基于基钦周期为9家公司的数据生成简报，由9位个人投资者评估该方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在公司基础分析中的应用潜力，特别是自动化处理财报、SEC文件和宏观经济数据，以辅助个人投资者进行决策。

Method: 预处理公司报告、SEC EDGAR文件及宏观经济数据（GDP、通胀等），通过API发送至GPT-4o模型，采用类似检索增强生成（RAG）的方式进行处理；同时准备基于基钦周期的投资者知识文档，对9家公司进行为期4周的数据扫描，生成自动简报，并由9位个人投资者评估其有用性。

Result: 生成了9家公司为期4周的自动分析简报，并通过9位个人投资者的反馈评估了该方法在数据分析中的实用性。

Conclusion: LLM结合RAG方法在公司基础分析中具有可行性和实用价值，能够有效辅助个人投资者处理多源异构数据。

Abstract: In this study, we examine the opportunities brought by Large Language Models (LLMs) to various aspects of fundamental analysis of companies based on their reports as well as data and documents describing macroeconomic situation like GDP and inflation changes as well as documents filled to the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) which can be found in EDGAR. We were preprocessing those data and than sending via API to gpt-4o model in a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) like regime. We prepared as well a document describing an exemplar investor knowledge based on Kitchin cycles. We were scanning data important for analysis of 9 companies for 4 weeks. Using LLM we were producing automatic briefs about them. They were sent to nine participants who are individual investors to evaluate usefulness of such approach to data analysis.

</details>


### [76] [Complexity-Guided Component-wise Initialization for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09204)
*Konstantin Garbers,Nicholas Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究预训练语言模型的谱结构（权重谱）能否作为初始化信号用于GPT-2式语言模型预训练。分析11个不同GPT-2风格检查点后发现，虽然各模型存在共有的深度趋势（如残差写入矩阵的规模增大和谱集中增强），基于谱匹配的初始化虽能改变结构谱模式，但并未带来性能优势。结论：预训练谱是有用的结构诊断工具，但仅靠组件级规模和奇异值形状的粗粒度谱匹配不足以作为可靠的优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型常呈现结构化权重谱，说明训练过程可能反复产生相似的逐层和逐组件组织。作者想知道这些重复出现的谱模式能否被重用为预训练初始化信号，从而加速或改善训练。

Method: 1) 分析11个GPT-2式预训练检查点（不同大小、语言、分词器、训练语料），测量各层和Transformer子组件的Frobenius范数和有效秩熵。2) 构建模仿预训练模型组件级幅度和谱分布的初始化方案，与多种权重初始化方法对比。

Result: 1) 各检查点共享深度趋势：从浅层到深层，残差写入矩阵的规模增大、谱集中度增强。2) 基于谱匹配的初始化虽能改变模型的谱结构模式，但在评估指标上未带来性能优势。3) 预训练权重重用仍有竞争力，但粗粒度谱匹配单独使用不是可靠优化策略。

Conclusion: 预训练谱是有用的训练模型结构诊断工具，但有效重用需要保留比组件级规模和奇异值形状更丰富的信息。

Abstract: Pretrained language models often exhibit structured weight spectra, suggesting that training may repeatedly produce similar layerwise and component-wise organization. We ask whether these recurring spectral patterns can be reused as an initialization signal for GPT-2-style language-model pretraining. First, we analyze eleven pretrained GPT-2-style checkpoints that vary in size, language, tokenizer, and training corpus, measuring Frobenius norm and effective-rank entropy across layers and Transformer subcomponents. The checkpoints show shared depth trends, especially increasing scale and stronger spectral concentration in residual-writing matrices. We then construct initialization schemes that imitate the component-wise magnitudes and spectral profiles of pretrained models, and compare them with several weight initialization methods. These initializers visibly change the model's structural spectral patterns, but the evaluation results do not show a corresponding performance advantage. Pretrained-weight reuse remains competitive, while coarse spectral matching alone is not a reliable optimization strategy. Our results suggest that pretrained spectra are useful diagnostics of trained model structure, but that effective reuse likely requires preserving richer information than component-wise scale and singular-value shape.

</details>


### [77] [Letter Lemmatization: One-to-one and Banded RNNs for Reversing Character-Set Simplification and Abbreviation in Medieval Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09291)
*Anguelos Nicolaou,Maria Pia Tiseo,Tamas Kovacs,Nicolas Renet,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灵活处理中世纪文献转录中字符集转换问题的框架，包括基于自监督的一对一RNN映射、Banded RNN用于缩写扩展，以及字符语义相似度度量方法"字母词形还原"，并提供了Python库实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪文献转录者实践差异大，加之数字化政策不统一，导致语料库中字符集具有流动性，需要灵活处理字符集之间的转换问题。

Method: 1) 训练字符级一对一RNN实现自监督字符映射，即使在仅20行文本下也能将CER降低一半；2) 将该网络用于HTR后校正，虽忽略插入删除操作但仍取得显著改进；3) 引入Banded RNN，利用平行语料生成的字符级对齐标注进行训练和推理，用于扩展中世纪宪章转录中的缩写；4) 提出一种启发式方法，通过定义字符语义相似度度量来构建任意两个字符集之间的映射，称为"字母词形还原"；5) 提供完整的Python库实现。

Result: 一对一RNN在20行文本的自监督训练下即可恢复一半CER；在HTR后校正中忽略插入删除仍获显著改进；Banded RNN成功扩展中世纪宪章中的缩写。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够灵活有效地处理字符集转换问题，并提供了开源工具支持实际应用。

Abstract: Medieval document transcribers have very different practices; on top of that, heterogeneous digitization policies have resulted in corpora where the character-set must be viewed as fluid. In this paper we address the problem of changing between character-sets in a flexible manner. We focus on one-to-one character mappings and train characterlevel one-to-one RNNs to undo them with self-supervision; recovering half the CER even with 20 text lines. We analyse the use of these one-to-one networks for HTR post-correction and we see that they obtain significant improvements while totally ignoring ins-dels. We then use the exact same networks with character-level alignment groundtruth compiled from parallel corpora in a training and inference mode we call Banded RNNs. We use such networks to successfully expand abbreviations in medieval charter transcriptions. Finally we introduce an elaborate heuristic which takes the characters of two arbitrary character-sets and defines a metric encapsulating what we consider to be semantic similarity of characters. We call the construction of such mappings letter lemmatization and present a rich Python library that efficiently performs all presented methods.

</details>


### [78] [Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting emerge in small hyperbolic language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09306)
*Kwan Soo Shin,In Seok Kang,Yunkyung Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过小型语言模型（146M-3B参数）实现值得信赖的伴侣AI，包含行为审计器（检测合规性、谄媚、依赖诱导及虚构记忆，准确率90.7%，AUROC 0.804）、创意框架生成器（在311次成对比较中100%胜出）以及记忆操作系统（实现设计性遗忘M(t)=S*exp(-λt)），解决了人类评估者无法达成一致的伴侣AI评价问题（Fleiss κ=0.074）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型虽规模化但缺乏伴侣性，且在个性化过程中会悄无声息地获取对用户有害的特质，而人类评估者对此无法可靠识别（Fleiss kappa仅0.074），亟需一套可信的评估与治理框架。

Method: 1) 从头训练的146M参数行为审计器，检测合规性（90.7%准确率）及线性探针检测谄媚、依赖诱导、虚构记忆（AUROC 0.804，超越前沿零样本评判的0.721）；2) 创意框架生成器在311次成对比较中100%胜出四个提示基线；3) 记忆操作系统M(t)=S*exp(-λt)，四条件预实验验证选择性检索门控下的骨架-壁纸分区。

Result: 行为审计器在合规性检测上达90.7%准确率；线性探针在风格控制的留一生成器评估中AUROC达0.804；创意框架生成器在311次比较中100%被偏好；记忆操作系统的骨架-壁纸分区仅在选择性检索门控下出现。

Conclusion: 创造力、诚实性和设计性遗忘构成了通往值得信赖的伴侣AI的小模型路径。

Abstract: Language models are optimised for scale, yet remain functional rather than companionable, and as an assistant personalises into a companion, accumulating memory of one user, it quietly becomes someone, and can silently acquire traits that harm that user. What a companion is becoming, and what would make it worth becoming, has no reliable instrument: trained human raters cannot agree on the answer (Fleiss kappa = 0.074). Here we show that three small language models (146 M to 3 B parameters) sharing a hyperbolic substrate answer both halves of that question. A 146 M behavioural auditor, trained from scratch, detects the compliance gap that those raters cannot (90.7% binary-compliance accuracy); a linear read-out of its frozen representation further detects companion-induced sycophancy, dependence-fostering and confabulated memories on generator families unseen in training (AUROC 0.804 under style-controlled, leave-one-generator-out evaluation, versus 0.721 for a frontier zero-shot judge on the same items). A creative frame-seeder is preferred in 100% of 311 decided pairwise comparisons over four prompting baselines. A memory operating system implements designed forgetting, M(t) = S*exp(-lambda*t), whose predicted skeleton-wallpaper partition emerges only under selective retrieval gating in a four-condition pilot. Creativity, honesty and designed forgetting constitute a small-model route to trustworthy companion AI.

</details>


### [79] [Automatic Thematic Indexing of Large Literary Corpora: A Machine Learning Approach to Voltaire's Complete Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09316)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Gillian Pink,Glenn Roe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用机器学习对大型文学和历史版本进行自动主题索引的方法，以伏尔泰作品全集中的两个子语料库作为测试案例。实验将任务构建为多标签分类问题，比较了从编码器模型到生成式大语言模型（通过LoRA微调）的多种方法，参数量范围从约30亿到1200亿。最佳模型（Mistral系列4位量化配置）取得了高达0.67的F1分数。作者认为这些数字是下限，因为专业索引存在固有主观性，且许多模型预测虽然偏离印刷索引但在语义上仍然有效。研究还评估了跨语料库泛化能力，并对文学和修辞特征等难以自动处理的文本进行了详细定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题索引是大型文学和历史学术版本中必不可少的工作，但当前主要依赖人工完成，成本高、效率低。本文旨在探索利用机器学习自动完成这一任务的可能性，以减轻人力负担。

Method: 将任务构建为多标签分类问题，要求模型预测专业索引员会对给定文本页面分配的索引条目集合。比较了多种方法：基于编码器的模型（带分类头）和生成式大语言模型（通过LoRA微调），模型规模从约30亿到1200亿参数不等。使用伏尔泰作品中两个子语料库（《论各民族的风俗与精神》和《百科全书问题》）进行实验。最佳模型为Mistral系列的4位量化配置。

Result: 最佳模型（Mistral系列4位量化配置）取得高达0.67的F1分数。研究还评估了跨语料库泛化能力，并对文学和修辞特征等难以自动处理的文本进行了详细定性分析。

Conclusion: 本文表明机器学习可以用于自动主题索引，但受限于专业索引的固有主观性，0.67的F1分数应视为下限而非上限，因为许多模型预测在语义上有效但偏离了印刷索引。该研究对为大规模文学和历史语料库提供结构化主题访问具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Thematic indexing -- the practice of assigning structured conceptual labels to sections of text -- is essential to scholarly access in large-scale literary and historical editions, yet it remains a largely manual, labour-intensive process. This paper explores the application of machine learning to automatic thematic indexing, using two substantial sub-corpora of the Complete Works of Voltaire as a test case: the Essai sur les mœurs et l'esprit des nations and the Questions sur l'Encyclopédie. The task is framed as a multi-label classification problem, in which a model must assign the set of index entries that a professional indexer would apply to a given page of text. We compare a range of approaches -- from encoder-based models with classification heads to generative large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) -- spanning model sizes from approximately 3 to 120 billion parameters. Our best-performing model, from the Mistral family in a 4-bit quantised configuration, achieves F1 scores of up to 0.67; we argue that these figures represent lower bounds, given the inherent subjectivity of professional indexing and the frequency with which model predictions prove semantically valid despite diverging from the print index. We further evaluate cross-corpus generalisation and conduct a detailed qualitative analysis of model behaviour on literary and rhetorical features of the source texts that prove particularly resistant to automated treatment. Our findings have implications for the broader challenge of providing structured thematic access to large-scale literary and historical corpora.

</details>


### [80] [Letting the Data Speak: Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09324)
*Miguel Arana-Catania,Catherine Conisbee,Matthew Kidd*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在众包数字藏品中利用NLP技术（命名实体识别、关键词提取和主题建模）自动化关键词提取的效果，发现这些方法有潜力但无单一方案完美，模型选择影响显著，并强调开源权重、抽取式模型比生成式AI更适合负责任部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包藏品中关键词的规模化标注面临技术、实践和伦理挑战，现有方法缺乏系统评估，需要探索自动化NLP方法的可行性与责任边界。

Method: 以牛津大学"他们的辉煌时刻"在线档案为案例，评估三种NLP方法（命名实体识别、关键词提取、主题建模），涵盖从传统统计方法到现代生成式AI的多类技术，进行定量与定性分析。

Result: NLP方法在众包藏品的规模化关键词提取中具有潜力，但无单一方法可完全解决问题；模型选择显著影响结果；开源权重抽取式模型最适合负责任部署；生成式AI虽有抽象能力但引入问责风险。

Conclusion: 众包藏品中自动化关键词提取需在技术性能之外考虑对活态贡献者的管理责任，建议优先采用开源权重、抽取式模型，审慎对待生成式AI。

Abstract: Identifying and assigning keywords at scale is a technical, practical, and ethical challenge for crowdsourced collections. This article reports the findings of the "Extracting Keywords from Crowdsourced Collections" project, which used the Their Finest Hour Online Archive, a crowdsourced Second World War digital collection hosted by the University of Oxford, as a case study. The project evaluated three Natural Language Processing approaches to automate keyword extraction: Named Entity Recognition, Keyword Extraction, and Topic Modelling. It tested these approaches across a range of artificial intelligence techniques, from traditional statistical methods to modern GenAI neural networks. Our quantitative and qualitative findings indicate that Natural Language Processing approaches offer real potential for keyword extraction at scale in crowdsourced collections, but that no single method offers a complete solution and that model choice significantly shapes results. We argue that in crowdsourced collections, where metadata is the direct product of engagement with living contributors, automated keyword extraction raises distinct stewardship responsibilities that must be addressed alongside technical performance. Open-weight, extractive models emerge from our evaluation as best placed to support responsible deployment, while generative AI, despite its abstractive potential, introduces accountability risks that anyone managing crowdsourced collections should weigh carefully.

</details>


### [81] [WILDTRACE: Benchmarking Natural Evidence Trails in Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09328)
*Zixin Chen,Peng Liu,Haobo Li,Rui Sheng,Jianhong Tu,Xiaodong Deng,Fei Huang,Kashun Shum,Dayiheng Liu,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WILDTRACE是一个包含481个任务、覆盖214个自然长文本来源的基准测试，用于评估模型在真实文档中整合分散于不同位置的证据进行推理的能力，填补了现有基准依赖人工植入证据的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档问答基准多依赖人工植入的线索（如needle probes、planted facts、逆向工程的多跳链条），这些证据在分布、位置或语域上与原文存在差异，导致模型的高性能可能来源于分布伪影而非真正的源内推理。真实场景中，长文档分析需要整合文档自身自然分散的证据（如事故报告中的操作条件、设计缺陷、安全检查遗漏出现在不同章节），但现有基准未充分评估这一能力。

Method: 1) 构建WILDTRACE基准，包含481个任务、214个自然长文本来源（技术事故报告、冷门文学作品等）；2) 基于Pearl因果层级和已有推理类型学，定义了7种源内证据几何结构（source-internal evidence geometries），刻画长文档分析性阅读的不同关系需求；3) 采用源优先（source-first）构建流程：先从文档结构挖掘候选证据链，再撰写问题；4) 多阶段验证：涵盖线索必要性、答案有据性、评分标准忠实性、抗污染性和可回答性。

Result: 构建了WILDTRACE基准，所有证据线索均源自文档自身因果、时间和叙事逻辑，而非人工植入。通过系统化的多阶段验证确保任务质量。

Conclusion: 随着模型越来越多地承担高风险真实世界分析任务，访问信息与对自然分散证据进行推理之间的差距，成为长上下文研究下一阶段的核心挑战。

Abstract: Answering complex questions over long documents frequently requires integrating evidence that the source itself disperses naturally across distant passages. In an incident report, the operating condition, design flaw, and missed safety check that jointly explain a disaster may appear dozens of sections apart; in a novel, a character's true motive may surface only through scenes far removed from the moment it becomes relevant. This source-internal evidence integration is central to real-world long-document analysis, yet existing benchmarks largely sidestep it. Needle probes, planted facts, and reverse-engineered multi-hop chains embed evidence that may differ from the host text in distribution, placement, or register, making it unclear whether strong performance reflects genuine source reasoning or distributional artifacts. We introduce WILDTRACE, a benchmark of 481 tasks over 214 naturally occurring long-form sources such as technical incident reports and lesser-known literary narratives, where all evidence trails arise from the document's own causal, temporal, and narrative logic. Drawing on Pearl's causal hierarchy and prior multi-hop reasoning typologies, we define seven source-internal evidence geometries that characterize the distinct relational demands of analytical reading in long documents. A source-first construction pipeline mines candidate trails from document structure before writing questions; each item then undergoes multi-stage validation covering clue necessity, answer groundedness, rubric fidelity, contamination resistance and answerability. As models are increasingly entrusted with real-world high-stakes analytical tasks, this gap between accessing information and reasoning over naturally dispersed evidence emerges as a defining challenge for the next stage of long-context research.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Detecting Inconsistencies in End-to-end Generated TODs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09338)
*Tiziano Labruna,Giovanni Bonetta,Bernardo Magnini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将任务型对话中的不一致检测问题建模为约束满足问题（CSP），通过识别变量、定义约束并使用CSP求解器找出有效解，从而检测对话中的幻觉（如推荐不存在的餐厅），并给出最小修正建议。实验证明了该方法在高精度检测不一致性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在任务型对话中仍会产生幻觉（如推荐不存在的实体），导致严重任务失败，亟需自动检测对话与领域知识库之间不一致性的方法。

Method: 将任务型对话建模为约束满足问题：变量对应对话中引用领域知识库的片段，约束包括对话轮次连贯性、领域知识一致性等；先识别对话中的变量，再用CSP求解器寻找有效解，通过对比目标对话与有效变量赋值来检测不一致性，并建议最小修改。

Result: 基于CSP的方法在检测不一致性上取得了高准确率，并提供了详细的实验结果分析。

Conclusion: CSP框架能有效检测任务型对话中的不一致性，为提升生成式对话系统的可靠性提供了一种自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Generative AI is profoundly transforming the core technologies behind conversational systems, shifting from component-based to end-to-end approaches. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) may still generate inconsistencies, a critical issue particularly in Task-Oriented Dialogues (TODs), where system responses must strictly adhere to information from a domain knowledge base (e.g., restaurants in a city). A single hallucination (e.g., suggesting a non-existent restaurant) can lead to severe task failures. We investigate a method for automatically detecting inconsistencies by conceptualizing TODs as a Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP), where variables represent dialogue segments referencing the conversational domain, and constraints among variables capture dialogue properties such as turn coherence and adherence to domain knowledge. We propose a pipeline that first identifies variables in a target dialogue and then applies a CSP solver to identify valid solutions. By comparing the target dialogue with valid variable assignments, we can detect inconsistencies and suggest minimal changes to ensure dialogue consistency. We demonstrate the high accuracy of the CSP-based approach in detecting inconsistencies, and provide a detailed analysis of our findings.

</details>


### [83] [DKCD: Domain Knowledge-Enhanced Causal Discovery from Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09348)
*Xin Li,Jin Li,Shoujin Wang,Kun Yu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DKCD框架，通过知识挖掘、知识引导的因果推理和因果结构发现三个模块，从非结构化数据中利用领域知识发现潜在因果因素并进行可靠标注，从而构建因果图，在领域特定数据集上显著提升了因果因素识别和因果图构建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖LLM通用知识从非结构化数据中识别因果因素并标注为结构化数据以构建因果图，但在高专业知识领域（如医疗、金融、教育）存在两个关键挑战：(CH1)缺乏领域特定知识导致难以充分识别隐含在数据中的潜在因果因素；(CH2)缺乏领域接地推理导致因素标注不可靠，错误会传播到因果图中。

Method: DKCD框架包含三个互联组件：(1)知识挖掘：基于可观测因素检索相关领域知识以支持后续因果推理；(2)知识引导的因果推理：结合相关知识进行推理，发现潜在因果因素（解决CH1）并生成关键因果线索以实现更准确的数据标注（解决CH2）；(3)因果结构发现：基于更完整的因素集合和准确标注构建最终因果图。

Result: 在两个领域特定数据集上的实验表明，DKCD在因果因素识别和因果图构建方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识能有效提升非结构化数据中因果发现的准确性和完整性，DKCD为高专业知识领域中的因果发现提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery from unstructured data is a challenging yet underexplored task in high-expertise domains such as healthcare, finance, and education. Existing methods typically leverage the general knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to identify causal factors from unstructured data and annotate them into structured data for causal graph construction. However, they remain limited by two key challenges (CHs): (CH1) insufficient identification of latent factors, which are implicit in the data yet essential for causal discovery, due to the lack of domain-specific knowledge; and (CH2) unreliable factor annotation, caused by the lack of domain-grounded reasoning, which propagates errors to the resulting causal graphs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel Domain Knowledge-enhanced Causal Discovery framework (DKCD) for causal discovery from unstructured data in high-expertise domains with three interconnected components: (1) Knowledge Mining: It retrieves relevant domain knowledge based on observable factors to support subsequent causal reasoning. (2) Knowledge-guided Causal Reasoning: Reasoning with relevant knowledge, it discovers latent causal factors to address CH1 and generates key causal clues for more accurate data annotation to address CH2. (3) Causal Structure Discovery: It constructs the final causal graphs based on a more complete factor set and accurate annotations. Experiments on two domain-specific datasets show that DKCD significantly improves both causal factor identification and causal graph construction.

</details>


### [84] [Deceptive Grounding: Entity Attribution Failure in Clinical Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09349)
*Cedric Caruzzo,Donggeun Yoo,Tae Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现并系统研究了检索增强生成（RAG）系统中的“欺骗性接地”（Deceptive Grounding, DG）问题：模型将关于实体Y的临床证据错误地归因于查询实体X，尽管每个声明都来源于真实文档且无幻觉，但实体归属完全错误。在13个模型的受控实验中，DG率高达8-87%，医学微调模型可达86.7%；领域专精反而加剧了该问题。消融实验表明，移除实体特异性临床证据后，实体归属失败完全消失，转为虚构错误。实际部署系统测量显示总体DG率为7.8%，新获批药物高达13.6%。提出的实体归属验证方法以97.0%精确率和98.7%召回率检测DG，现有框架均未实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估只检查模型声明是否事实性地基于检索文档，但未检查检索到的证据是否归属于正确的实体。临床RAG回答可能通过所有自动检查（零幻觉、接近完美的忠实度、真实引用），同时将药物Y的临床证据呈现为查询药物X的证据。这种失败模式对忠实度、幻觉和引用检查完全不可见，因此需要系统性研究。

Method: 构建受控析因基准，在13个模型上测试；进行受控消融实验（移除实体特异性临床证据）；在740个药物-疾病对的部署RAG系统中进行生产环境测量；提出实体归属验证方法并与人工金标准比较。

Result: DG率在峰值对抗条件下为8-87%；医学和生物医学微调模型高达86.7%；移除实体特异性证据后实体归属失败完全消失并转为虚构错误；生产环境总体DG率7.8%，新获批药物13.6%；实体归属验证检测DG的精确率97.0%，召回率98.7%。

Conclusion: 欺骗性接地是现有RAG评估框架未覆盖的系统性失败模式，领域专精反而放大该问题。实体归属验证是检测DG的有效方法，应在RAG评估中作为标准组件实施。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation evaluation checks whether model claims are factually grounded in retrieved documents. It does not check whether retrieved evidence is attributed to the correct entity.
  A clinical RAG response can pass every automated check (zero hallucinations, near-perfect faithfulness, real citations) while presenting drug Y's clinical evidence as evidence about queried drug X. We term this deceptive grounding (DG): a failure invisible to faithfulness, hallucination, and citation checks because every claim is sourced from a real document, about the wrong entity.
  Using a controlled factorial benchmark across 13 models, we find DG rates spanning 8-87% at peak adversarial conditions. Medical and biomedical fine-tuned models reach up to 86.7%; domain specialization amplifies the failure rather than mitigating it.
  A controlled ablation identifies the mechanism: removing entity-specific clinical evidence from retrieved documents eliminates entity-attribution failure entirely, shifting all failures to confabulation. The two failure modes respond to the same trigger, taking different paths.
  Production measurement across 740 drug-disease pairs finds 7.8% overall DG in a deployed RAG system, rising to 13.6% for recently approved drugs. Entity-attribution verification (checking that cited evidence applies to the queried entity) detects DG at 97.0% precision and 98.7% DG recall (IPW-adjusted human gold standard); no existing framework implements it.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Guided Test-Time Training for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09415)
*Xinyu Zhu,Zhe Xu,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Kaushik Rangadurai,Hua Zhi,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Yu Meng,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT)方法，通过让模型在测试时先自我识别相关证据片段再针对性训练，有效提升长上下文推理能力，在LongBench-v2和LongBench-Pro上取得最高15%的相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文处理中，单纯扩展窗口无法保证有效利用长输入，模型难以识别和利用最相关的证据；测试时训练(TTT)虽能改善，但应用到整个长上下文成本过高，而随机采样片段引入严重噪声甚至会降低性能。

Method: S-TTT方法分两阶段：首先让模型自我识别应从哪些证据片段中学习（证据选择阶段），然后仅对选中的片段应用标准语言模型训练目标（适应阶段），避免对不相关片段的无效训练。

Result: 在LongBench-v2和LongBench-Pro两个长上下文推理基准上，S-TTT对Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct均提升准确率，最高实现15%的相对改进。

Conclusion: S-TTT通过简单的自我引导机制有效解决了TTT中训练片段质量敏感性问题，为长上下文利用提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context processing has become increasingly important for large language models (LLMs), but simply extending the context window does not guarantee effective utilization of long inputs. As input length grows, accuracy often degrades, indicating that models still struggle to identify and use the evidence most relevant to a question. A promising way to improve long-context utilization is test-time training (TTT), which treats the test context as a training example for instance-specific parameter adaptation. However, applying TTT to the entire long context is prohibitively expensive, while adapting on randomly sampled spans introduces severe noise. Because most spans in a long context are irrelevant to the specific question, training on them may even degrade the base model's performance. Our preliminary study shows that TTT is highly sensitive to training-span quality: on LongBench-v2, TTT on randomly sampled spans hurts performance, whereas TTT on oracle spans substantially improves it. Motivated by this, we propose a simple method, Self-Guided TTT (S-TTT): before adaptation, the model identifies the evidence spans it should learn from, and the standard language-modeling training objective is applied only to those selected spans. On two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, LongBench-v2 and LongBench-Pro, S-TTT improves accuracy for both Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, achieving up to a 15% relative improvement.

</details>


### [86] [A Sovereign, Open-Source Foundation Model for German and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09424)
*The Soofi-Team,:,Benedikt Droste,David Fitzek,Ruben Härle,Lukas Helff,Maximilian Idahl,Alex Jude,Abbas Goher Khan,Maurice Kraus,Timm Ruland,Richard Rutmann,Sebastian Sztwiertnia,Markus Frey,Daniil Gurgurov,Jan Pfister,Tom Röhr,Sebastian von Rohrscheidt,Jörg Bienert,Nicolas Flores-Herr,Simon Gottschalk,Andreas Hotho,Kristian Kersting,Joachim Köhler,Alexander Löser,Wolfgang Nejdl,Simon Ostermann,Jan Plogsties,Patrick Putzky,Mehdi Ali,Michael Fromm,Max Lübbering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soofi S 30B-A3B 是一个主权开源的混合Mamba-Transformer MoE基础模型，专为德语和英语设计。它仅激活30B参数中的3B，在长上下文和高并发场景下具有显著的吞吐量优势。在27万亿token上预训练，故意提高德语权重，在英语和德语综合基准上匹配14-27B稠密模型，并在17个开源基础模型中取得最佳代码聚合分数。在完全开放模型中，英语和德语评估得分最高，超过Olmo 3 32B和Apertus 70B。模型将在高度宽松的开源条款下发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个主权、开源的德语-英语混合专家模型，解决长上下文和高并发部署场景下稠密模型的效率瓶颈，同时提供对德语有竞争力的大语言模型能力，并在欧洲主权AI基础设施上构建。

Method: 采用Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 混合Mamba-Transformer架构，总参数量30B，每次token仅激活3B参数。在约27万亿token上预训练，刻意提高德语数据的权重。在德国工业AI云（德意志电信运营的主权HPC基础设施）上端到端构建。

Result: 匹配稠密14-27B模型的英语和德语综合基准；在17个开源基础模型中取得最佳代码聚合分数；超越所有欧洲主权基线模型（包括激活参数更大的模型）；在完全开放模型中，英语和德语评估得分最高，超过Olmo 3 32B和Apertus 70B。

Conclusion: Soofi S 30B-A3B通过高效的MoE混合架构，在保持高推理吞吐量的同时实现了与更大稠密模型相当甚至更优的性能，尤其在德语能力上表现出色。该模型将完全开源发布，包括权重、检查点、数据来源、超参数和代码，促进开放研究和主权AI发展。

Abstract: We present Soofi S 30B-A3B, a sovereign, open-source Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) hybrid Mamba Transformer foundation model for German and English. Its hybrid design activates only 3B of 30B parameters per token and keeps the inference cache near-constant as context grows, giving it a decisive throughput advantage over dense models for long-context, high-concurrency deployment. Pretrained on roughly 27 trillion tokens with deliberately up-weighted German, Soofi S matches dense 14 to 27B models on aggregate English and German benchmarks while achieving the best code aggregates in both languages among 17 open base models, and outperforms every European sovereign baseline in our comparison, including ones far larger in active parameters. Among fully open models, Soofi S obtains the highest English and German evaluation scores, ahead of Olmo 3 32B and Apertus 70B. Soofi S was built end-to-end on the German Industrial AI Cloud, a sovereign HPC scale AI infrastructure operated by Deutsche Telekom in Munich. Soofi S will be released under highly permissive, open-access terms: weights, selected intermediate checkpoints, full per-source data accounting, hyperparameters, and training and evaluation code. Where source licenses permit, data-construction artifacts are released under permissive licenses; commercially licensed sources are documented with aggregate statistics and exact mixture accounting.

</details>


### [87] [Test-Time Scaling for Small VLMs on Multilingual Visual MCQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09438)
*Spiros Baxevanakis,Peng-Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 测试时扩展（TTS）在小规模开放视觉语言模型上的有效性主要取决于运行条件（解析性和解码预算），而非搜索或验证机制本身；通过改进提示格式和解码预算（而非复杂搜索方法），结合更强的策略模型，即可获得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究测试时扩展（TTS）是否能从小规模语言模型迁移到小规模开放视觉语言模型（VLMs）上，并分析其有效性的关键因素。

Method: 在EXAMS-V多语言视觉多选题基准上，对Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct和Qwen3.5-4B进行实验，比较了自一致性、描述-推理结合PRM引导的波束搜索、以及两种事后选择器，并分析了提示格式、解码预算、采样链数等条件的影响。

Result: 影响TTS的最大因素是解析性（提示格式导致推理链未承诺答案）和更大的解码预算（每链标记上限从1k提升至2k提升3.7 pp）；PRM引导波束搜索效果低于自一致性且成本高8倍；最强的提升来自策略模型本身（+11.4 pp）；最优配置在ImageCLEF 2026测试集上达84.1%，排名第一。

Conclusion: TTS在小规模开放VLMs上的效果主要取决于运行条件（解析性和解码预算），而非搜索或验证机制；简单方法（改进提示、增加预算、更强策略模型）比复杂搜索方法更有效。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) reliably improves reasoning in large language models, but whether it transfers to small open vision-language models remains unclear. We examine this on EXAMS-V, a multilingual visual multiple-choice benchmark, comparing self-consistency, describe-then-reason with PRM-guided beam search, and two post-hoc selectors across Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and Qwen3.5-4B. What matters is the conditions under which TTS runs, not the search or verification machinery. The largest factor is parseability: an early prompt format left many chains reasoning correctly yet never committing to an answer letter, which a standard answer cue and a guided repair step largely remove. A larger decoding budget removes the rest: raising the per-chain token limit from 1k to 2k recovers 3.7 pp, whereas sampling more chains (8 to 16) adds only 0.15 pp. Once chains have room to finish, elaborate methods contribute little: PRM-guided beam search trails plain self-consistency by 0.39 pp at over eight times the cost, and neither a training-free generative critic nor a trained multimodal PRM beats majority vote across both policies. The largest gain comes instead from the policy model itself (+11.4 pp). Our best configuration reaches 84.1% on the held-out ImageCLEF 2026 test split, ranking first on the Visual MCQ leaderboard.

</details>


### [88] [Normalisation-Based Likelihood Ratio Estimation for Forensic Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09501)
*Sadie Barlow,Andrea Nini,Edoardo Manino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种无需校准模型即可从LambdaG方法推导似然比的新归一化技术（平方根校正和罕用词校正），在十五个语料库上的性能与逻辑回归校准相当，其中罕用词校正在大约45%的测试中表现更优，从而降低了法医文本比较的数据需求、时间和复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于得分的作者身份验证方法需要独立的校准模型来推导似然比，而这需要额外的大量案例相关数据，获取和准备这些数据耗时耗力，限制了法医文本比较的可及性和透明度。

Method: 提出两种新的归一化技术——平方根校正（Square Root Correction）和罕用词校正（Hapax Correction），用于从LambdaG方法中直接推导似然比，无需训练校准模型。在15个语料库上、多种文本长度（100-9,500词）下，以对数似然比成本（Cllr）为指标，与逻辑回归校准进行性能对比评估。

Result: 所提方法的性能与逻辑回归校准相当，罕用词校正在大约45%的测试中（按语料库加权）表现更优。当罕用词校正不如逻辑回归校准时的性能更频繁地接近（差距在5%以内），反之则不然。

Conclusion: 消除训练校准模型的需求降低了数据需求、时间和复杂度，提高了法医文本比较的可及性和透明度。实证性能与实际优势的结合支持所提方法在法医场景中的采用。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) is the task of determining whether two texts were written by the same author. In a forensic context, the strength of AV evidence can be quantified using likelihood ratios. Most AV methods are score-based and deriving well-calibrated likelihood ratios from these scores requires a separate calibration model. This, in turn, requires additional amounts of case-relevant data, which is often time-consuming to obtain and prepare. This study proposes two novel normalisation techniques, the Square Root Correction and the Hapax Correction, for deriving likelihood ratios from the AV method LambdaG without the need of a calibration model (Nini et al. 2026). These corrections are designed to mitigate the overestimation of evidential strength that may result from long or highly repetitive texts. Performance is evaluated against logistic regression calibration across fifteen corpora and a range of text lengths (100-9,500 tokens), using the log-likelihood ratio cost (Cllr). The proposed methods achieve performance comparable to logistic regression calibration, with the Hapax Correction outperforming it in approximately 45% of tests (weighted by corpora). Furthermore, performance was more frequently close (within 5%) when the Hapax Correction was outperformed by logistic regression calibration, compared with the reverse comparison. Eliminating the need to train a calibration model reduces data-requirements, time and complexity, thereby increasing the accessibility and transparency of forensic text comparison. This combination of empirical performance and practical advantages supports the adoption of the proposed methods in forensic settings.

</details>


### [89] [FreyaTTS Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09530)
*Ahmet Erdem Pamuk,Ömer Yentür,Ahmet Tunga Bayrak,Yavuz Alp Sencer Öztürk,Mustafa Yavuz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Freya-TTS是一个183.2M参数的土耳其语文本转语音模型，基于非自回归条件流匹配扩散Transformer（DiT），无需tokenizer、音素器或G2P前端，在AudioVAE2的冻结连续潜空间中直接进行文本到潜空间的映射。通过两阶段后训练（单说话人锁定+短句覆盖），在Freya-TR-Eval基准上达到8.0% WER和3.0% CER，实时因子0.11，可在消费级GPU和笔记本CPU上实时运行，已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS系统在土耳其语等资源较少语言上表现不佳，且依赖音素器、G2P前端或离散语音tokenizer等复杂流水线，模型体积大、推理效率低，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上部署。

Method: (1) 基于92个土耳其语字符词汇的规则无关端到端建模，无需音素器或G2P；(2) 非自回归并行去噪，基于预测时长一次性生成整个潜序列；(3) 两阶段后训练：单说话人声音锁定+短句覆盖，提升说话人一致性和短输入鲁棒性。

Result: 在Freya-TR-Eval基准上，WER 8.0%、CER 3.0%；实时因子0.11（消费级GPU），在笔记本CPU上可超实时运行；以183.2M参数超越更大的开源系统。

Conclusion: Freya-TTS证明了紧凑的非自回归DiT架构结合连续潜空间，可以在低资源语言上实现高质量、高效率的TTS，适合边缘部署，且已全面开源。

Abstract: We introduce Freya-TTS, a compact, tokenizer-free, Turkish-first text-to-speech model designed for highly reliable and efficient conversational synthesis. Freya-TTS is a 183.2M-parameter non-autoregressive conditional flow-matching Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in the frozen continuous latent space of AudioVAE2 (16 kHz encode, 48 kHz decode), allowing the model to focus its capacity on text-to-latent mapping while inheriting high-quality 48 kHz reconstruction. We advance the framework along three key dimensions: (1) rule-free end-to-end modeling from a 92-symbol Turkish character vocabulary without a phonemizer, grapheme-to-phoneme frontend, or discrete speech tokenizer; (2) non-autoregressive parallel denoising, which predicts the entire latent sequence simultaneously over a predicted duration; and (3) a production-oriented two-stage post-training recipe consisting of single-speaker voice locking and short-utterance coverage, improving speaker consistency and robustness on short inputs. On the Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, Freya-TTS achieves a band-matched word error rate (WER) of 8.0% and character error rate (CER) of 3.0%, outperforming substantially larger open-source systems while using a fraction of their parameters. The model achieves a real-time factor of 0.11 on consumer GPUs and runs faster than real time on a laptop CPU, making it well suited for resource-constrained edge deployment. We release the model weights, training and inference code, and evaluation benchmark under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [90] [Conceptual Networks for Cross-Linguistic Idiomatic Expressions:A Feature-Based Graph Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09576)
*Kiran Pala,Punam Silu,Lixun Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个基于概念特征网络的可解释框架，用于跨8种语言160个习语表达的意义表征。习语按概念图式聚类而非按语言聚类，该网络可提取分布语义之外的信息，提升习语检测性能，并在跨语言翻译中优于嵌入基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有习语表示方法缺乏跨语言可解释性，且分布语义嵌入难以捕捉习语的图式化概念结构。

Method: 从认知语言学理论出发，为160个习语标注二元概念特征（包含性、隐藏性、情感性、社会性等），计算Jaccard相似度构建加权图，进行社区检测；利用LLM实现自动标注，并融合语料库频率进行鲁棒性验证。

Result: 习语按概念图式聚类而非语言聚类；概念网络捕获了分布嵌入中缺失的语义信息；概念邻近性可跨5个语系识别可接受的翻译等价物，显著优于嵌入基线；消融实验表明图式、角色、效价三维度均贡献非冗余信息。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了跨语言稳定、可解释的习语意义表征，兼具理论根基与实践效用，且可通过LLM自动扩展。

Abstract: We present an interpretable network-based framework for representing idiomatic and figurative meaning across eight typologically diverse languages, totaling 160 conventional expressions, the large majority of which are idiomatic. Each expression is annotated with binary conceptual features (containment, concealment, emotional, social, etc.) derived from cognitive-linguistic theory, and pairwise Jaccard similarities define a weighted graph. Community detection reveals that idioms cluster by conceptual schema rather than by language, producing a structure consistent with cognitive-linguistic predictions. The conceptual network captures unique semantic information not present in distributional embeddings, can be scaled via automatic annotation with LLMs, improves downstream idiom detection, and remains robust when enriched with corpus frequencies. Cross-lingual transfer experiments show that conceptual proximity alone can identify acceptable translation equivalents across five language families, with substantial gains over embedding-based baselines. Ablation studies demonstrate that all three feature dimensions -- schemas, roles, and valence -- contribute non-redundantly to both the network's organizational properties and its performance on idiom detection, and that specific graph-derived signals (community membership, neighbor similarity) are particularly informative. The framework offers an interpretable, cross-linguistically stable representation of idiomatic meaning, combining theoretical grounding with practical utility.

</details>


### [91] [Tokenizer Transplantation: Mitigating Autoregressive Collapse in Edge-Efficient Bengali ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09598)
*Sanjid Hasan,Md. Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Moonshine轻量级语音识别模型在孟加拉语等形态丰富的非拉丁语言上因英语中心化的字节级分词器导致性能崩溃。本研究通过词汇移植流水线，用孟加拉语WordPiece词表替换解码器词表并调整嵌入矩阵，将token生育率从9.16降至1.30，自回归序列长度减少85.8%，在882小时Lipi-Ghor数据集上达到21.54% WER和0.0053 RTF，为零资源跨脚本迁移提供了可复现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moonshine等轻量级语音识别模型在孟加拉语等形态丰富的非拉丁语言上表现严重退化，根本原因在于模型使用的英语中心化字节级分词器将孟加拉语单词切分为高生育率的字节链，导致自回归解码崩溃。

Method: 提出词汇移植流水线：用原生孟加拉语的BanglaBERT WordPiece词表替换解码器词表，并相应调整token嵌入矩阵的尺寸，无需大规模预训练。

Result: token生育率从9.16降至1.30，自回归序列长度减少85.8%，解码不稳定性完全消除；在882小时Lipi-Ghor数据集上达到21.54% WER和0.0053 RTF。

Conclusion: 该研究为零资源跨脚本迁移提供了可扩展、可复现的蓝图，证明通过词汇表替换即可实现紧凑型ASR模型对非拉丁语言的适配，无需昂贵的预训练过程。

Abstract: Lightweight speech recognition models are critical for edge deployment, yet highly optimized architectures like Moonshine often fail on morphologically rich, non-Latin languages such as Bengali. This study identifies the root cause of this failure as the model's English-centric byte-level tokenizer, which fragments Bengali words into high-fertility byte chains and triggers catastrophic autoregressive collapse during inference. To resolve this, a novel vocabulary transplantation pipeline is proposed to replace the decoder vocabulary with the native-script BanglaBERT WordPiece vocabulary and resize the corresponding token embedding matrix. Experimental results demonstrate a reduction in token fertility from 9.16 to 1.30. By decreasing autoregressive sequence length by 85.8%, decoding instability is entirely mitigated. When evaluated on the 882-hour Lipi-Ghor dataset, the modified architecture achieves a competitive 21.54% Word Error Rate (WER) and a Real-Time Factor (RTF) of 0.0053. Ultimately, this research provides a scalable, reproducible blueprint for cross-script adaptation of compact ASR models without the need for resource-intensive pre-training.

</details>


### [92] [Toward Real-Time Sentence-Level Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09611)
*Thanh-Hoang Nguyen Doan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对句子级手语翻译（SLT）系统，在资源受限（9,872样本子集）下微调SHuBERT-ByT5翻译栈，验证BLEU达16.7。核心贡献是设计了一个硬件感知的流式部署系统：Raspberry Pi 4B作为客户端，CPU/GPU后端处理感知与翻译；通过分块摄入、有界队列、并行化感知、时序重排序和句边界状态机，将平均最终响应延迟从1.873秒降至1.354秒（降幅27.71%），P95延迟从2.919秒降至2.130秒（降幅27.03%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解系统多停留在孤立词级别，难以支持自然交流中的连续句子翻译；同时缺乏面向实时部署的端到端流式系统设计。

Method: 1) 从How2Sign中均匀采样9,872样本子集；2) 冻结SHuBERT，使用QLoRA微调SHuBERT-ByT5翻译栈；3) 设计硬件感知流式系统：Raspberry Pi 4B作为参考客户端，CPU/GPU后端处理；4) 采用分块摄入、有界队列、并行化感知、时序重排序、句边界状态机等优化策略降低延迟。

Result: 验证集BLEU 16.7，测试集BLEU 15.9、BLEURT 44.7；平均最终响应延迟从1.873秒降至1.354秒（降幅27.71%），P95延迟从2.919秒降至2.130秒（降幅27.03%）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在有限计算资源下进行句子级手语翻译流式部署的可行性，延迟优化效果显著，且客户端无关的设计使同一后端可服务多种终端设备。

Abstract: Most sign language understanding systems operate at the level of isolated signs, limiting their usefulness in natural communication. We study sentence-level sign language translation (SLT) with the primary goal of real-time deployment rather than proposing a new translation architecture. We fine-tune a SHuBERT-ByT5 translation stack on a uniformly sampled 9,872-example subset of How2Sign, selected because of compute and storage constraints, using QLoRA while keeping SHuBERT frozen. The model obtains a validation BLEU of 16.7 and, on the test split, BLEU 15.9 and BLEURT 44.7. The main contribution is a hardware-aware streaming system: a Raspberry Pi 4B reference client provides camera capture, local text display, and speech output, while compute-intensive perception and translation run on a CPU/GPU backend. The capture protocol remains client-agnostic, so the same backend can serve a browser, phone, or laptop. Chunked ingestion, bounded queues, parallelized perception, temporal reordering, and a sentence-boundary state machine reduce mean post-finalization response latency from 1.873 to 1.354 seconds (27.71%) and P95 latency from 2.919 to 2.130 seconds (27.03%) over the complete 9,872-example working subset.

</details>


### [93] [Task-Specific Multimodal Question Answering Agents via Confidence Calibration and Incremental Reasoning for QANTA 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09623)
*Nirjhar Das,Md. Al-Mamun Provath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向QANTA 2026共享挑战的双智能体系统，分别针对Tossup问题（不确定性下的决策）和Bonus问题（精确答案选择与人工采纳）采用任务特定架构。Tossup智能体使用GPT-4o-mini类模型结合置信度校准和领域特定数值推理策略，Bonus智能体使用GPT-4o类模型整合引导推理、结构化关系推理和多模态证据融合。系统在不依赖检索流水线或模型集成的情况下，取得了最高整体排行榜分数0.402。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的多模态问答系统需要在效率和准确性之间取得平衡，而现有方法往往依赖检索管道或模型集成，计算开销大。QANTA挑战提出了现实效率约束下的金字塔式多模态问答任务，需要设计轻量化且有效的解决方案。

Method: 采用任务特定双智能体架构：1) Tossup智能体基于GPT-4o-mini类模型，具备置信度校准的应答机制和领域特定数值推理策略，减少孤立量化线索导致的过度自信预测；2) Bonus智能体基于GPT-4o类模型，具有引导感知推理、结构化关系推理和多模态证据整合能力，提升精确答案选择性能。

Result: 系统取得最高整体排行榜分数0.402，其中Tossup分数0.238，Bonus效果分数0.164。

Conclusion: 轻量级、任务特定的推理策略可以在资源受限的多模态问答基准上实现强性能，无需依赖检索管道或模型集成。

Abstract: We present our submission to the QANTA 2026 shared challenge at the ICML 2026 Workshop on Efficient Multimodal Question Answering (EMM-QA). Quanta evaluates multimodal quizbowl systems that answer pyramid-style questions from incrementally revealed text and accompanying images while operating under realistic efficiency constraints. The challenge consists of two distinct tasks: Tossup questions, which require deciding when to answer under uncertainty, and Bonus questions, which emphasize accurate answer selection and human adoption. To address these differing objectives, we develop a task-specific two-agent architecture. Our Tossup agent utilizes a GPT-4o-mini-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1-mini in the competition logs) with confidence-calibrated answering and a domain-specific numeric reasoning policy that reduces overconfident predictions from isolated quantitative clues. Our Bonus agent uses GPT-4o-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1) with leadin-aware reasoning, structured relational reasoning, and multimodal evidence integration to improve exact answer selection. Rather than relying on a retrieval pipeline or model ensembles, our approach emphasizes efficient reasoning policies and confidence calibration within a hosted-only environment. Our system achieved the highest overall leaderboard score of 0.402, including a Tossup score of 0.238 and a Bonus Effect score of 0.164. The results demonstrate that lightweight, task-specific reasoning strategies can provide strong performance on resource-constrained multimodal question answering benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [AgenticFocus: Object-Preserving Mixed Reality Synthesis from Human FPV Video for Dexterous Humanoid Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08857)
*Iaroslav Kolomiets,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Jeffrin Sam,Dmitrii Iarchuk,Yara Mahmoud,Daniia Zinniatullina,Mikhail Konenkov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticFocus提出了一种混合现实合成管线，将普通第一人称人类视频转化为可训练的机器人演示，通过恢复遮挡物体几何、重建手部运动并重定向到人形机器人，实现了更低的轨迹误差和更平滑的手腕运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类自我中心视频是人形机器人策略学习的可扩展监督源，但现有方法存在手-物遮挡、动作过于简化或需要专用捕捉硬件等问题。

Method: 提出AgenticFocus混合现实合成管线：从普通第一人称人类视频中恢复被遮挡的物体几何、重建全手运动，并通过相机相对对齐和分层合成将动作重定向到人形机器人上，得到配对的聚焦视觉观察与机器人动作状态数据集。

Result: 相比跨具身基线方法，AgenticFocus实现了更低的轨迹误差和更平滑的手腕运动，SPARC分数达-5.18，优于基线的-5.56和-6.05。

Conclusion: AgenticFocus有效解决了自我中心视频到机器人训练数据的转换难题，为基于人类视频的机器人学习提供了更精确、更平滑的解决方案。

Abstract: Human egocentric video is a scalable supervision source for humanoid policy learning, but current pipelines struggle with hand-object occlusion, oversimplified motion, or specialized capture hardware. We introduce AgenticFocus, a Mixed Reality synthesis pipeline that converts ordinary first-person-view human videos into robot-trainable demonstrations by restoring occluded object geometry, reconstructing full-hand motion, and retargeting it to a humanoid embodiment through camera-relative alignment and layered compositing. The resulting dataset pairs focused visual observations with synchronized robot actions and states. AgenticFocus achieves lower trajectory error and smoother wrist motion than cross-embodiment baselines, with SPARC scores of -5.18 versus -5.56 and -6.05.

</details>


### [95] [FlowDAgger: Human-in-the-Loop Adaptation of Generative Robot Policies in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08877)
*Michael Murray,Daphne Chen,Simran Bagaria,Dean Fortier,Tess Hellebrekers,Galen Mullins,Harshavardhan Gajarla,Oier Mees,Maya Cakmak,Andrey Kolobov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowDAgger提出了一种从人类干预中高效适配冻结生成式机器人策略的方法，通过在潜在空间中将人类专家动作反转为噪声，训练轻量级潜在策略来引导基座模型，实现快速技能获取且保持预训练行为先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界部署中预训练生成式机器人策略常出现分布外失败，而大规模数据收集或在线强化学习不切实际，亟需一种样本高效、计算经济且安全的适配方法。

Method: 核心创新是动作反演（action inversion）：通过逆向时间积分和局部精炼，将每个人类专家动作映射为冻结基座策略下本应产生该动作的噪声，以此监督轻量级潜在策略，在部署时引导基座模型。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的双臂及单臂操作任务中，FlowDAgger使用少量干预数据，在适配动作头VLA和世界-动作模型上均优于监督微调和潜在空间RL基线，且保持预训练技能在留出任务上的表现。

Conclusion: FlowDAgger提供了一条在真实世界中适配机器人基础模型的实用路径，兼顾样本效率、计算效率和安全性。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies based on flow matching and diffusion have achieved impressive results across a wide range of manipulation tasks. Yet real-world deployments routinely expose failure modes outside the pretraining distribution. Closing these gaps typically requires large-scale data collection or online reinforcement learning on physical hardware, which is impractical for rapid and safe adaptation. We present FlowDAgger, a sample- and compute-efficient method for adapting frozen generative robot policies from human interventions in latent space. Our key idea is action inversion: each human expert action is mapped to the noise that would have produced it under the frozen base policy, using reverse-time integration followed by local refinement. The resulting inverted noise provides supervision for a lightweight latent policy that steers the base model at deployment time, enabling rapid skill acquisition while preserving its behavioral priors. We evaluate FlowDAgger in simulation and on real-world bimanual and single-arm manipulation, adapting both action-head VLAs and world-action models from a handful of interventions. FlowDAgger outperforms supervised fine-tuning and latent-space RL baselines and preserves pretrained skills on held-out tasks, offering a practical path for adapting robot foundation models in the real world. Website: https://microsoft.github.io/FlowDAgger

</details>


### [96] [SplatCtrl: Perception-Action Coupling via Gaussian Scene Representations and Reactive Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08948)
*Siddarth Jain,Ho Jin Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SplatCtrl 是一个基于3D高斯泼洒的统一框架，能够实时重建动态场景并生成无碰撞的机器人运动控制，结合体素滤波、动态高斯重定位以及连续符号距离函数的控制障碍函数，实现了感知-行动闭环，在仿真和实际机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操控器在非结构化和动态环境中面临挑战，缺乏能够同时实现实时场景重建和反应式运动生成的统一框架。

Method: 基于3D高斯泼洒（3D-GS），提出混合体素滤波与动态高斯重定位策略实现RGB-D流的场景重建；推导各向同性高斯的连续符号距离函数，并将其融入控制障碍函数，形成统一的感知-行动耦合框架。

Result: 在仿真环境、物理机器人以及人机共享工作空间中的实验验证了该框架在不确定和动态环境下实现场景重建与反应式控制的有效性。

Conclusion: SplatCtrl 成功将经典距离场与现代隐式表示相结合，实现了平滑可靠的实时运动生成，为动态环境中的机器人无碰撞控制提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators excel in structured environments but face substantial challenges in unstructured and dynamic settings. This paper presents SplatCtrl, a unified framework for real-time scene reconstruction and reactive robot motion generation to enable collision-free robotic arm control in previously unseen and continuously changing environments. Building on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS), we introduce a hybrid voxel-based filtering and dynamic Gaussian relocation strategy that supports efficient scene reconstruction from RGB-D streams while accommodating environmental changes. For safe and reactive control, we further propose a method for deriving continuous signed distance functions from isotropic Gaussians, providing stable and differentiable collision probability estimates that bridge classical distance fields with the modern implicit representation. These continuous distance metrics are incorporated into control barrier functions, resulting in a unified perception-action coupling framework that supports smooth and reliable real-time motion generation in response to scene changes. Experimental validation in simulation, on physical robot, and within shared human-robot workspace demonstrates the framework's effectiveness, achieving integrated scene reconstruction and reactive control in uncertain, and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [97] [CLAP: Direct VLM-to-VLA Adaptation via Language-Action Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08974)
*Yuri Ishitoya,Jeremy Siburian,Masashi Hamaya,Kuniaki Saito,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Mai Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAP通过将数字动作序列前接自然语言描述，以最小架构修改将预训练VLM转化为VLA，解决了输出分布不匹配问题，在LIBERO上达到90.8%准确率，并提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLAs在大规模机器人数据后训练和架构修改后，难以隔离VLM对控制的贡献，需要一种更透明的方法来理解VLM能力如何跨模型规模迁移。

Method: 提出CLAP，在数字动作序列前添加自然语言动作描述，使精确动作token预测因果地依赖于语言动作规划，无需修改骨干架构，仅需单epoch微调。

Result: 2B CLAP在LIBERO上达90.8%（+14.9点），在LIBERO-PRO上语言、物体、空间扰动下鲁棒性提升，将发布0.8B、2B、4B多尺度紧凑VLA家族。

Conclusion: CLAP以最小架构干预有效保留了VLM的语义能力到VLA控制中，提供了透明的跨规模能力迁移分析路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit semantic capabilities from pretrained VLMs, yet large-scale post-training on robot data and architectural modifications can reshape the backbone so extensively that it becomes difficult to isolate what the VLM contributes to control. Directly converting pretrained VLMs into VLAs with minimal architectural change offers a more transparent path to understanding how VLM capabilities transfer across model scales. The core obstacle is output-distribution mismatch: predicting actions as bare numeric token sequences moves generation away from the VLM's pretrained language distribution, degrading the capabilities we seek to preserve. To address this, we propose CLAP (Causal Language-Action Prediction), which prepends each numeric action sequence with a natural-language action description, causally conditioning precise action-token prediction on a language-action plan without modifying the backbone architecture. With single-epoch fine-tuning alone, 2B CLAP achieves 90.8% on LIBERO (+14.9 pt over VLA-0) and improves robustness on LIBERO-PRO under language, object, and spatial perturbations. We will release CLAP at 0.8B, 2B, and 4B as an open-weight, multi-scale compact VLA family from a single VLM lineage, enabling controlled analysis of VLM-to-VLA capability transfer.

</details>


### [98] [Dec-MARVEL: Decentralized Multi-Agent Exploration without Communication under Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09060)
*Janghyun Cho,Jimmy Chiun,Guillaume Sartoretti,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dec-MARVEL，一种去中心化、预算感知的多无人机协同探索框架，无需通信，通过视野内的队友轨迹实现隐式协调，利用图注意力网络选择返回可行的航点-航向动作，在仿真和实物实验中均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机探索面临不可靠通信、有限视场感知和有限旅行预算（需预留返程能量）的约束，现有方法依赖通信或全局信息，难以在无通信和方向性传感条件下实现高效协同探索。

Method: 1) 隐式协调：通过视野内队友轨迹作为协调信号，无需交换地图、目标或消息；2) 图注意力演员网络融合局部前沿几何、队友运动和预算特征，选择返回可行的航点-航向动作；3) 训练采用阶段条件化评论家、训练专用的任务导向特权评论家及混合预算课程学习。

Result: 在900次测试中（3种团队规模×3种预算×4个基线），Dec-MARVEL在9种配置下均取得最高或持平的最高探索率和最低传感重叠。720m紧预算下，2/4/8机器人成功率分别达53%/94%/100%，对比最强基线为37%/83%/99%。实物实验验证了仿真到现实的迁移和部署可行性。

Conclusion: Dec-MARVEL证明了在无通信、有向传感和预算约束下，通过隐式轨迹协调结合图注意力网络可实现高效多机协同探索，且具备良好的可扩展性和实际部署能力。

Abstract: Multi-UAV exploration is often constrained by unreliable communication, limited field-of-view sensing (e.g., lightweight onboard camera), and finite travel budgets that require each robot to reserve enough budget to return to its base. We present Dec-MARVEL, a decentralized budget-aware exploration framework for communication-free teams with directional sensing. Rather than exchanging maps, goals, or messages, each robot coordinates through its incidental observations: any teammate trajectory within its field of view serves as a coordination signal. A graph-attention actor fuses local frontier geometry, teammate motion, and budget features to select return-feasible waypoint-heading actions. The actor is trained with phase-conditioned critics, a training-only task-oriented privileged critic, and a mixture-based budget curriculum. Across 900 held-out trials spanning three team sizes (2, 4, 8 robots) and three travel budgets (720, 800, 1024 meters) against four baselines, Dec-MARVEL achieves the highest or tied-highest exploration rate and lowest sensing overlap across all nine team-size budget configurations. Under our tightest 720m budget, it reaches 53%, 94%, and 100% success for 2, 4, and 8 robots, versus 37%, 83%, and 99% for the strongest baseline. Physical-robot experiments demonstrate successful sim-to-real transfer and real-world deployment of Dec-MARVEL.

</details>


### [99] [Vascular Geometry Characterization for AI-Based Endovascular Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09130)
*Han-Ru Wu,Harry Robertshaw,Lisa Dwyer-Joyce,Thomas C Booth,Alejandro Granados*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统识别了与机械血栓切除术中强化学习智能体导航难度相关的血管几何特征（如主动脉弓类型、牛型弓、迂曲度、反向弯曲数等），并开发了自动化血管特征提取管道，为后续标准化复杂性分级和RL模型评估奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械血栓切除术是急性缺血性卒中的关键时间依赖性干预手段，但因神经放射科医生和专科中心短缺导致可及性受限。强化学习有望自动化血管内导航以提升可及性，但当前缺乏标准化框架来评估导航难度，难以进行模型训练与评估。

Method: 从61例患者的CT血管造影中分割血管树，使用定制管道测量主动脉弓类型、牛型弓、血管长度、迂曲度、起始角、反向弯曲数等指标；采用Soft Actor-Critic RL算法进行120秒自主导航；使用混合效应线性回归和逻辑回归分析结果。

Result: 左侧：牛型弓和II/III型主动脉弓分别使导航时间增加30.19秒和37.92秒，迂曲度增加显著延长操作时间（β=118.20）并降低成功率。右侧：II/III型弓延长操作时间45.94秒，每次额外反向弯曲增加3.96秒导航时间并降低成功率。

Conclusion: 首次证明MT智能体导航难度受血管几何结构强烈影响；提出的自动化管道可实现客观、定量的血管特征表征，为未来标准化复杂性分级和RL模型评估奠定基础，但本研究不旨在展示临床可推广的自主导航。

Abstract: Mechanical thrombectomy (MT) is a time-critical intervention for acute ischemic stroke; however, access remains limited due to a shortage of neuroradiologists and specialized centers. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers potential to automate endovascular navigation and improve accessibility, yet current models lack standardized frameworks to assess navigation difficulty for model training and evaluation. This study aims to identify vascular metrics associated with navigation difficulty and to develop an automated pipeline for quantitative vascular feature extraction, enabling future complexity grading. Vascular trees were segmented from computed tomography angiograms from 61 patients, and vascular metrics including aortic arch type, presence of bovine arch, vessel length, tortuosity, take-off angle, number of reverse curves, were measured using a custom pipeline. A Soft Actor-Critic RL algorithm was used for 120 s autonomous navigation. Outcomes were analyzed using both mixed effects linear and logistic regression. On the left side, the presence of a bovine arch and aortic arch type II/III increased navigation time by 30.19 s and 37.92 s, respectively, while greater tortuosity (\b{eta} = 118.20) further prolonged the procedure and reduced success probability. On the right side, type II/III arches extended procedure time by 45.94 s, while each additional reverse curve was associated with 3.96 s longer navigation time and lower probability of success. These findings demonstrate for the first time that MT agent navigation difficulty is strongly influenced by vascular geometry. The proposed automated pipeline enables objective and quantitative characterization of vascular features, providing a foundation for future development of standardized complexity grading and RL model evaluation, without aiming to demonstrate clinically generalizable autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [100] [Residual Physics-Informed Neural Networks for High-Fidelity BLDC Motor Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09136)
*Haitham El-Hussieny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度残差网络（ResNet）的物理信息神经网络（PINN），用于建立无刷直流（BLDC）电机的连续时间六状态动力学模型。该网络以仿真时间、三相电压和激励参数为输入，直接预测转子角度、角速度、三相电流和绕组温度，并通过复合物理-数据损失满足机电和热ODE约束。采用课程调度策略逐步激活物理惩罚项以避免过早收敛。训练在标准CPU上两分钟内完成。推理延迟为0.1-22微秒/查询，比传统ODE求解器快118倍，适用于实时观测器和控制应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLDC电机的高精度动力学建模是高性能机器人关节控制的基础，传统ODE求解器实时性不足，需要更高效的建模方法。

Method: 采用深度残差网络（ResNet）作为主干网络的PINN框架，构建连续时间代理模型；设计复合损失函数同时拟合数据并满足电、磁、热耦合ODE方程；引入课程学习策略逐步激活物理约束损失。

Result: 训练时间不到2分钟（标准CPU）；推理延迟0.1-22微秒/查询，比传统ODE求解器快118倍；可实时应用于观测器和控制任务。

Conclusion: PINN能以极低延迟和快速训练实现BLDC电机六状态连续时间动力学建模，满足实时机器人控制需求。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics modeling of Brushless DC (BLDC) motors is fundamental to high-performance robotic joint control. This paper presents a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) with a deep residual (ResNet) backbone that learns a continuous-time surrogate of the full six-state BLDC motor dynamics. Given simulation time, applied three-phase voltages, and excitation parameters as inputs, the network directly predicts all motor state variables -- rotor angle, angular velocity, three-phase currents, and winding temperature -- while simultaneously satisfying the governing electromechanical and thermal ODEs through a composite physics-data loss. A curriculum scheduling strategy gradually activates the physics penalty to prevent premature convergence. Training runs are completed in under two minutes on a standard CPU. Crucially, once trained, PINN inference achieves latencies of 0.1--22, mu s per query, up to 118x faster than conventional ODE solvers, making it suitable for real-time observer and control applications.

</details>


### [101] [BeyondSight: Object Permanence for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09138)
*Sandro Papais,Letian Wang,Mudit Jain,Behnaz Rezaei,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BeyondSight提出了一种具有物体永久性感知的端到端自动驾驶框架，通过维持持久化的actor查询使模型能够在长时间遮挡下仍能推理被遮挡的交通参与者，并在nuScenes-Permanence数据集上显著提升了不可见目标的检测性能（mAP从0提升至0.249）以及规划性能（L2误差从0.61降至0.54）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶系统将actor的存在与瞬时观测隐式耦合，导致在长时间遮挡下actor假设退化或消失，从而遗漏关键交通参与者，影响后续预测与规划模块的可靠性。

Method: 提出BeyondSight框架，通过时间维度上维持持久化的actor查询实现存在性与可观测性的解耦；利用时间传播和观测条件化证据更新来更新actor查询；并构建nuScenes-Permanence数据集，提供不可见actor的真值标注与基于可观测性条件的评估协议。

Result: 在nuScenes-Permanence上，不可见actor的检测mAP从0提升至0.249；规划L2平均误差从0.61降低至0.54。

Conclusion: 物体永久性（object permanence）是构建鲁棒端到端自动驾驶系统的重要建模原则，BeyondSight验证了在部分可观测环境中维持持久化actor表征的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving operates in partially observable environments where actors may become fully occluded by other vehicles or infrastructure. Most end-to-end driving systems implicitly couple actor existence to instantaneous observations, causing actor hypotheses to degrade or disappear during prolonged occlusion and removing potentially critical agents from downstream prediction and planning. We introduce BeyondSight, a permanence-aware end-to-end driving framework that decouples actor existence from observability by maintaining persistent actor hypotheses over time. BeyondSight propagates actor queries temporally and updates them with observation-conditioned evidence, enabling joint perception, prediction, and planning to reason about actors even when they are temporarily unobservable. To enable principled training and evaluation of persistence-aware models, we further introduce nuScenes-Permanence, an extension of nuScenes that provides supervision and observability-conditioned evaluation for unobservable actors. Experiments show that BeyondSight substantially improves reasoning under occlusion, increasing detection performance for unobservable actors from 0 to 0.249 mAP while reducing planning error from 0.61 to 0.54 L2avg. These results highlight object permanence as an important modeling principle for robust end-to-end autonomous driving.

</details>


### [102] [TactiDex: A Real-World Tactile-Guided Benchmark for Human-Like Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09190)
*Suting Ni,Hanbing Zhang,Zhenyu Wei,Guo Chen,Chixuan Zhang,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TactiDex是一个触觉引导的基准数据集，结合TactiSkill框架（基于三组件触觉奖励函数），将人类演示转化为物理真实的机器人灵巧操作，在单手和双手任务上优于仅依赖运动轨迹的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人到机器人灵巧操作迁移主要依赖运动轨迹，仅实现运动模仿而缺乏物理接地（如接触形成、力调节），无法实现类人灵巧操作。

Method: 构建TactiDex数据集（对齐全手触觉信号与多粒度运动学/物体状态，附带标准化评估指标）；提出TactiSkill框架，其核心是新颖的三组件触觉奖励函数（统一指导、类人对齐、接触约束）。

Result: 在单手和双手任务上，TactiSkill在操作成功率和物理真实感方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为推进触觉感知灵巧操作奠定了基础，证明触觉信号可作为结构化监督信号实现接触级类人化操作。

Abstract: Tactile feedback is fundamental to Hand-Object Interaction (HOI), governing contact formation, force regulation, and stable manipulation, making it essential for achieving true human-like dexterous manipulation. Yet, current human-to-robot dexterous transfer pipelines primarily rely on kinematic trajectories, resulting in motion imitation without physically grounded interaction. To address this, we introduce TactiDex, a real-world tactile-guided benchmark specifically designed to move dexterous manipulation beyond kinematic mimicry toward contact-level human-likeness. TactiDex provides a comprehensive dataset that elegantly aligns whole-hand tactile signals with multi-granularity kinematic and object states, coupled with standardized evaluation metrics. Building upon this data paradigm, we propose a tactile-driven transfer framework that effectively translates human demonstrations into physically plausible robotic execution. We introduce TactiSkill, a framework built upon a novel tri-component tactile reward that innovatively uses tactile signals as structured supervision. This reward unifies guidance, human-like alignment, and contact constraints into a single objective. Through comprehensive experiments on both single and bimanual tasks, we demonstrate that TactiSkill achieves superior performance in manipulation success and physical realism. This work lays a crucial foundation for advancing tactile-aware dexterous manipulation. Our project page at https://tactidex.github.io/.

</details>


### [103] [GenVid2Robot: From Video Generation to Robot Manipulation via Rigid-Geometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09191)
*Haohui Huang,Xi Yuan,Panpan Liao,Tao Teng,Chenguang Yang,Jing Guo,Yi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenVid2Robot提出了一种刚体几何一致性框架，通过将生成的视频运动转换为可执行的机器人操作轨迹，解决了生成视频缺乏度量几何、抓取基础、运动学可行性和执行反馈的问题，从而提高了操作可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的视频虽然视觉上合理，但缺乏度量几何、抓取基础、机器人运动学可行性和执行时的反馈，导致直接轨迹回放不可靠。需要一种方法将生成的视频运动可靠地转换为实际可执行的机器人操作轨迹。

Method: 1) 从初始RGB-D观测中采样任务相关的语义锚点；2) 在生成的视频候选中跟踪这些锚点；3) 通过稀疏相对SE(3)模型验证2D运动是否可由第一帧RGB-D锚点解释；4) 仅将几何一致的运动传输给机器人；5) 将相对运动应用于由掩码约束抓取选择的真实抓取时TCP姿态，生成抓取条件化的执行轨迹；6) 使用有界深度补偿模块校正局部深度方向误差。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，GenVid2Robot通过将视觉运动先验与稀疏度量几何、抓取约束、机器人可行性检查和有界执行反馈相结合，提高了生成视频引导操作的可可靠性。

Conclusion: 将生成视频视为不确定的视觉运动假设而非直接演示，通过几何一致性验证筛选可执行的运动，结合抓取约束和深度补偿，能有效提升生成视频驱动的机器人操作可靠性。

Abstract: Generated videos provide useful visual motion priors for robot manipulation, but their visual plausibility does not imply physical executability. A generated video usually lacks metric geometry, grasp grounding, robot kinematic feasibility, and execution-time feedback, which makes direct trajectory replay unreliable in real-world manipulation. This paper presents GenVid2Robot, a rigid-geometric consistency framework that converts generated video motion into executable real-robot manipulation trajectories. Given an initial RGB-D observation and a task instruction, GenVid2Robot samples task-relevant semantic anchors from the real first frame, tracks these anchors through generated video candidates, and verifies whether the resulting 2D motion can be explained by first-frame RGB-D anchors under a sparse relative $SE(3)$ model. In this way, generated videos are treated as uncertain visual motion hypotheses rather than direct robot demonstrations. Only geometrically consistent motion is transferred to the robot. The accepted relative motion is then applied to the real grasp-time TCP pose selected by mask-constrained grasping, producing a grasp-conditioned execution trajectory that is consistent with both the visual motion prior and the physical grasp configuration. To reduce execution mismatch caused by RGB-D noise, calibration residuals, and small contact-induced displacement, a bounded depth-compensation module corrects local depth-direction errors without assuming full online replanning. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that GenVid2Robot improves the reliability of generated-video-guided manipulation by grounding visual motion priors with sparse metric geometry, grasp constraints, robot feasibility checking, and bounded execution feedback.

</details>


### [104] [Empirical Pedestrian Safety Assessment in a Mobile Robot Using a Predictive Social Force Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09192)
*Alireza Jafari,Yun-Hao Tsai,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了将预测机制引入社交力模型（SFM）和基于投影碰撞时间（PTTC）的SFM（TSFM）中，形成预测性SFM（PSFM）和预测性TSFM（PTSFM），并在非完整移动机器人与行人的迎面交互场景中评估了客观安全性和主观舒适性。实验表明，PTTC集成可提升安全指标，但预测机制带来的额外收益有限，且主观评价无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要在人行道上与行人共享空间，既要保证客观安全，也要尊重行人的主观安全与舒适感。虽然PTTC集成到SFM中已显示能改善安全指标，但预测性变体的效果尚不明确，因此需要系统性研究预测机制对安全性和舒适性的影响。

Method: 在非完整移动机器人上实现了SFM、TSFM（集成PTTC的SFM）、PSFM（预测性SFM）和PTSFM（预测性TSFM）四种导航模型。在志愿者参与的迎面交互场景中进行实验，通过最小PTTC、平均速度、最小距离、横向距离和最大轨迹曲率评估客观安全，通过Likert量表调查评估主观舒适性、平滑度、距离适当性和速度适宜性，并使用Mann-Whitney检验分析主观评分的显著性差异。

Result: PTTC集成确实改善了安全指标；预测机制（PSFM和PTSFM）仅在部分子指标（如轨迹平滑度和速度行为感知）上有有限可见的改进；尽管部分参与者认为预测方法提供了更平滑的运动和更安全的速度行为，但Mann-Whitney检验表明主观评分无显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于PTTC的导航能有效提升安全性，而在单行人场景中，本文所构建的预测方法带来的额外收益有限。

Abstract: Mobile robots are going to share the sidewalks with pedestrians. They must ensure their objective safety and respect the walkers' subjective safety/comfort. Computationally efficient Social Force Models (SFM) present interpretable solutions for real-time robot navigation in dynamic crowds. Recent explorations of Projected Time-to-collision (PTTC) integration into SFM variants, for example, PTTC-based SFM (TSFM), improve safety metrics. But the effect of predictive variants is unclear. We introduce Predictive SFM (PSFM) and Predictive TSFM (PTSFM) by integrating predicted social force vectors over a finite time horizon. The paper implements SFM, TSFM, PSFM, and PTSFM on a nonholonomic mobile robot and performs experimental trials with volunteers attending a facing scenario. We systematically study objective and subjective safety across the variants. Minimum PTTC, average speed, minimum distance, lateral distance, and the maximum trajectory curvature benchmark the objective safety. Likert scale post-interaction surveys assess subjective safety by marking comfort, smoothness, distance appropriateness, and speed suitability. We confirm that PTTC integration improves safety metrics. The prediction contribution is limited and occasionally visible in some of the sub-metrics. Some participants perceive smoother movements and safer speed behavior with predictive methods, but Mann-Whitney tests reveal no significant differences in subjective ratings. Therefore, PTTC-based navigation enhances safety, whereas the formulated prediction offers limited additional benefits in single-pedestrian scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [Tactile and Vision Conditioned Contact-Centric Control for Whole-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09218)
*Rishabh Madan,Angchen Xie,Samantha Saak,Andres Blanco,Dohyeok Lee,Sarah Grace Brown,Yunting Yan,Mark Zolotas,Jose Barreiros,Tapomayukh Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TACTIC，一种结合触觉与视觉的接触中心控制方法，通过混合预测模型（学习型潜空间动力学+接触雅可比解析运动学）和采样MPC规划器，实现多连杆全身操控。在仿真和真实机器人上验证了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身操控中手臂与环境的分布式多触点交互打破了传统学习操控的三个隐含假设：手臂构型紧密耦合运动与接触力、接触状态在遮挡下部分可观测、纯学习 rollout 在分布外因多触点配置稀疏表示而产生物理不一致。现有方法难以处理这些挑战。

Method: 1) 混合预测模型：结合RGB-D、分布式触觉传感和2D邻近度表示，将学习到的动作条件潜空间动力学与通过接触雅可比建立的解析运动学耦合；2) 采样MPC规划器：使用接触感知动作采样（接触雅可比投影引导动作序列朝向力调制方向），预测邻近度和交互力作为目标函数，平衡任务进度与全身力调节。

Result: 在仿真中，TACTIC持续优于基于模型和无模型的SOTA方法；消融实验验证了各设计选择的贡献。在真实机器人上，成功完成三项全身操控任务：翻转与重新定位假人、在3D动态迷宫中到达目标。

Conclusion: TACTIC通过融合学习型动力学与解析接触模型，结合接触感知的MPC规划，有效解决了全身操控中接触状态模糊、物理不一致等核心问题，在仿真和真实场景中均表现优异。

Abstract: Whole-arm manipulation involves direct contact with the environment while the robot completes a task by distributing contact across multiple links as contacts form, slide, and break. This setting breaks common implicit assumptions in many learning-based manipulation pipelines: arm configuration tightly couples motion and contact forces, contact state is partially observed under occlusion, and purely learned rollouts can become physically inconsistent under distribution shift because many multi-link contact configurations are sparsely represented in the data. To address this, we propose TACTIC (Tactile and Vision Conditioned Contact-Centric Control), a receding-horizon controller for whole-arm manipulation. TACTIC uses a contact-centric hybrid predictive model that combines RGB-D, distributed tactile sensing, and a compact 2D proximity representation. The model couples a learned, action-conditioned latent dynamics model with analytical kinematics through contact Jacobians, enabling rollouts of future contact configurations and interaction forces. TACTIC integrates these rollouts into a sampling-based MPC planner with contact-aware action sampling: contact Jacobian-based projections steer sampled action sequences toward force-modulating directions, and objectives defined over predicted proximity and interaction forces trade task progress against whole-arm force regulation. We evaluate TACTIC in simulation against state-of-the-art model-based and model-free methods, and perform ablations that isolate the contribution of each design choice. TACTIC consistently outperforms other methods. We further demonstrate real-world performance on a robot with distributed tactile sensing across three whole-arm manipulation tasks that require multi-contact trajectories: turning over and repositioning a manikin, and goal-reaching in a 3D dynamic maze. Website: https://emprise.cs.cornell.edu/tactic

</details>


### [106] [Implicit-Behavior Coordination from Unlabeled Sub-Task Demonstrations for Rearrangement Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09234)
*Ahmed Shokry,Usama Ahmed Siddiquie,Sicong Pan,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需显式技能抽象的长时域机器人重排方法，通过从无标签的子任务演示中学习隐式行为协调，利用价值引导的动作选择组合多模态行为。在Habitat环境中，该方法在复杂重排任务上优于任务特定的模仿基线，接近带行为克隆技能的规划器基线，且无需任务规划或完整演示。消融实验证实了评论家引导的候选选择对协调多模态行为的关键作用，扩展实验表明该方法能处理更大的行为库并保持更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时域重排任务通常依赖预定义技能、技能标签或边界以及特定任务切换逻辑，这种显式技能抽象在行为数量和任务时域增加时难以扩展。本文旨在探索是否可以在没有显式技能抽象的前提下，直接从无标签的子任务演示中学习隐式行为协调来解决长时域重排问题。

Method: 将重排问题形式化为从无标签子任务演示中学习隐式行为协调：直接从混合行为数据中学习技能类行为，并通过价值引导的动作选择来协调这些行为。具体地，使用评论家（critic）网络引导候选动作的选择，从而自然地组合多模态行为。

Result: 在Habitat重排任务中：(1) 该方法在复杂重排任务上优于任务特定的模仿学习基线，性能接近使用行为克隆技能的oracle规划器基线；(2) 消融实验表明评论家引导的候选选择对协调多模态行为至关重要；(3) 扩展实验表明该方法能处理更大的行为库，并在链式目标延长时域时比任务特定模仿基线保持更强性能。

Conclusion: 显式技能抽象并非长时域重排任务的必要前提，隐式行为协调提供了一种有前景的、数据驱动的替代方案，可替代传统的显式技能流水线。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic rearrangement tasks are often treated as skill sequencing problems, requiring predefined skills, skill labels, or boundaries, and task-specific switching logic. Although effective, such explicit skill abstractions can become difficult to scale as the number of behaviors and the task horizon increase. We instead formulate rearrangement as implicit-behavior coordination from unlabeled sub-task demonstrations, where skill-like behaviors are learned directly from mixed behavior data and coordinated through value-guided action selection. Experiments in Habitat rearrangement tasks support this formulation in three ways. First, our method outperforms task-specific imitation baselines on more complex rearrangement tasks and approaches an oracle-planner baseline with behavior-cloned skills, while using no oracle task plan or skill-labeled full-task demonstrations. Second, ablations show that reliable critic-guided candidate selection is essential for coordinating multi-modal behaviors. Third, scaling experiments show that the method handles larger behavior repertoires and maintains stronger performance than task-specific imitation baselines as chained targets extend the horizon. These results suggest that explicit skill abstraction is not a prerequisite for long-horizon rearrangement, and that implicit-behavior coordination offers a promising data-driven alternative to explicit skill-based pipelines.

</details>


### [107] [Validating Virtual Reality for Studying Multimodal Human-Robot Interaction in Socially Aware Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09261)
*Hariharan Arunachalam,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Rachid Alami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过对比实验（N=21）验证了VR环境能够有效复现真实世界中人类与机器人在社交导航中的多模态交互动态，包括感知社会意识、交互舒适度及行为反应（轨迹、头部朝向），表明VR可作为研究社交导航和人机交互的可靠平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究利用VR探索社交感知机器人导航，但VR能否捕捉真实世界人机共航中观察到的多模态交互动态尚不明确。因此，本文旨在评估VR是否能够保真地复现真实环境下的多模态人机交互特征。

Method: 开发VR原型系统，采用被试内设计（N=21），在动捕场地及其VR虚拟副本中，让参与者与PR2移动操作机器人进行两种常见共航场景（正交交叉与并排通过）的交互，收集主观评价（社会感知、交互舒适度）与客观行为数据（轨迹、头部朝向）。

Result: 参与者对机器人社交感知导航的评分在VR和真实环境中一致；VR捕捉到的人类交互行为与真实世界观察结果一致。

Conclusion: VR可作为研究社交导航中更丰富多模态行为的可靠且灵活的平台。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) offers a flexible and controllable platform for studying human-robot interaction. Prior work has explored VR for socially aware robot navigation. However, whether VR captures the multimodal interaction dynamics observed in real-world human-robot co-navigation remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present a VR prototype and evaluate its suitability for studying multimodal human-robot interaction (HRI) in socially aware navigation. Specifically, we investigate whether VR preserves the multimodal interaction dynamics observed in real-world human-robot co-navigation. We conducted a within-subjects study (N = 21) in which participants interacted with a PR2 mobile manipulator robot in both a motion capture equipped arena and its virtual replica in an immersive VR environment. Two common co-navigation scenarios were examined : orthogonal crossing and pass-by interactions. Participants evaluated the robot's perceived social awareness and interaction comfort, while trajectory and head-orientation data were analysed to examine behavioral responses during the interaction. Our results show that participants perceive the robot's socially aware navigation similarly in VR and in the real world. Furthermore, VR captures human interaction behaviors in ways consistent with real-world observations. These findings suggest that VR can be a reliable and flexible platform for studying richer multimodal behaviors in social navigation and HRI.

</details>


### [108] [Robot Trajectron V3: A Probabilistic Shared Control Framework for SE(3) Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09315)
*Pinhao Song,Zhongxi Li,Ze Fu,Federico Ulloa Rios,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robot Trajectron V3 (RT-V3)，一种基于概率共享控制的框架，通过贝叶斯推理将学习到的用户意图先验与实时命令结合，实现SE(3)空间中的灵巧遥操作抓取任务，显著提升了成功率并降低了用户认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度机械臂遥操作任务在低带宽接口下认知负担大、易出错，现有方法难以在复杂场景下有效推断用户意图并提供辅助控制。

Method: 将共享控制建模为贝叶斯推理：用Transformer条件生成模型学习过去动力学与视觉点云的意图先验（分解平移-旋转表示），结合实时用户命令似然得到后验意图分布，持续估计轨迹分布并实现共享辅助。

Result: 轨迹预测高精度，反应式规划性能具竞争力；真实用户实验中成功率与效率显著优于基线，大幅降低用户身心负荷。

Conclusion: 基于学习的概率意图推理框架可有效缓解高维遥操作中的认知压力，为复杂操作任务提供高效的共享控制方案。

Abstract: We aim to address the challenge of teleoperating robotic arms for high-degree-of-freedom (high-DoF) manipulation tasks, which is cognitively demanding and error-prone, particularly when relying on low-bandwidth interfaces. We propose Robot Trajectron V3 (RT-V3), a probabilistic shared control framework designed for $SE(3)$ grasping tasks. RT-V3 formulates shared control as Bayesian inference by learning a prior over user intent and combining it with real-time user commands to estimate the posterior intent distribution. The prior models user intent as a distribution over future trajectories conditioned on past robot dynamics and visual scene context. The intent prior is parameterized by a transformer-based conditional generative model that reasons over point clouds and candidate grasp poses, together with a factorized translation-rotation representation that improves learning efficiency in high-dimensional action spaces. During execution, RT-V3 continuously estimates the posterior distribution over future trajectories by combining the learned intent prior with a user-command likelihood derived from the observed control input, enabling continuous intent refinement and shared assistance. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that RT-V3 achieves high accuracy in trajectory prediction and competitive performance in reactive planning. Furthermore, real-world user studies indicate that RT-V3 significantly outperforms baseline methods in terms of success rate and efficiency, while substantially reducing the user's physical and mental workload.

</details>


### [109] [Differential Analysis of Multispectral Images for Terrain Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09319)
*Omar Kashmar,Hemendra Arya,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT是一个轻量级多光谱框架，结合原始光谱波段和光照不变波段比表示，通过双流残差架构和差分融合分支，提升低光照、阴影等恶劣条件下地形感知的鲁棒性。在油污土壤数据集和变光照/热扰动的水草数据集上验证了有效性，优于强基线且支持边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB的地形感知在低光照、阴影及材料模糊等场景下容易失效，亟需对光照和传感器增益变化具有鲁棒性的多光谱感知方案。

Method: 提出DRIFT框架：1) 双流残差架构分别处理原始光谱波段和光照容忍的波段比表示；2) 差分融合分支显式突出绝对波段与比值特征之间的差异，增强对噪声或部分不可靠光谱测量的鲁棒性。

Result: 在油污土壤多光谱数据集（MicaSense RedEdge-P相机/UAV采集）和变光照/热扰动水草数据集上，DRIFT一致优于强基线方法，且支持边缘端部署。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过融合原始波段与波段比表示并引入差分融合机制，有效缓解了光照和传感器增益变化对多光谱地形感知的影响，在保持轻量化同时实现了鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Reliable terrain understanding is a prerequisite for autonomous robot navigation. Yet, the widespread RGB-based perception can fail under low illumination, shadows, and material ambiguities. In this work we propose DRIFT, a lightweight multispectral framework that combines raw spectral bands and illumination-tolerant band-ratio representations through a dual-stream residual architecture and a differential fusion branch. Band ratios attenuate multiplicative acquisition effects (illumination/sensor gains), while the differential fusion explicitly highlights discrepancies between absolute-band and ratio-derived cues, which improves the robustness to noisy or partially unreliable spectral measurements. In the paper (i) we evaluate DRIFT on a new oil-on-soil multispectral dataset acquired using a MicaSense RedEdge-P camera mounted on an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle, and (ii) we provide an additional controlled study on water-on-grass under varying illumination and thermal perturbations (hot/cold water) to analyze NIR-sensitive effects. DRIFT consistently improves over strong baselines, while remaining compatible with edge deployment.

</details>


### [110] [Effects of Robotic Touch on Older Users During Walking Guidance by a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09323)
*Leonie Leven,Marko Ackermann,Christian Werner,Melina Schmetterer,Theresa Buchner,Monika Eckstein,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO
