TL;DR: 本研究探讨了老年人在机器人引导行走中对不同触觉模式的感知与反应。24名老年人在四种接触条件下（无接触、握手腕、挽手臂、前臂支撑）与TIAGo Pro机器人完成行走任务。通过生理信号、行为数据和问卷调查的综合评估，发现虽然生理指标显示轻度应激增加，但行为和主观指标表明老年人普遍接受机器人触觉交互，尤其偏好握手腕和前臂支撑等接触力较大的方式，这些方式与更高的信任感、安全感和舒适度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 养老护理中人员短缺问题日益严峻，机器人可提供辅助和情感支持。步行引导是重要应用场景，但涉及直接身体接触，需了解老年人对不同触觉模式的感知与反应，以设计更适老的引导交互方式。

Method: 24名老年人（68-88岁）在四种接触条件下（无接触NC、握手腕HH、挽手臂LA、前臂支撑FC）由TIAGo Pro机器人引导行走十米轨迹。采用多模态评估方法：心电图、皮电活动、接触力、距机器人距离及问卷调查。

Result: 生理结果表明机器人交互期间应激水平略有升高。行为和主观测量显示老年人总体接受机器人触觉交互。接触力较大的两种条件（HH和FC）下参与者与机器人的相对距离更小，表明信任和信心更高。问卷调查支持这些发现，表明这些条件下感知安全、信任和舒适度更高。

Conclusion: 老年人更偏好温和、稳定的触觉交互而非无接触交互。该研究为机器人步行引导辅助设计提供了重要见解。

Abstract: The shortage of healthcare staff is a challenge in geriatric care. To address this, robots can be integrated into care settings to provide assistance and emotional support. A promising application is walking guidance, particularly benefiting older adults as navigation skills deteriorate with aging. As walking guidance involves direct contact, the aim of this study is to understand how older adults perceive and respond to different touch modes during guided walking. 24 older adults (68 - 88 yrs.) walked four times a ten-meter trajectory guided by the robot TIAGo Pro in four contact conditions: no physical contact (NC); physical contact through holding the robot's wrist with the hand (HH); physical interaction through linking arms with the robot (LA); and physical contact through resting the forearm on the robots forearm (FC). A multimodal assessment approach included electrocardiogram, electrodermal activity, contact force, distance to robot, and questionnaires. Physiological results reveal a slight increase in stress levels during robot interaction. Behavioural and subjective measures, however, show overall acceptance of robotic touch. The two conditions corresponding to larger interaction forces (HH and FC) were associated with lower relative distances between participant and robot, indicating a higher trust and confidence. Questionnaire responses supported these findings, evidencing greater perceived safety, trust and comfort in these conditions. This study provides insights for the design of robotic walking guidance assistance, indicating that gentle, stable touch is preferred by older adults in comparison to contactless interaction.

</details>


### [111] [PhysV2A: Reachability-Gated and Semantic-Mask-Constrained Feasibility Completion for Video-to-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09365)
*Haohui Huang,Junda Duan,Tao Teng,Chenguang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysV2A 提出了一种可达性门控与语义掩码约束的可行性补全框架，将视频衍生的6D物体运动转化为机器人可执行的操控轨迹，通过层次化可达性筛选和VLM辅助的语义掩码约束优化，在桌面操控任务上显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视频的操控方法提供了物体中心的运动先验，但这些先验通常与具身无关，无法直接被特定机器人执行。现有方法缺乏将视频运动先验转化为机器人可执行轨迹的有效机制。

Method: 提出PhysV2A框架：1) 将抓取可行性与轨迹耦合，形成抓取条件化的TCP轨迹假设；2) 层次化可达性门控筛选，拒绝不可行的抓取-轨迹对；3) VLM辅助且规则验证的语义掩码（S-Mask），识别任务关键与可松弛的笛卡尔分量；4) 冗余优先优化和有界笛卡尔松弛的语义掩码约束可操作性优化。

Result: 在四个桌面操控任务上的真实机器人实验中，PhysV2A相比代表性的视频先验基线和仅IK基线提升了任务成功率，减少了运动学可行性失败，生成了条件更好的轨迹且语义偏差可控。

Conclusion: 将抓取可行性与轨迹耦合处理并结合语义掩码约束优化，能够有效将视频衍生的物体运动转化为机器人可执行的操控轨迹，提升任务执行的成功率和轨迹质量。

Abstract: Video-based manipulation provides object-centric motion priors from human demonstrations, generated videos, or RGB-D observations, but such priors are typically embodiment-agnostic and cannot be directly executed by a specific robot. This paper presents \textbf{PhysV2A}, a reachability-gated and semantic-mask-constrained feasibility-completion framework for converting video-derived 6D object motion into robot-executable manipulation trajectories. The key idea is to treat grasp feasibility as trajectory-conditioned rather than local: each RGB-D-generated 6-DoF grasp candidate is rigidly coupled with the recovered object motion to form a grasp-conditioned TCP trajectory hypothesis. PhysV2A then performs hierarchical reachability-gated selection, where infeasible grasp--trajectory pairs are rejected by robot-centric kinematic checks and surviving candidates are ranked by downstream execution suitability. For the selected reachable trajectory, a VLM-assisted and rule-validated S-Mask identifies task-critical and relaxable Cartesian components, enabling semantic-mask-constrained manipulability refinement through redundancy-first optimization and bounded Cartesian relaxation. Real-robot experiments on four tabletop manipulation tasks show that PhysV2A improves task success over representative video-prior and IK-only baselines, reduces kinematic-feasibility failures, and produces better-conditioned trajectories with bounded semantic deviations.

</details>


### [112] [One-Shot Multimodal Learning from Demonstration with Force-Constrained Elastic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09515)
*Brendan Hertel,Jonathan Spanos,Navya Garg,Reza Azadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于单次示教的多模态学习框架，通过自适应加权空间与力模态的分段方法、融入外力约束的弹性图编码及凸优化过程，实现从单一示教中同时复现运动轨迹与接触力特征，并在两种力传感配置的五种真实操作任务上验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有示教学习方法大多仅建模空间轨迹而忽略与环境的力交互，导致在力约束场景下鲁棒性差、执行不安全或不一致。

Method: 1）提出多模态概率分段方法，自适应加权空间与力模态以自动提取力感知运动基元；2）扩展弹性图表示以融入外力约束，设计凸优化过程学习力一致轨迹模型。

Result: 在UR5e+Robotiq 2f-85（腕部力传感）和Kinova Gen3+Openhand Model O（指部力传感）两种配置的五种真实操作任务上，实现了鲁棒的多模态分段、精确的力感知复现和跨平台泛化。

Conclusion: 该方法能从单次示教中同时复现运动与接触力特征，提升力约束环境下操作的安全性，并具有良好的跨平台通用性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks often require simultaneous reasoning over motion and contact forces, yet most Learning from Demonstration (LfD) methods model only spatial trajectories and neglect force interactions with the environment. This limitation reduces robustness and can lead to unsafe or inconsistent task reproduction in force-constrained settings. We propose a novel one-shot multimodal LfD framework for the segmentation, encoding, and reproduction of force-inclusive demonstrations. First, we introduce a multimodal probabilistic segmentation method that adaptively weighs spatial and force modalities over time, enabling the automatic extraction of force-aware motion primitives. Second, we extend the elastic maps representation to incorporate external force constraints during skill encoding and formulate a convex optimization procedure for learning force-consistent trajectory models. The resulting skills reproduce both motion and contact characteristics from a single demonstration while promoting safer execution by accounting for demonstrated force profiles. We validate our approach on five real-world manipulation tasks across two distinct force-sensing configurations: wrist force sensing on a UR5e with a Robotiq 2f-85 gripper and finger force sensing on a Kinova Gen3 with an Openhand Model O gripper. Experimental results demonstrate robust multimodal segmentation, accurate force-aware reproduction, and cross-platform generality.

</details>


### [113] [DemoBridge: A Simulation-in-the-Loop Toolkit for Single-View Human Demonstration Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09519)
*Zehao Wang,Fabien Despinoy,Sergey Zakharov,Tinne Tuytelaars,Rahaf Aljundi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoBridge是一个将单视角RGB人手演示视频转化为可执行的、经过物理验证的机器人手臂轨迹的工具包，核心是一个全局碰撞感知规划器，通过物理仿真器在环验证和回溯机制，解决跨实体映射中的无碰撞解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨实体映射（人手到机械臂）存在巨大的具身差异：机械臂是长连杆结构，碰撞体积远大于人手，传统逆运动学映射末端执行器姿态常常无碰撞解，且单视角噪声导致演示参考不准确。

Method: 核心是一个全局碰撞感知规划器，联合优化整个关节轨迹，同时考虑替代抓取姿态、全身及抓取物体的碰撞、对演示路径的忠实度；采用物理仿真器在环验证，失败时回溯重规划而非丢弃。

Result: 在三个真实演示任务上评估了完整流水线的重定向效果，并在受控合成基准上测试了规划器。

Conclusion: DemoBridge生成的机器人动作序列动态稳定且忠实于演示操作，可作为策略学习的即用仿真rollout；抓取时序自动推断，感知后端、机器人和流水线阶段均可配置切换。

Abstract: We present DemoBridge, an toolkit that turns a single-view RGB stereo recording of a human hand demonstration into an executable, physics-validated robot-arm trajectory. Retargeting across the embodiment gap is hard. A robot arm reaches a target with a long, articulated body whose links carry far more collision volume than a hand. Solving inverse kinematics for the mapped end-effector pose often yields no collision-free solution, and a trajectory imposes this at every waypoint. A single view adds noise, leaving the demonstrated reference inaccurate. At the core of DemoBridge is a single collision-aware planner. It optimizes the whole joint trajectory at once, reasoning jointly over alternative grasp poses, whole-arm and grasped-object collision, and fidelity to the demonstrated path. A physics simulator runs in the loop. It validates each phase as it is produced and backtracks on failure, so a demonstration that cannot be reproduced as given is re-planned rather than discarded. The resulting action sequence is dynamically stable and faithful to the demonstrated manipulation. It also doubles as a ready-to-use simulation rollout for policy learning. Grasp timing is inferred automatically, and the perception backends, robot, and pipeline stages are swappable from configuration. We evaluate whole-pipeline retargeting on three real-demonstration tasks and the planner on a controlled synthetic benchmark. Our code is available at https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/u0123974/demo-bridge/ .

</details>


### [114] [How Mobile Gas Sensor Trajectories Govern Hydrogen Leak Detection: A Safety Gap in Manual Leak Inspection of Hydrogen System Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09527)
*Christian Masuhr,Arne Wendt,Thorsten Schüppstuhl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究量化了氢能设备制造中示踪气体嗅探轨迹运动学对泄漏检测可靠性的影响，发现线性扫描易导致假阴性，而几何特定路径（如环绕密封点的圆周运动）能保持高安全裕度，并提出了轨迹规则和动态信号衰减模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电解槽制造中的氢泄漏检测依赖人工示踪气体嗅探，但标准缺乏空间探头引导说明，检测可靠性完全依赖于操作人员执行，且传感器信号延迟进一步降低可靠性，急需量化研究扫描轨迹对检测效果的影响。

Method: 使用机器人引导测试台消除操作变异性，在标准化泄漏率（5 vol%氢气/氮气）和不同扫描速度下，获取代表性几何结构的静态浓度场和动态轨迹通过数据，分析扫描速度和探头空间方向对可检测性的影响。

Result: 扫描速度和探头空间方向强烈影响可检测性；传统线性轨迹在动态条件下频繁漏检导致严重假阴性；而几何特定路径（如密封点周围的圆周切入路径）能保持高安全裕度。

Conclusion: 当前标准操作程序存在显著安全风险，基于观察结果提出了几何特定路径规则和动态信号损失衰减因子模型，并实现了从3D模型直接生成验证轨迹的软件原型，可集成至辅助系统中。

Abstract: The integrity of hydrogen infrastructure relies on reliable leak detection, performed almost exclusively via manual tracer gas sniffing in electrolyzer manufacturing. Although mandated by standards, the lack of spatial probe guidance instructions leaves detection reliability entirely to operator execution, further compromised by sensor signal delays. This study quantifies how sniffer trajectory kinematics affect detection reliability at small-scale pipes and fittings, a near-field regime largely neglected by macroscopic dispersion research. Using a robotically guided test bench to eliminate operator variability, static concentration fields and dynamic trajectory passes were acquired across representative geometries under standardized leak rates (5 vol% hydrogen in nitrogen) and varying scanning velocities. Results demonstrate that scanning velocity and spatial probe orientation strongly dictate detectability. Conventional linear trajectories frequently miss leaks under dynamic conditions, causing severe false negatives. Conversely, geometry-specific routing, such as circumferential plunging paths around sealing points, maintains a high safety margin. From these observations, geometry-specific routing rules and a reduction-factor model for dynamic signal loss are derived. The findings reveal that current standard operating procedures pose a tangible safety risk. To operationalize these rules, a proof-of-concept software pipeline is presented, generating validated trajectories directly from 3D models for visualization in assistance systems.

</details>


### [115] [Task-Adaptive Design of Modular Aerial Manipulators Under Airflow Exposure Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09548)
*Mengguang Li,Heinz Koeppl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向模块化空中机械臂的优化设计框架，联合考虑任务扳手可行性、末端执行器位置和气流暴露约束，通过锥-球包络近似建模旋翼气流，优化平台重构以适应多样化任务需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼平台的空中操作在复杂环境中具有重要应用价值，但旋翼诱导气流对气流敏感目标或周围环境构成关键限制，现有设计通常假设固定的末端执行器位置，缺乏对气流暴露约束的系统性考虑。

Method: (1) 提出目标侧气流耐受度的新分类并形式化为几何约束；(2) 引入紧凑的锥-球包络模型近似四旋翼气流的扩散结构，保证优化计算可行性；(3) 提出重构优化框架，联合优化末端执行器位置与平台配置，同时施加目标侧气流暴露和平台内气流干扰约束。

Result: 可扩展性实验和消融研究验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的优化框架能够有效适应多样化任务扳手需求，同时管理气流暴露约束，优于固定末端执行器位置的传统设计方法。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation with multirotor platforms enables physical interaction in complex environments, but rotor-induced airflow remains a critical limitation for tasks involving airflow-sensitive targets or surroundings. This paper presents an optimization-based design framework for modular aerial manipulators that jointly considers task wrench feasibility, end-effector placement, and airflow exposure constraints. We first introduce a novel categorization of target-side airflow tolerance and formulate the corresponding exposure requirements as geometric constraints. To efficiently model rotor-induced airflow, we introduce a compact cone-sphere envelope that approximates the spreading structure of a quadrotor's airflow while preserving computational tractability for optimization. Building on this formulation, we propose a reconfiguration optimization that adapts a modular aerial manipulator to diverse task wrench requirements while enforcing both target-side airflow exposure and intra-platform airflow interference constraints. Unlike prior designs that assume a fixed end-effector location, the proposed framework optimizes the end-effector placement together with the platform configuration. Scalability experiments and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [116] [CORAL-AUV: CFD Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09557)
*Steven Roche,Milo Van Mooy,Nathan McGuire,Levi Cai,Jonathan P. How,Yogesh Girdhar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次成功将基于计算流体力学(CFD)数据训练的代理阻力模型(SDM)用于强化学习(RL)策略训练，实现了六自由度自主水下机器人(AUV)的零样本迁移控制，相比传统简化物理模型控制器，能耗降低31%，航点间穿越速度提升11%，误差减少19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV的精细控制对于采样、维护和勘测任务至关重要，但传统控制方法劳动强度大且对车辆构型或环境条件变化缺乏鲁棒性。强化学习结合域随机化虽有前景，但受限于仿真建模真实物理的能力，尤其是阻力物理难以建模且是仿真到现实差距的主要来源。CFD提供高保真阻力模型却因计算开销难以融入RL框架。

Method: 提出训练CFD模型的代理近似（代理阻力模型SDM），实现RL管线内的快速推理。在六自由度AUV上训练零样本RL策略，策略训练基于由CFD数据训练的SDM，并在受控水池环境和实地场景中评估。

Result: 相比简化物理模型控制器，SDM基RL控制器能耗降低31%，航点间速度提升11%，误差减少19%。SDM基RL控制器能更好地预测零样本迁移，对奖励塑造设计选择更鲁棒。在使用域随机化完成参数扰动任务时，CFD策略是唯一成功迁移的控制器。

Conclusion: 基于CFD代理模型训练的RL策略能够实现AUV的高效零样本迁移控制，在能耗、速度和精度方面均优于传统简化模型，且对参数变化具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine grain control and positioning of autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for sampling, maintenance, and survey applications. Traditional control methods for AUVs are labor intensive and are not robust to changes in the vehicle configuration or environmental conditions. Reinforcement learning (RL) promises rapid controller development while handling a range of deployment parameters via domain randomization (DR). However, DR is still limited by the capacity of the underlying simulation to model real physics. In particular, drag physics are difficult to model and are a large contributor to sim-to-real gaps. Meanwhile, computational fluid dynamics (CFD) provides high fidelity drag models but is challenging to leverage within reinforcement learning frameworks due to its computational overhead. Thus, in this paper we exploit the idea of training surrogate approximations of CFD models of a given vehicle, enabling fast inference within RL pipelines. We are the first to successfully deploy a zero-shot RL policy on a 6-DOF AUV in which policy training is performed on surrogate drag models (SDMs) trained on CFD data. We find 31% lower energy usage compared to a controller using simplified physics while traversing between waypoints 11% faster with 19% less error. Our SDM based RL controller better predicts zero-shot transfer and is more robust across reward shaping design choices. When using DR to complete a task with perturbed parameters, we find that the CFD policy is the only controller that successfully transfers. The policies are evaluated in a controlled tank environment and in the field providing extensive testing of the policies' capabilities.

</details>


### [117] [CoDiMAD: Diffusion-Based Privileged Distillation for Communication-Free Multi-Robot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09587)
*Jiyue Tao,Shunheng Xin,Tongsheng Shen,Dexin Zhao,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对通信受限下多机器人协调中确定性蒸馏导致多模态动作分布坍缩为均值的问题，提出CoDiMAD框架，利用扩散概率模型近似条件动作分布，实现去中心化策略的有效蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，相同的局部观测可能对应多个需要不同协作动作的全局状态，导致条件动作分布本质上是多模态的。标准确定性蒸馏将这些模态坍缩为均值，产生无效或犹豫的动作，影响去中心化协调性能。

Method: 提出CoDiMAD三阶段框架：1) 使用MAPPO训练全局先知oracle；2) 构建局部观测-先知动作的离线数据集；3) 将先知策略蒸馏为条件去噪扩散概率模型参数化的去中心化学生策略，通过扩散逆向过程采样连贯协调模式下的果断动作。

Result: 在三个协作任务上，CoDiMAD一致优于直接局部MARL和确定性蒸馏基线方法。理论分析刻画了确定性蒸馏的模式平均失败以及扩散蒸馏的分布恢复性质。

Conclusion: 通过扩散模型保持多模态动作分布，CoDiMAD有效解决了去中心化多机器人协调中观测歧义导致的模式坍缩问题，为通信受限场景下的策略蒸馏提供了新范式。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-robot coordination under partial observability remains challenging, especially in communication-free settings where agents must act solely from local sensor observations. Privileged policy distillation provides a promising approach by transferring knowledge from a globally informed oracle to sensor-constrained students. However, in multi-agent systems, the same local observation may correspond to multiple global configurations requiring qualitatively different cooperative actions, making the conditional action distribution inherently multi-modal. Standard deterministic distillation collapses these modes to their mean, often yielding invalid or hesitant actions. To address this issue, we propose CoDiMAD, a three-stage framework that trains a privileged oracle with MAPPO, constructs an offline dataset of local-observation-oracle-action pairs, and distills the oracle into decentralized students parameterized as conditional denoising diffusion probabilistic models. By approximating the conditional oracle-action distribution through the diffusion reverse process, CoDiMAD samples decisive actions from coherent coordination modes rather than averaging across them. Theoretical analysis characterizes the mode-averaging failure of deterministic distillation and the distributional recovery property of diffusion-based distillation. Experiments on three cooperative tasks show that CoDiMAD consistently outperforms direct local MARL and deterministic distillation baselines. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [118] [PAC-ACT: Post-training Actor-Critic for Action Chunking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09590)
*Yujie Pang,Zudong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PAC-ACT框架，通过强化学习后训练对预训练的Action Chunking Transformer策略进行精调，在不牺牲实时性和低资源消耗的前提下，提升工业精密接触操作的鲁棒性和力安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型推理延迟高、GPU显存开销大，不适合实时工业控制；而视觉-动作分块策略虽适用于实时场景，但通常由行为克隆训练，在接触密集任务中存在分布偏移问题，导致策略在扰动下可靠性不足。

Method: 提出PAC-ACT框架，包含：(1) 分块级别的策略优化；(2) ACT迁移的Actor-Critic架构；(3) 混合行为先验约束，在在线微调过程中保留预训练动作分布。

Result: 在工业精密接触基准任务上，PAC-ACT提升了任务成功率、接触稳定性和力安全性，同时保持低延迟和低GPU显存占用。在Contour任务中，峰值接触力显著降低，超过60N的力读数比例减少46倍。稀疏奖励消融实验表明行为先验约束在随机初始位姿下能实现有效探索。

Conclusion: PAC-ACT通过强化学习后训练有效解决了行为克隆导致的分布偏移问题，为工业精密接触操作提供了一种兼具实时性、低资源消耗和高鲁棒性的策略优化方案。

Abstract: Precision industrial contact manipulation requires reliable robot policies under pose perturbations and contact-force constraints. Vision-language-action models offer broad generalization but often introduce high inference latency and GPU-memory cost, while vision-action chunking policies are more suitable for real-time industrial control. However, these policies are usually trained by behavior cloning and suffer from distribution shift in contact-rich tasks. This paper proposes PAC-ACT, a reinforcement-learning post-training framework for pretrained Action Chunking Transformer policies. PAC-ACT reformulates policy optimization at the chunk level, constructs an ACT-transferred actor-critic architecture, and introduces a hybrid behavior-prior constraint to preserve the pretrained action distribution during online fine-tuning. Experiments on industrial precision-contact benchmarks show that PAC-ACT improves task success, contact stability, and force safety while retaining low latency and low GPU-memory usage. On the Contour task, PAC-ACT significantly reduces peak contact force and decreases the proportion of force readings above 60 N by 46 times. Sparse-reward ablations further show that the proposed behavior-prior constraint enables effective exploration under randomized initial poses.

</details>


### [119] [B-spline Policy: Accelerating Manipulation Policies via B-spline Action Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09648)
*Xiaoshen Han,Haoyu Xiong,Haonan Chen,Chaoqi Liu,Antonio Torralba,Yuke Zhu,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出B-spline Policy (BSP)，一种将动作表示为连续B样条曲线的动作表征方法，通过预测B样条参数替代离散时间动作块，实现平滑、时间连续的轨迹，显著加速机器人操作策略并缩短任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作策略通常预测离散时间步的动作块，这导致动作不连续、执行频率受限，无法充分利用低层控制器的高频特性，从而拖慢任务完成速度。

Method: BSP将动作参数化为连续的B样条曲线，通过一组节点和控制点定义。策略网络直接预测B样条参数，生成的时间连续轨迹可被低层控制器以更高频率和速度执行，并支持时间缩放。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界任务中，BSP显著减少了任务完成时间，相比基线方法实现了大幅提升，同时保持了较高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: B样条参数化动作表征可无缝集成到标准策略学习流程中，是一种有效加速机器人操作策略的方法。

Abstract: In this work, we present B-spline Policy (BSP), an action representation designed for accelerating robot manipulation policies. Rather than predicting discrete-time action chunks, BSP parameterizes actions as continuous B-spline curves defined by a set of knots and control points. This representation yields smooth, time-continuous trajectories that can be temporally scaled and executed by low-level controllers at higher frequencies and speeds. We show that B-spline-parameterized actions can be seamlessly integrated into standard policy learning pipelines by directly predicting B-spline parameters. Experiments on simulated and real-world tasks demonstrate that BSP significantly reduces task completion time, achieving substantial improvements over baseline methods while maintaining strong success rates. More results: https://b-spline-policy.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [A Unified Approach to Interpreting Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models via Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08776)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhenxin Qin,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，通过将LLM输出分数分解为多个交互项（每个交互代表一组输入变量之间的非线性关系），揭示了多种知识蒸馏方法的共同机制是交互稀疏化——学生模型保留少量交互进行推理，抑制其他交互。不同KD方法的性能差异源于处理复杂交互的能力，越能实现复杂交互高稀疏性的方法性能越好。基于此，作者提出即插即用的复杂交互惩罚（CIP）损失函数，在蒸馏过程中显式强制复杂交互的稀疏性，实验表明CIP能一致提升多种KD方法在域内和分布外基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在大型语言模型中取得了成功，但其背后的机制尚不明确，本文旨在探索多种KD方法的共同机制。

Method: 将LLM输出分数分解为多个交互项的加和，每个交互代表一组输入变量（如词）之间的非线性关系；基于分解的交互分析KD的共同机制；提出复杂交互惩罚（CIP）损失函数。

Result: 发现KD的共同机制是交互稀疏化；性能差异源于处理复杂交互的能力；CIP损失函数一致提升了多种KD方法在域内和分布外基准上的性能。

Conclusion: 交互稀疏化是知识蒸馏的核心机制，CIP损失函数能有效提升蒸馏性能。

Abstract: Despite the success of knowledge distillation (KD) in Large Language Models (LLMs), the underlying mechanism behind its efficacy remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a unified approach to explore the common mechanism of various KD methods using interactions. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into the sum of numerous interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables (e.g., words). Based on the decomposed interactions, we discover that the common mechanism underlying various KD methods is the sparsification of interactions, i.e., student models retain fewer interactions for inference while suppressing other interactions to zero effects. Furthermore, we discover that the performance variance across different KD methods arises from their capabilities in handling complex interactions. A KD method typically yields better performance if it enables the student model to achieve higher sparsity of complex interactions. Motivated by these insights, we propose a plug-and-play loss function called Complex Interaction Penalty (CIP) to explicitly enforce the sparsity of complex interactions during the distillation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that integrating CIP consistently improves the performance of diverse KD methods on both in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [iLENS: Interpretable LLM-Guided Mixture-of-Experts for Neuroimaging Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08778)
*Farica Zhuang,Seong Woo Han,Zixuan Wen,Shu Yang,Yize Zhao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iLENS是一个基于大语言模型和混合专家架构的可解释生存预测框架，用于阿尔茨海默病转化预测，能整合结构化神经影像和非结构化信息，实现竞争性预测性能、患者亚型分型，并提供透明的生物学决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AD风险预测模型是静态的，缺乏可解释性和自然语言推理能力，亟需一种既能保持高性能又具有可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出iLENS框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）整合结构化神经影像测量和非结构化信息，基于混合专家（MoE）架构进行生存预测，LLM用于指导专家路由决策。

Result: 展示了具有竞争力的预测性能和患者亚型分型能力，同时提供透明且生物学上有依据的路由决策解释。

Conclusion: iLENS弥合了高性能生存分析与可解释临床决策支持之间的差距，为AD转化预测提供了兼具准确性和可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Disease (AD) is a complex neurodegenerative disorder that continues to impact millions of people worldwide. Predicting AD conversion during the prodromal stage remains critical for disease understanding and patient care. As such, survival models are widely used for AD risk prediction, yet they are typically static predictors with limited interpretability and no capacity for natural language reasoning. In this work, we propose iLENS, an interpretable large language model (LLM) guided framework based on mixture-of-experts (MoE) for survival prediction in AD conversion. Our approach uses LLM to synthesize structured neuroimaging measurements and unstructured information to guide expert routing. Our framework demonstrates competitive predictive performance and capability in patient subtyping. Furthermore, our framework provides transparent, biologically grounded rationales for its routing decisions, bridging the gap between high-performance survival analysis and interpretable clinical decision support.

</details>


### [122] [Signed Symmetric Quantization for Few-Bit Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08779)
*Ian Colbert,Eashan Dash,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Juan Amboage,Srinidhi N,Giuseppe Franco,Nicholas J. Fraser,Arun Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有符号对称量化方法（signed absmax grid），通过将额外的可表示值分配给主导离群值所在尾数，在不增加推理开销的前提下解决了标准对称量化器因正负数表示范围不对称导致的截断误差问题，理论分析表明该方法在ℓ₂量化误差上条件性边界最优，并在Qwen3、Llama3等大模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对称整数量化器由于有符号整数正负表示范围不对称（负数比正数多一个可表示值），导致正数侧的离群值被迫截断，在低位宽下产生显著的量化误差。非对称量化虽可通过零点偏移解决此问题，但会带来运行时开销（如llama.cpp上4-bit对称格式比非对称内存少9%、吞吐量高2.45倍）。因此需要一种既能避免截断误差又不牺牲推理效率的量化方案。

Method: 提出有符号对称量化（signed absmax grid）：通过一个轻量级的符号选择规则，将多出的一个可表示值分配给主导离群值所在的尾数侧，同时保持零点为零。理论证明该网格在ℓ₂量化误差上条件性边界最优，并证明了对称量化器的尺度取反等价于有符号整数字母表上的单位零点偏移。

Result: 理论分析表明，在低位宽下预训练大语言模型88-99%的权重分组满足该方法的条件最优性。在Qwen3、Qwen3.5和Llama3系列模型上的实验验证了该方法相比标准无符号对称量化器在困惑度和下游少样本准确率上有明显提升，且推理成本为零额外开销。

Conclusion: 有符号对称量化保留了对称量化的运行时效率，同时避免了非对称量化的性能惩罚，是一种实用的低位宽量化方案。

Abstract: The signed integer alphabet contains one more negative representable value than positive. Yet, by convention, the standard symmetric integer quantizer fixes its scale to be strictly positive, which assigns this extra representable value to the negative tail and can force clipping of positive outliers. In this work, we show that, at few-bit precision, such clipping is a non-trivial source of quantization error. Asymmetric quantization addresses this problem with a zero point, shifting the grid toward the observed data range; however, this flexibility is well-known to carry a runtime penalty. For example, in llama.cpp on an AMD EPYC(TM) "Turin" CPU, a 4-bit symmetric format uses up to 9% less memory with up to 2.45$\times$ higher throughput than its asymmetric counterpart. We highlight signed symmetric quantization as a third option that retains the runtime profile of symmetric quantization without the penalty of the asymmetric format: our signed absmax grid places the extra representable value on the dominant-outlier tail through a principled and lightweight sign selection rule while keeping the zero point at zero. Our theoretical analysis offers two main results. First, we establish the signed absmax grid as conditionally bound-optimal on $\ell_2$ quantization error, and show that the condition holds for 88-99% of weight groups across pre-trained large language models (LLMs) at low bit widths. Second, we show that negating the scale of a standard symmetric quantizer is analytically equivalent to a unit zero point shift on the same signed integer alphabet. We empirically validate our proposal on models from the Qwen3, Qwen3.5, and Llama3 families, and observe improvement in perplexity and downstream few-shot accuracy over the standard unsigned symmetric quantizer at no extra inference cost

</details>


### [123] [Sticky Routing: Training MoE Models for Memory-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08780)
*Ali Kayyam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StickyMoE通过在训练过程中引入可微分的路由一致性损失，鼓励相邻token保持相同的专家分配，从而在不改变架构的前提下，将MoE模型的专家切换率降低高达60%，而困惑度恶化不到4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中相邻token频繁激活不同专家，导致边缘设备上持续在慢速存储和快速内存之间进行权重交换，现有方法（系统级缓存启发式或事后路由器微调）未能在预训练阶段解决根本原因。

Method: 提出StickyMoE，一种可微分的路由一致性损失函数，惩罚相邻token之间的专家切换，鼓励路由器在语义连贯的片段内保持相同的专家分配。该方法无需架构改动，只需引入一个超参数lambda，且允许专家表示和路由决策从第一步训练开始共同适应。

Result: 在小规模MoE语言模型上，StickyMoE将专家切换率降低高达60%，困惑度恶化不到4%，在质量-局部性前沿上Pareto支配事后微调方法。

Conclusion: 路由时间局部性最有效的灌输方式是在训练阶段进行，而非事后处理。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models activate only a sparse subset of experts per token, yet consecutive tokens frequently activate different experts -- causing constant weight swapping between slow storage and fast memory on edge devices. Existing remedies are either system-level (caching heuristics) or post-hoc (router fine-tuning), leaving the root cause unchanged during pretraining. We propose StickyMoE, a differentiable routing consistency loss that penalises abrupt expert switches between adjacent tokens, encouraging the router to maintain the same expert assignment across semantically coherent spans. StickyMoE requires no architectural changes, adds a single hyperparameter lambda, and unlike post-hoc methods, allows expert representations and routing decisions to co-adapt from the first training step. Experiments on small-scale MoE language models show that StickyMoE reduces the expert switch rate by up to 60% with less than 4% perplexity degradation, Pareto-dominating post-hoc fine-tuning on the quality-locality frontier. Routing temporal locality is most efficiently instilled at training time.

</details>


### [124] [Reward Transport: Property Control in Flow Matching via Noise-Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08781)
*Kehan Guo,Yili Shen,Yujun Zhou,Yue Huang,Chujie Gao,Shiyi Du,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward Transport方法，通过在流匹配训练中使用最优传输耦合将分子奖励对齐到噪声空间的标量坐标上，实现无需额外计算的连续可控分子生成，并在ZINC-250K和GuacaMol数据集上验证了对logP和QED属性的单调控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流匹配中噪声与数据的耦合仅被视为计算选择，本文旨在证明该耦合可作为对齐接口，将可控结构直接嵌入到学习的流场中，从而实现对生成分布的属性控制。

Method: 在训练时使用最优传输耦合将标量噪声空间坐标与分子奖励对齐；推理时通过改变该坐标值即可引导生成分布，无需条件模型、奖励模型、梯度引导或额外计算。在耦合保持极限下，对该坐标进行阈值化可恢复交叉熵方法中的截断奖励分布。

Result: 在ZINC-250K和GuacaMol上，扫描标量坐标可实现对logP的单调控制和QED在其操作范围内的一致控制；不同目标产生相反的结构响应（logP使分子变大，QED使分子变小），排除了通用尺寸偏差；该方法与无分类器引导和条件流匹配互补。

Conclusion: 通过耦合级别对齐可在不增加推理开销的情况下实现可控生成，为流匹配模型提供了一种简洁且原理性的分布级控制手段，同时揭示了epsilon预测扩散中耦合级对齐结构缺失的局限性。

Abstract: The coupling in flow matching -- the rule pairing noise vectors with data points -- is typically treated as a computational choice. We show that this coupling can instead serve as an alignment interface: by matching noise and data according to a target molecular property, it embeds controllable structure directly into the learned flow field. Building on this view, we introduce Reward Transport, which uses optimal transport coupling at training time to align a scalar noise-space coordinate with molecular rewards; at inference, varying this coordinate steers the generated distribution without requiring an oracle, reward model, gradient guidance, or additional computation. In the coupling-preserving limit, thresholding this coordinate recovers the Cross-Entropy Method's truncated reward distribution, providing a principled, continuously adjustable distribution-level control knob. Empirically, on ZINC-250K and GuacaMol, sweeping the scalar induces monotone control of logP and consistent QED control over its operating range; most tellingly, the same knob produces opposite structural responses for different targets, growing molecules for logP but shrinking them for QED, which rules out a generic size bias. The interface is complementary to classifier-free guidance and conditional flow matching, while a negative result under epsilon-prediction diffusion clarifies where coupling-level alignment is structurally absent. Code: https://github.com/KehanGuo2/reward-transport

</details>


### [125] [Director: Accelerating Distributed MoE Serving via Online Proactive Expert Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08782)
*Qianli Liu,Kaibin Guo,Zicong Hong,Peng Li,Fahao Chen,Haodong Wang,Jian Lin,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Director是一个面向MoE模型的新型分布式推理系统，通过预测驱动的在线专家放置优化，在多项式时间内实现(1+ε)近似比，相比现有工作降低11~55%的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家放置优化方法依赖历史请求的专家激活模式，难以应对多样化和快速变化的请求模式，缺乏在线、主动的放置优化方案。

Method: 1) 轻量级级联预测器或低比特量化副本预测请求的专家激活模式；2) 在线迁移模块在计算密集型阶段执行迁移，实现近零停机；3) 基于松弛优化的专家放置算法，在容量约束下多项式时间内达到(1+ε)近似比。

Result: 在Mistral、DeepSeek、Qwen等主流MoE模型上，相比现有工作降低11~55%的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: Director通过预测驱动的在线专家放置优化，有效应对动态请求模式，显著降低MoE模型推理的端到端延迟。

Abstract: Expert parallelism has become the prevailing paradigm to serve Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Its efficiency depends on the communication and computation latencies of the GPUs, which are linked to the placement of experts in the GPUs. Existing works for optimizing expert placement focus on leveraging past requests' expert activation patterns. However, they demonstrate deficiencies facing diverse and rapidly changing request patterns, calling for an online, proactive approach. Implementing such an approach requires addressing several challenges: the uncertainty associated with incoming requests' expert activation, the cost of expert migration, and the NP-hard complexity in optimization. Therefore, we present Director, a new distributed MoE serving system that minimizes end-to-end latency via prediction-driven, online expert placement. Director uses either a lightweight cascaded predictor or a low-bit quantized replica for expert activation patterns of incoming requests. An online migration module then enacts the changes with near-zero downtime by executing migrations in compute-bound phases, keeping disruption bounded. At its core, a relaxation-based expert placement optimizer operates under capacity constraints, runs in polynomial time, and achieves a $(1+ε)$ approximation ratio. Finally, we implement a prototype and demonstrate, through extensive experiments, a reduction in end-to-end latency of $11\sim55\%$ for popular MoE models (e.g., Mistral, DeepSeek and Qwen) compared to existing work.

</details>


### [126] [LieBN: Batch Normalization over Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08783)
*Ziheng Chen,Yue Song,Rui Wang,Xiao-Jun Wu,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LieBN框架，利用李群上自然存在的左/右不变度量，实现适用于多种流形的黎曼批量归一化，并在9种几何结构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼归一化方法要么针对特定流形设计，要么无法有效归一化流形值样本分布，缺乏通用且有效的框架。

Method: 提出LieBN框架，利用李群上天然的左/右不变度量，提供控制黎曼均值和方差的理论保证。在9种几何结构上实例化（SPD流形4种、旋转矩阵群1种、满秩相关矩阵流形4种），其中引入了一种新的右不变度量，并通过矩阵幂变形扩展了三种现有李群结构。

Result: 在不同流形上的大量实验验证了LieBN框架的有效性。

Conclusion: LieBN提供了一个通用且理论保障的黎曼批量归一化框架，可适用于多种流形几何，拓展了流形深度学习中归一化技术的适用范围。

Abstract: Manifold-valued measurements are prevalent in various machine learning tasks. Recent advances have extended Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) to operate on manifolds, accompanied by normalization techniques tailored to different geometries, collectively referred to as Riemannian normalization. However, most existing Riemannian normalization methods are either designed for specific manifolds or fail to effectively normalize manifold-valued sample distributions. To address these limitations, we propose LieBN, a framework for Riemannian Batch Normalization (RBN) over Lie groups. Our approach leverages the theoretically convenient left- and right-invariant metrics, which naturally exist in every Lie group, and provides theoretical guarantees for controlling the Riemannian mean and variance. We instantiate LieBN across nine distinct geometries: four on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, one on the group of rotation matrices, and four on the manifold of full-rank correlation matrices. Notably, among the SPD metrics, we introduce a novel right-invariant metric and extend three existing Lie group structures via matrix power deformation. Extensive experiments on different manifolds validate the effectiveness of our framework. The code is available at https://github.com/GitZH-Chen/LieBN.git.

</details>


### [127] [HERO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Benchmark Library for Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08784)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Le-Tuan Nguyen,Minh-Duong Nguyen,Nhi Trinh,Anh Tran Nam Nguyet,Dung D. Le,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HERO基准库，通过解耦任务分割、客户端数据分割和任务序列三个维度，实现联邦持续学习(FCL)方法的可重复、可对比评估，实验表明方法性能随异质性变化、平均准确率掩盖低分客户端表现、任务顺序错配与同步评估偏好不同策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FCL评估因同时改变任务分割、客户端数据分割、任务顺序、骨干网络、内存假设和报告规则而难以比较，缺乏统一、可重复的基准。

Method: 提出HERO基准库，解耦任务分割、客户端数据分割和客户端任务序列三个通常耦合的选项；HERO-Core中用α控制客户端数据偏斜、ρ控制任务顺序错配；支持图像基准(CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet)和图域增量学习案例(OGB-MolPCBA)。

Result: 不同方法在简单与异质设置下表现不同；平均准确率隐藏了低分客户端性能；任务顺序错配与同步评估偏好不同策略；HERO接口可扩展至图域增量学习。

Conclusion: HERO提供了基准流、配置、方法实现和报告脚本，支持可重复且对异质性敏感的FCL评估。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) evaluates how distributed clients learn from changing data streams while retaining previously learned knowledge. Existing evaluations are difficult to compare because they often change datasets, task splits, client data splits, task orders, backbones, memory assumptions, and reporting rules simultaneously. We introduce \textbf{HERO}, a heterogeneity-aware benchmark library for FCL. HERO builds benchmark streams by separating three choices that are often coupled, namely the task split, the client data split, and the client task sequence. In HERO-Core, the main comparable benchmark, $α$ controls client data skew and $ρ$ controls task-order mismatch. We evaluate representative FCL methods on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet using final average accuracy, average forgetting, and bottom-10\% client accuracy. We also include a graph-based Domain-IL portability case study on OGB-MolPCBA, where scaffold-domain granularity changes the input distribution while the prediction task remains fixed. Our results show that method behavior changes across easy and heterogeneous settings, that average accuracy can hide weak bottom-client performance, that task-order mismatch favors different strategies from synchronized evaluation, and that the same HERO interface can expose domain-shift difficulty beyond image-based FCIL. HERO releases benchmark streams, configurations, method implementations, and reporting scripts to support reproducible and setting-aware FCL evaluation.

</details>


### [128] [DaDaDa: A Dataset for Data Pricing in Data Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08785)
*Qiheng Sun,Hongwei Zhang,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DaDaDa是首个数据产品定价数据集，包含来自9个主要数据市场的16,147个数据产品元数据，通过训练定价模型为数据产品建立价格基准，并支持分类和检索等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数据产品具有近乎零边际成本和收入不可预测的特殊属性，传统成本法和收益法无法有效定价，而市场比较法又缺乏标准化定价基准，因此亟需构建数据产品定价数据集来建立价格基准。

Method: 收集了来自9个主要数据市场的16,147个数据产品元数据，构建了首个数据产品定价数据集DaDaDa，可用于训练定价模型、建立价格基准，并支持数据产品分类和检索任务。

Result: 通过实验和检索原型验证了DaDaDa在数据产品定价、分类和检索任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: DaDaDa数据集有效解决了数据产品定价缺乏基准的问题，为数据市场中的定价、分类和检索提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: High-quality data drives machine learning advances across industries. Recognizing the value of data, data transactions are increasingly common, giving rise to many data marketplaces, e.g., AWS Marketplace, Databricks, and Datarade. However, determining the appropriate prices for data products remains a significant challenge due to the unique properties of data products. Traditional pricing methods in economics can be categorized into the cost approach, the income approach, and the sales comparison approach. The cost approach fails in data pricing due to near-zero marginal cost from data replication, and the income approach fails due to inherently unpredictable data revenue. The sales comparison approach remains viable, yet its application is hindered by the absence of standardized pricing benchmarks for data products across marketplaces. To address this challenge, we introduce \texttt{DaDaDa}, the first dataset for data product pricing, containing metadata for 16,147 data products from 9 major data marketplaces worldwide. \texttt{DaDaDa} enables the training of pricing models, thereby establishing price benchmarks for new data products. In addition, \texttt{DaDaDa} can be utilized for other important tasks in data markets, such as data product classification and retrieval. Experiments and a retrieval prototype demonstrate the effectiveness of \texttt{DaDaDa} for pricing, classification, and retrieval of data products. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DaDaDa.

</details>


### [129] [Accelerating GPU Inference of Large Language Models with Moderately Unstructured Sparse Weight Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08786)
*Tao Lu,Haoyu Wang,Zonghui Wang,Keshen Xiang,Jiaheng Zhang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对中等稀疏度（约50%）LLM权重矩阵的高效GPU推理方法，通过创新的三层矩阵存储格式（Sparse-TC层、Slot-Filling层、Residual层）联合利用稀疏张量核心和CUDA核心，首次在配备HBM的现代GPU上超越稠密矩阵乘法，最高实现1.64x内核级加速和1.41x端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理成本日益成为关键挑战。剪枝技术引入的稀疏性可加速推理，但现有方法在维持模型质量的前提下只能达到约50%的中等非结构化稀疏度，在此稀疏度下现有GPU稀疏矩阵乘法（SpMM）内核无法超越稠密矩阵乘法。

Method: 提出三层矩阵存储格式：(i) Sparse-TC层使稀疏张量核心加速SpMM；(ii) Slot-Filling层利用并行差分距离进行矩阵压缩并支持低开销片上解码；(iii) 轻量级Residual层确保正确SpMM计算。基于此格式设计联合利用稀疏张量核心和CUDA核心的SpMM内核，实现高效执行流水线并重叠片上计算与内存访问。

Result: 在配备HBM的现代GPU上首次超越稠密矩阵乘法，内核级相比SpInfer (EuroSys'25, Best Paper)最高加速1.64倍，端到端相比FlashLLM (VLDB'24)最高加速1.41倍。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效解决了中等稀疏度下SpMM无法超越稠密矩阵乘法的问题，通过创新的存储格式和异构核心协同计算，实现了显著的推理加速。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of large language models (LLMs), LLM inference cost has become a key challenge. Pruning techniques that introduce sparsity into weight matrices can accelerate inference. However, maintaining model quality typically limits pruning to moderate unstructured sparsity (around 50\%). At these sparsity levels, none of the existing GPU kernels for sparse matrix multiplication (SpMM) can outperform their dense counterparts. This paper proposes an efficient GPU inference method for LLMs with moderate sparsity. We propose a three-layer matrix storage format comprising: (i) a Sparse-TC layer enabling sparse tensor cores to accelerate SpMM; (ii) a Slot-Filling layer using parallel differential distance for matrix compression while supporting low-cost on-chip decoding; (iii) a lightweight Residual Layer ensuring correct SpMM computation. Building on this format, we design a SpMM kernel that jointly utilizes sparse tensor cores and CUDA cores. This design enables an efficient execution pipeline and overlaps on-chip computation with memory access. Evaluations show that our work is the first to outperform dense matrix multiplication on modern GPUs equipped with high-bandwidth memory (HBM). It achieves up to 1.64x kernel-level speedup over SpInfer (EuroSys'25, Best paper) and up to 1.41x end-to-end speedups over FlashLLM (VLDB'24). Our source code: https://github.com/moui0/cudac.

</details>


### [130] [Adaptive Bayes exactly tracks information over intrinsic time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08789)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，贝叶斯更新和乘法权重更新所产生的遗憾（regret）遵循精确的信息核算恒等式：每一轮中，学习者的超额损失等于当轮不确定性支付（内在时间）加上与比较器之间信息距离的减少量。这种路径依赖的分解是精确的而非上界，涵盖Hedge、乐观变体、连续先验、Boosting、在线凸优化、上下文赌博机及重复博弈等多种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遗憾分析多采用上界形式，在随机或低噪声场景下会产生松弛（slack），无法精确刻画遗憾的来源与构成。作者希望建立一个精确的、路径依赖的遗憾分解框架，使得不同场景下的优化行为能够通过同一信息核算恒等式统一理解。

Method: 作者建立了贝叶斯更新和乘法权重更新的精确信息核算恒等式：每轮超额损失 = 当轮不确定性支付（内在时间） + 从当前权重到比较器的信息距离减少量。通过将单步平衡求和，得到两种累积遗憾的精确自适应分解方式。该分析覆盖了Hedge算法、乐观变体、边信息变体、连续先验、Boosting、在线凸优化、上下文赌博机和重复博弈等多种设置。

Result: 得到了精确而非上界的遗憾分解恒等式；在随机或低噪声场景下，有利性质表现为实现的内在时间（intrinsic time）的自有界性质，而非最坏情况分析中的松弛；展示了该框架在多种经典算法和问题设置中的统一适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为贝叶斯更新和乘法权重更新的遗憾分析提供了一个统一的、精确的信息核算视角。路径依赖的内在时间概念能够自然捕捉不同场景下的学习动态，使得随机和低噪声情况下的性能优势得到精确刻画，无需依赖最坏情况分析的松弛项。

Abstract: Bayesian and multiplicative-weights updates reweight experts, models, or actions from sequential feedback. We show that the regret of any such update obeys an exact information-accounting identity. On each round, the learner's excess loss to any chosen comparator is the sum of an immediate payment for the uncertainty exposed by the round and a reduction in the information distance from the learner's current weights to the comparator. The cumulative payment defines a pathwise uncertainty clock, the \emph{intrinsic time} of the realized sequence. Summing one-step balances yields two exact adaptive decompositions of cumulative regret, one for each natural way of composing the update across rounds. Because the decompositions are exact rather than upper bounds, favorable stochastic or low-noise regimes appear as self-bounding properties of the realized intrinsic time, not as slack in worst-case analyses. The same calculus covers Hedge, optimistic and side-information variants, continuous priors, boosting, online convex optimization, contextual bandits, and repeated games: the pathwise account is the same in every case.

</details>


### [131] [Prompt-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08837)
*Sunshine Jiang,John Marangola,David Zhang,Raghuram Kowdeed,Ruiyang Luo,Nitish Dashora,Richard Li,Pulkit Agrawal,Zhang-Wei Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出提示驱动探索（PDE），利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）从 rollout 视频中推理并重写自然语言提示，实现全局策略探索，使强化学习能从零奖励起点学习成功策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL的探索方法仅在动作空间注入随机性，仅能产生局部扰动，难以帮助弱策略逃出局部最优。需要一种能引发全局行为变化的探索机制。

Method: 利用VLM对rollout视频进行推理，诊断策略失败原因，并重写自然语言提示以引导策略在下一轮产生更好的全局行为。该方法实现了提示层面的后验采样（posterior sampling）。

Result: 在操作任务和推理任务上，PDE使RL能从零奖励起点学习到成功策略，并广泛提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 利用VLM在提示空间进行探索是一种有效的全局探索策略，能将后验采样框架应用于提示层面，弥补传统动作噪声探索的局限。

Abstract: Exploration is essential to RL since a policy cannot improve by repeatedly sampling the behaviors it already prefers. Standard methods inject stochasticity in the action space, but such jitter only yields rollouts close to the original. Escaping a weak policy often requires global perturbations that action noise cannot produce. Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a pathway: they condition the policy on a natural language prompt, and since the rollout follows from it, modifying the prompt induces global changes. The challenge is finding prompts that induce useful global changes. With a weak policy that rarely succeeds, reward is too sparse to select on. Our idea is to refine prompts from the rollouts themselves: a vision-language model (VLM) reasons over the rollout video, diagnoses how the policy responded, and rewrites the prompt to elicit better behavior next time. This procedure realizes posterior sampling, a classical RL exploration framework, at the level of prompts: the VLM maintains an implicit distribution over useful prompts and updates it from observed rollouts. We call this strategy Prompt-Driven Exploration (PDE). Across manipulation and reasoning tasks, PDE enables RL to learn successful policies even from zero-reward starts, and improves sample efficiency more broadly. Our website is available at https://xinyunsunshine.github.io/prompt-rl.

</details>


### [132] [How are linear representations learned? Exact solutions to the dynamics of abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08843)
*William W. Yang,Andrew M. Saxe,Peter E. Latham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络训练过程中概念方向（线性表示）如何随时间演化的动力学机制，提出了"抽象化"（abstraction）框架，在线性网络中得到精确解，揭示数据几何、目标几何、网络深度和初始化尺度的影响，并将理论推广到非线性网络，发现非线性激活会削弱抽象化程度的衰减规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究关注训练后概念方向是否存在（线性表示假说），但这些方向在训练过程中如何涌现的动力学机制尚不清楚。理解这一过程对于可解释性和神经调控至关重要。

Method: 构建"抽象化"框架研究训练过程中概念方向的对齐过程。在线性最小网络设置下获得抽象化完整轨迹的精确解；将理论扩展到非线性网络（erf和ReLU），分析非线性选择对抽象化动力学的影响；在开源模型（DINOv3, Gemma 4）中验证理论预测。

Result: (i) 数据几何和目标几何共同决定学习结束时的抽象化程度；(ii) 网络深度提升抽象化；(iii) 初始化尺度控制训练中可达的最大抽象化程度。非线性网络中，erf网络近似线性理论，ReLU网络的抽象化更依赖输入几何而非目标几何。发现衰减规律：两种非线性均削弱激活值相对于预激活值的抽象化程度。

Conclusion: 本文提供了概念抽象化过程的动力学理论，有助于理解神经网络的可解释性和控制方法，并展示了如何应用该理论改进LLM中线性探针的泛化能力。

Abstract: In artificial and biological neural networks, concepts are often encoded as consistent linear directions in representation space. In deep learning, this idea is known as the linear representation hypothesis and underpins many interpretability and control methods based on linear probes, from concept detection to activation steering. Yet while prior work has studied whether such directions should exist $\textit{after}$ training, the dynamics of how they emerge $\textit{during}$ training remain poorly understood. Here, we develop a framework to study the alignment of concept directions during training - a process we call "abstraction". In a minimal linear network setting, we obtain exact solutions for the full trajectory of abstraction. These solutions reveal key analytic principles governing abstraction: (i) data and target geometry jointly determine abstraction at the end-of-learning, (ii) abstraction improves with network depth, and (iii) initialization scale controls the maximum abstraction reached during training. Extending our theory to nonlinear networks, we analyze how the choice of nonlinearity affects abstraction dynamics: erf networks approximate the linear theory, while abstraction in ReLU networks depends less on target geometry and more on input geometry. Across both, we prove a striking attenuation law: both nonlinearities weaken abstraction in activations relative to preactivations. We find evidence for this law in open models (DINOv3, Gemma 4) and apply our theory to improve linear probe generalization in LLMs. Together, our results provide a dynamical theory of abstraction with implications for interpretability and control.

</details>


### [133] [Optimizing Against Safety Representations: Activation-Guided Adversarial Suffixes and the Geometry of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08883)
*Ege Çakar,Hannah Guan,Kayden Kehe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型中安全表征的结构与脆弱性，引入Activation-Guided GCG和Soft-GCG两种方法攻击模型的内部安全方向，发现安全表征分布在整个前向传播过程中而非局部化，并提出连续松弛优化实现33倍加速，同时揭示小模型更易受攻击而大模型抵抗性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大型语言模型中安全表征的结构特性（是否局部化）及其脆弱性，探究通过激活空间攻击安全方向的可行性。

Method: 提出Activation-Guided GCG（用内部拒绝方向损失替代输出目标）和Soft-GCG（使用Gumbel-Softmax对离散后缀优化进行连续松弛），在不同层和位置维度上测试攻击效果。

Result: 全局抑制拒绝方向比单层-位置定位更有效；Soft-GCG实现33倍加速且提高攻击成功率；小模型易受攻击，大模型在计算受限条件下表现出更强抵抗性。

Conclusion: 安全表征是分布式的而非局部化；对抗性后缀可通过优化激活表征绕过安全机制；为构建更鲁棒的、表征感知的对齐策略提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Behavioral alignment in large language models often masks fragile internal safety representations. Recent work suggests that refusal behavior is mediated by low-dimensional directions in activation space. This raises questions about how such representations are structured, localized, and accessed by optimization. We study adversarial suffix attacks as a probe of representational alignment. We introduce Activation-Guided GCG, which replaces output-based objectives with losses that directly target a model's internal refusal direction. Across several objective variants, we find that suppressing refusal globally across all layers and positions is more effective than targeting a single layer-position pair. This suggests that safety representations are distributed across the forward pass rather than causally localized to a single site. We further introduce Soft-GCG, a continuous relaxation of discrete suffix optimization using Gumbel-Softmax. Soft-GCG achieves a 33 $\times$ speedup over standard GCG while improving attack success rates. Evaluating across model scales, we find that smaller models remain vulnerable while larger models resist both activation- and suffix-based attacks at our compute-constrained settings, consistent with larger and better safety trained models being harder to jailbreak. Together, our results clarify how safety mechanisms are encoded and can be broken in contemporary models. These insights provide concrete guidance for designing more robust and representation-aware alignment strategies.

</details>


### [134] [Pattern-Aware Graph Neural Networks for Handling Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08915)
*Minett Tran,Taehee Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种模式感知图神经网络，通过显式编码缺失值模式来处理缺失数据，在7个UCI数据集上平均平衡准确率提升17%，F1-macro提升22%。研究发现，即使简单的随机模式嵌入也能取得与学习嵌入相当的效果，且注意力机制在模式信息可用时并非关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集普遍存在缺失值问题，传统方法要么丢弃不完整样本，要么使用忽略缺失模式的插补技术，隐含地假设缺失是随机发生的。然而，缺失模式本身可能包含额外信息，现有方法未能充分利用这一信息。

Method: 提出了四种模式编码策略：学习嵌入、冻结随机嵌入、统计特征和层次化表示，将这些信息融入图神经网络中。在7个具有自然缺失的UCI数据集上进行评估。

Result: 模式感知方法在平衡准确率上平均提升17%，F1-macro平均提升22%。不同数据集差异显著：annealing数据集提升80%，hepatitis和soybean仅提升4-5%。随机模式嵌入（0.650）与学习嵌入（0.663）性能相当。简单均值聚合（0.640）与注意力变体（0.645）差距微小。

Conclusion: 区分不同缺失模式比任务特定的参数优化更为重要；当模式信息可用时，注意力机制并非关键组件。

Abstract: Missing data is ubiquitous in real-world datasets. Traditional methods either discard incomplete samples or apply imputation techniques that ignore potentially informative missingness patterns, implicitly assuming that missingness occurs randomly. However, missingness patterns might provide additional information. We propose pattern-aware graph neural networks that explicitly encode which features are missing alongside observed values. We used four encoding strategies -- learned embeddings, frozen random embeddings, statistical features, and hierarchical representations -- across seven UCI datasets with naturally occurring missingness. Our Pattern-aware methods achieve substantial improvements over baselines, with an average improvement of 17\% in balanced accuracy and 22\% in F1-macro across all datasets. The benefits vary significantly by dataset: annealing shows dramatic improvement (+80\% balanced accuracy), while hepatitis and soybean show minimal gains (+4--5\%). Notably, even simple random pattern embeddings perform comparably to learned embeddings (0.650 vs 0.663 balanced accuracy), suggesting that distinguishing between patterns may be more important than task-specific optimization. Our ablation study reveals that attention mechanisms, while helpful, are not critical when pattern information is available -- simple mean aggregation with pattern awareness achieves 0.640 balanced accuracy compared to 0.645 for attention-based variants.

</details>


### [135] [A Machine Learning Surrogate for Component Criticality Ranking in Interdependent Power-Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08918)
*Sohini Roy,Xheni Hylviu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电力-通信耦合系统中连锁故障评估计算量大的问题，基于改进隐含相互依赖模型（MIIM）开发了机器学习代理模型，利用无泄露结构特征预测事故严重性和组件关键性排序。在IEEE 118节点系统上，梯度提升代理模型实现了0.849的严重性预测斯皮尔曼相关系数和0.853的关键性排序相关系数，达到与MIIM本身相当的精度上限。特征消融实验表明，代理模型优势主要来自层间依赖信息。研究支持先由代理模型快速排序、再以MIIM选择性验证的两阶段工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力-通信耦合系统（信息物理电力系统）中连锁故障风险评估对弹性规划至关重要，但使用高保真模拟器在大规模N-k事故集上进行评估计算成本极高，亟需高效的替代方法。

Method: 以改进隐含相互依赖模型（MIIM）为地面真值级联模拟器，提取无泄漏结构特征，训练梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）机器学习代理模型，用于预测事故严重性和推导组件关键性排序。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统上，代理模型在事故严重性预测上达到斯皮尔曼相关系数0.849，组件关键性排序上达到0.853；在三个独立采样数据集上保持稳定。MIIM本身在当前采样流程下仅能达到约0.85的相关性，代理模型达到此经验上限。拓扑中心性指标基线为0.60-0.69，特征消融表明层间依赖信息是关键优势来源。

Conclusion: 提出两阶段工作流程：先用代理模型快速排序候选组件，再保留MIIM进行选择性验证，在保证评估精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为弹性规划提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: Cyber-physical power systems are vulnerable to cascading failures caused by tight interdependencies between power and communication infrastructures. Evaluating these failures over large N-k contingency sets with a high-fidelity simulator is computationally prohibitive for resilience planning. Using the previously published Modified Implicative Interdependency Model (MIIM) as the ground-truth cascade simulator, this paper develops a machine-learning surrogate that predicts contingency severity from leakage-free structural features and derives a component-criticality ranking for prioritized hardening analysis. On the IEEE 118-bus system, the Gradient Boosting surrogate achieves Spearman correlations of 0.849 for per-contingency severity prediction and 0.853 for per-component criticality ranking, while remaining stable across three independently sampled datasets. MIIM-derived component criticality itself reproduces only to a Spearman of approximately 0.85 under the present sampling pipeline, and the surrogate operates at this empirical ceiling to within sampling variation. Topological centrality measures on the full interdependent network provide meaningful baselines (Spearman 0.60-0.69), and feature ablation shows that the surrogate's advantage is driven primarily by inter-layer dependency information. These results support a two-stage workflow in which the surrogate rapidly ranks candidate components and MIIM is reserved for selective verification.

</details>


### [136] [SafeExplorer: An Unbiased Policy Gradient for Reinforcement Learning with Recovery Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08925)
*Elham Daneshmand,Majid Khadiv,Glen Berseth,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无偏策略梯度估计方法，通过仅在安全时间步使用得分函数，避免评估恢复策略密度，解决了混合策略 rollout 对 on-policy 更新的偏差问题。结合恢复触发状态闭式值函数和模仿损失，在三个机器人环境中将训练期跌倒次数减少26-233倍，同时保持或超越标准PPO的最终奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理机器人上的强化学习训练中，每次跌倒都会造成实际损坏且无法像仿真器重置那样撤销，因此需要最小化训练期间的跌倒次数。现有的约束MDP方法和安全区域+恢复策略的混合策略方法存在偏差问题，且重要性采样修正无法处理确定性恢复策略的情况。

Method: 提出一种对PPO的即插即用修改：(1) 无偏策略梯度估计器，仅使用安全时间步的得分函数，不评估恢复策略密度；(2) 恢复触发状态的闭式值函数（当动力学和恢复均为确定性时）；(3) 模仿损失，仅在恢复成功时复制恢复动作。三者结合形成算法。

Result: 在HalfCheetah、Ant和Unitree Go1三个环境上，与标准PPO相比，训练期跌倒次数分别减少233倍、48倍和26倍，同时最终奖励匹配或超过PPO。在Ant环境中（恢复策略不可靠），该方法是唯一能达到最佳最终奖励80%的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了混合策略rollout的偏差问题，在显著减少训练期跌倒次数的同时不牺牲最终性能，特别适用于恢复策略不可靠或确定性的场景，优于重要性采样方法。

Abstract: Training reinforcement-learning agents directly on physical robots makes every fall costly, since a fall can damage the platform and cannot be undone like a simulator reset; the goal is therefore to minimize falls during training rather than trade them off against return, as constrained Markov decision process (MDP) formulations do. A standard mitigation hands control to a separate recovery policy whenever the agent leaves a designer-specified safe region (a subset of state space it should stay within), but the resulting mixed-policy rollouts silently bias every on-policy update, and the importance-sampling correction that would remove this bias is ill-defined whenever the recovery policy is deterministic. We address this bias with a drop-in modification of proximal policy optimization (PPO). Its core is an unbiased policy-gradient estimator that uses the score function only at safe timesteps and never evaluates the recovery policy's density, so it stays valid even when the recovery policy is deterministic, exactly where importance sampling breaks, and it empirically dominates importance sampling even when the recovery policy is stochastic. Because the recovery policy still makes credit assignment slow near the safe-region boundary, two further components accelerate learning: a closed-form value for recovery-triggering states when dynamics and recovery are deterministic, and an imitation loss that copies recovery actions only when recovery succeeds. On a three-environment, five-seed benchmark, the resulting algorithm reduces training-time falls by factors of 233x, 48x, and 26x on HalfCheetah, Ant, and Unitree Go1 over standard PPO, while matching or exceeding PPO's final reward, and on Ant, where the recovery policy is unreliable, it is the only method that reaches 80% of the best final reward.

</details>


### [137] [BlockServe: Block-Grained Continuous Batching for High-Throughput Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08930)
*Yuanjie Zhu,Liangwei Yang,Ke Xu,Weizhi Zhang,Shanghao Li,Zihe Song,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlockServe提出了一种基于块粒度调度的连续批处理框架，通过混合状态执行和计算感知准入控制，解决了扩散大语言模型推理中的收敛异构性问题，在Dream和LLaDA上实现了1.9-10.6倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的服务效率受限于收敛异构性：批处理多个请求时，不同序列以不同速率收敛，导致快速请求被慢速拖后腿，产生计算气泡和尾部延迟。

Method: 1) 块粒度调度（Block-grained scheduling）：在块边界处立即驱逐已完成请求；2) 混合状态执行（Mixed-state execution）：通过收集-分散索引扩展双缓存和并行解码以处理异构批次；3) 计算感知准入控制（Compute-aware admission controller）：通过令牌预算补给扩展有效批容量。

Result: 在Dream和LLaDA两个模型、五个基准测试上，相较于Fast-dLLM实现了1.9-10.6倍的吞吐量提升，且生成质量相当。

Conclusion: 块粒度调度可作为高吞吐量离线dLLM推理的基础框架，有效缓解收敛异构性带来的效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Efficient serving of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is hindered by convergence heterogeneity: when batching multiple requests, different sequences converge at different rates, causing faster requests to stall behind slower stragglers and introducing compute bubbles and tail latency. We present BlockServe, a continuous batching framework that integrates block-grained scheduling -- immediately evicting completed requests at block boundaries -- with mixed-state execution that extends dual cache and parallel decoding to heterogeneous batches via gather-scatter indexing. Furthermore, a compute-aware admission controller expands effective batch capacity through token-budgeted refill. On Dream and LLaDA across five benchmarks, BlockServe achieves 1.9--10.6$\times$ throughput over Fast-dLLM with comparable generation quality, establishing block-grained scheduling as a foundation for high-throughput offline dLLM inference.

</details>


### [138] [TSRouter: Dynamic Modality-Model Selection for Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08940)
*Fangxu Yu,Tao Feng,Dehai Min,Lu Cheng,Ge Liu,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSRouter是一个基于图的动态路由框架，通过构建任务、查询、模态和模型节点的异构图来建模它们之间的复杂交互，从而为时间序列推理任务动态选择最优的模态-模型组合，在4个任务上相比基线实现了16%至46%的相对提升，并展现出零样本泛化能力和成本感知优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和VLM在处理时间序列数据时能力互补：LLM擅长精确数值理解但难以把握全局模式，VLM能高效捕捉可视化模式但可能丢失细粒度细节。不同模型在任务专长和推理成本上差异显著，因此需要为每个查询动态选择最合适的模态和模型，但这一过程因需要建模任务、查询、模态和模型之间的复杂交互而具有挑战性。

Method: TSRouter构建包含任务、查询、模态和模型节点的异构图，对查询特征、模态属性和模型能力之间的交互进行上下文建模。将路由问题形式化为候选评分问题，基于用户定义的性能-成本偏好对每个模态-模型对进行评估，从而选择最优候选。

Result: 在4个不同的时间序列推理任务上的综合评估显示，TSRouter相比多种基线实现了16%至46%的相对提升。此外，TSRouter展现出对未见过的模型和新任务的鲁棒零样本即插即用泛化能力，并通过成本感知优化在降低计算开销的同时保持高性能。

Conclusion: TSRouter通过图结构动态路由有效解决了时间序列推理中模态和模型选择的难题，在性能、泛化性和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Time series reasoning is essential for real-world problem-solving. While both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can reason about time-series data, their capabilities are complementary: LLMs process time series as text sequences and thus preserve exact numerical understanding, but struggle with global patterns, whereas VLMs efficiently capture these patterns by visualizing time series but may lose fine-grained details. Moreover, models vary significantly in task-specific expertise and inference costs. Dynamically selecting the most suitable modality and model for each query is therefore crucial, yet challenging because it requires modeling the complex interactions among tasks, queries, modalities, and models, which carry rich contextual signals. To this end, we introduce TSRouter, a graph-based dynamic routing framework. TSRouter constructs a heterogeneous graph of task, query, modality, and model nodes to contextualize the interactions among query characteristics, modality attributes, and model capabilities. TSRouter formulates routing as a candidate scoring problem, where each modality-model pair is evaluated based on user-defined performance-cost preferences to select the optimal candidate. Comprehensive evaluations on 4 distinct time series reasoning tasks reveal that TSRouter substantially outperforms diverse baselines with 16\% to 46\% relative improvements. Furthermore, TSRouter demonstrates robust zero-shot plug-and-play generalization to unseen models and novel tasks and preserves high performance while reducing computational overhead through cost-aware optimization. Our code is available at https://github.com/tianyi-lab/TSRouter.

</details>


### [139] [Training, Reading, and Editing Legible Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08946)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过清晰度惩罚和方差约束构建可解释Transformer的方法，使78%的前馈算子和50%的注意力通道变为清晰且上下文相关的检测器，同时编辑定位性提升50-184倍，且质量与基线持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可解释的Transformer——使算子成为边界清晰、可命名的模糊集运算，而非密集激活的黑箱，但训练中施加的清晰度压力存在导致算子坍缩为死常数的失败模式。

Method: 通过恒等式E[v(1-v)] = μ(1-μ) - var揭示清晰度惩罚本质是方差最小化器，提出每个通道的方差下限作为损失函数；引入可学习的每单元分数替代手工设定的保留GELU分区；施加单元间去相关压力作为可解释性旋钮。

Result: 78%前馈算子和50%注意力值通道为清晰且上下文相关的检测器；逐头可解释性从浅层的18%升至深层的78%；深层编辑定位性提升50-184倍；模型保留0%纯GELU单元，87%负载计算通过清晰算子完成；质量与常规基线持平。

Conclusion: 通过正确的损失设计，可以在不牺牲质量的前提下构建高度可解释的Transformer，清晰算子支持局部编辑和显式逻辑组合，去相关压力可在无质量损失下调节电路复用与独立性的权衡。

Abstract: A transformer can be built from operators that are legible by construction -- bounded, named units that read as fuzzy set operations rather than dense activations -- but legibility must be pressed for during training, and the pressure has a failure mode. A crispness penalty meant to sharpen a bounded operator into a decisive detector instead collapses it into a dead constant. An identity, E[v(1-v)] = mu(1-mu) - var, shows why -- the penalty is a variance-minimizer blind to the difference between a live detector and a constant -- and names the fix: a per-channel variance floor, the target legibility metric written as a loss, which recovers both legibility and quality. A learned per-unit fraction then retires the hand-set reserved-GELU partition of prior work: given the choice the model keeps no unit as pure GELU and routes 87% of its load-bearing computation through crisp operators. The result is the most legible transformer we have built -- 78% of its feed-forward operands and 50% of its attention value channels are crisp-and-contextual detectors, and per-head legibility rises from 18% in shallow layers to 78% in deep ones. Read in the correct rotated per-layer frame, these units separate a clean detection (what a unit responds to) from a harder naming (what its output decodes to); and because the objective makes each unit crisp and sparse, edits to them are far more local -- 50-184x in the deep layers where the edit sites concentrate -- and can target explicit conjunctions a single neuron cannot express. Finally, a between-unit decorrelation pressure exposes a legibility dial: it trades a circuit's reuse for independence at no quality cost, turning concepts into single, surgically editable units and a prediction into a short explanation read off a handful of named operations. Quality holds at parity with a conventional baseline throughout.

</details>


### [140] [FairSelect: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Level and Intersectional Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08953)
*Nick Souligne,Isabella Mixton-Garcia,Vignesh Subbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairSelect是一个用于系统性评估公平性缓解策略的工具包，支持预处理、处理中、后处理阶段的单独和组合应用，涵盖交叉子群评估和公平性-效用权衡分析。在合成数据集和真实临床预测任务中验证发现，公平性干预存在非加性且依赖于上下文的交互效应，某些组合能同时提升公平性和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性方法大多在孤立环境下沿单一人口统计轴评估，缺乏跨交叉子群和建模生命周期多个阶段的系统性策略选择指导。

Method: FairSelect工具包支持预处理、处理中、后处理阶段的公平性缓解策略单独或组合应用，涵盖多种模型架构、交叉子群评估以及基线、单一方法和多层级配置间的公平性-效用权衡比较。在合成临床数据集和真实房颤患者两年卒中风险预测任务上进行验证。

Result: 合成实验表明针对性公平性方法普遍减少子群差异，组合策略产生更大平均公平性改进且效用权衡适中。临床预测任务中缓解效果高度可变，部分组合同时提升公平性和预测性能，其他组合无效甚至适得其反。

Conclusion: 公平性干预存在非加性且依赖于上下文的交互效应，FairSelect为系统性识别改善子群公平性同时保持模型性能的策略提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness methods are increasingly used to identify and mitigate bias in machine learning models, yet most approaches are evaluated in isolation and along single demographic axes. This limits practical guidance for selecting fairness strategies, where disparities may arise across intersectional subgroups and across multiple stages of the modeling lifecycle. This work presents FairSelect, a toolkit for systematically evaluating fairness mitigation strategies applied individually and in combination across preprocessing, inprocessing, and postprocessing stages. FairSelect supports multiple model architectures, intersectional subgroup evaluation, and comparison of fairness utility tradeoffs across baseline, single method, and multi level configurations. The framework was validated using synthetic clinical datasets designed to represent specific bias mechanisms and a real-world replication of two-year stroke risk prediction among patients with atrial fibrillation. Synthetic experiments showed that targeted fairness methods generally reduced intended subgroup disparities, while combined strategies produced larger average fairness improvements with modest utility tradeoffs. In the clinical prediction task, mitigation effects were highly variable, with some combinations improving both fairness and predictive performance while others were ineffective or counterproductive. These findings demonstrate that fairness interventions interact in nonadditive and context dependent ways. FairSelect provides a practical framework for systematically identifying fairness strategies that improve subgroup equity while preserving model performance in clinical machine learning.

</details>


### [141] [Eluna: An Agentic LLM System for Automating Warehouse Operations with Reasoning and Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08960)
*Ning Liu,Kalle Kujanpää,Zhaoxuan Zhu,P Aditya Sreekar,Kaiwen Liu,Chuanneng Sun,Jorge Marchena Menendez,Matthew Bales,Tianyu Yang,Shahnawaz Alam,Rose Yu,Baoyuan Liu,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Eluna 是一个生产级部署的图引导多智能体系统，通过将SOP编码为有向无环图、渐进式信息揭示、并行子智能体执行以及非对称情节式蒸馏（教师模型通过错误记忆改进，学生模型在精简后的轨迹上微调），实现了对复杂仓库SOP的高可靠执行，在13任务基准和生产应用中达到94%专家一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体缺乏强制遵循SOP的机制，且完整SOP规范引入的上下文过载会导致性能下降，而仓库操作要求在严格时间约束下可靠执行复杂的多系统决策逻辑。

Method: 1) 将SOP编码为有向无环图(DAG)并采用渐进式信息揭示；2) 图引导的多智能体框架，将独立任务委托给并行的子智能体，每个子智能体具有持久代码执行和实时数据访问能力；3) 非对称情节式蒸馏：强教师模型通过情节错误记忆进行改进，然后在去除记忆的精简轨迹上微调较小的学生模型，从而内化修正而不引入推理时开销。

Result: 微调后的模型在13任务基准和两个生产应用中匹配或超越教师模型，击败所有更大的现成基线模型，在工单处理应用中达到94%的专家一致率。

Conclusion: Eluna通过图引导的框架和情节式蒸馏方法，在不增加推理时开销的前提下，实现了对复杂SOP的可靠遵守和高效执行，验证了其在生产环境中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Warehouse operations are governed by Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs) that encode complex, multi-system decision logic, which must be executed reliably under strict time constraints, yet LLM agents lack mechanisms to enforce procedural compliance and degrade under the context overload full SOP specifications introduce. We present Eluna, a production-deployed agentic system for reliable SOP execution. Eluna is a graph-guided, multi-agent framework that encodes SOPs as directed acyclic graphs with progressive disclosure and delegates independent tasks to parallel sub-agents, each with persistent code execution and live data access. To meet production latency and accuracy needs, we use asymmetric episodic distillation where a strong teacher is improved through episodic error memories, then a smaller student is fine-tuned on the corrected trajectories with memory stripped, internalizing corrections without inference-time overhead. On a 13-task benchmark and two production applications, our fine-tuned models match or exceed their teacher, beat all larger off-the-shelf baselines, and reach 94% expert agreement on the ticket processing application.

</details>


### [142] [NL-PAC: Specification Ambiguity and Certified Minimax Risk Floors in LLM-Mediated Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08961)
*Berkay Anahtarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NL-PAC框架，证明当自然语言监督存在多义性时，额外标签无法解决识别问题，风险下界由点态可接受目标类的直径决定，且随机化极小极大风险可由数据无关策略实现。实验表明，特定提示可获得模型级认证，但释义和精确规则控制为零，桥接审计中候选阅读子句无法满足可接受性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当自然语言规范存在多义性时，标准监督信号（如人工标签）无法揭示具体采用哪种解读，导致识别问题（identification problem）。现有方法增加标签数量仅能减少抽样误差，但无法解决多义性带来的根本性不确定性问题。

Method: 提出NL-PAC框架：利用固定模型的阈值化解码律定义可接受标签和候选目标集；定义点态可接受目标类的直径作为多个标签可接受的概率；推导任意样本量下最坏情况风险的下界（至少为直径的一半），证明数据无关随机策略达到精确随机化极小极大风险；给出有限样本置信度边界，使这些量可通过留出未标注输入进行认证。

Result: 在冻结的Qwen 2.5-3B审计中：一个预设提示获得正向模型级认证；而释义和精确规则控制的结果为零。桥接审计中，提供的候选阅读子句无法满足将认证迁移至连贯解读所需的可接受性条件。

Conclusion: NL-PAC提供的认证仅针对被审计的模型、提示、阈值和输入分布；将其扩展到人类解读需要外部验证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly provide labels, evaluations, and feedback for tasks specified in natural language. When a specification admits multiple readings but the supervision channel does not reveal which is operative, additional labels reduce sampling error without resolving the resulting identification problem. We introduce Natural Language PAC (NL-PAC), a framework that uses a fixed model's thresholded decoding law to define admissible labels and candidate targets. The probability that multiple labels are admissible equals the diameter of the pointwise-admissible target class, and under target-blind supervision every learner incurs worst-case risk of at least half this diameter, at every sample size; the exact randomized minimax risk over this class is attained by a data-independent strategy. Finite-sample confidence bounds make these quantities certifiable from held-out unlabeled inputs. In a frozen Qwen~2.5--3B audit, one prespecified prompt yields a positive model-relative certificate, whereas a paraphrase and exact-rule controls yield zero. A held-out bridge audit finds that supplied candidate reading clauses fail the admissibility condition needed to transfer the certificate to coherent readings. The guarantee is specific to the audited model, prompt, threshold, and input distribution; extending it to human interpretations requires external validation.

</details>


### [143] [Sensitivity-Aware Thresholding and Token Routing for Activation Sparsification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08991)
*Bishmoy Paul,Youngmin Yi,Hoeseok Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种提升LLM推理效率的方法：SATS（基于敏感度的门控阈值校准方法）和基于token级别的条件路由框架，在保持模型质量的同时通过MLP激活稀疏化和动态路由优化计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中需要在减少计算量与保持模型质量之间取得平衡，现有基于百分位数的阈值校准方法忽略了不同层对激活变化的敏感度差异，且静态的激活修改方式对所有token统一处理不够灵活。

Method: 1) SATS：利用局部MLP输出敏感度代理来校准逐层门控阈值，替代基于激活百分位数的校准方法；2) Token路由框架：在逐token基础上动态选择基础路径或修改路径，而非对所有token统一应用修改后的计算。

Result: 在多个开源LLM上，SATS在匹配实际稀疏度的情况下优于基于阈值的稀疏化基线；Token路由在质量-吞吐量权衡上优于静态激活修改基线。

Conclusion: 改进的阈值校准和token路由能够有效提升LLM的质量-吞吐量权衡，为高效推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Efficient inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) requires deciding where computation can be reduced while preserving model quality. We study this problem through multilayer perceptron (MLP) activation sparsification and token-level conditional routing. We first propose Sensitivity-Aware Thresholding for Sparsity (SATS), a threshold calibration method to choose layerwise gate thresholds using a local MLP output sensitivity proxy rather than calibrating thresholds directly from activation percentiles. While SATS retains the existing mechanism of sparsifying MLP activations by thresholding gate activations, it replaces percentile-based calibration with a sensitivity-aware selection rule. We then introduce a lightweight token routing framework that dynamically selects between a base path and a modified path on a per-token basis, rather than applying the modified computation uniformly to all tokens. We evaluate both methods on multiple recent open-weight LLMs. Our results show that SATS improves over the threshold-based sparsification baseline at matched actual sparsity and that token routing yields a more favorable quality-throughput trade-off than static activation modification baselines. Overall, our results suggest that improved threshold calibration and token routing can improve the quality-throughput trade-off in LLMs.

</details>


### [144] [Stochastic Linear Bandits with Partially Observed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08971)
*Gautam Dasarathy,Vineet Gattani,Lalit Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机线性赌博机中动作部分可观测的问题，提出TOFU-POV算法，利用动作向量的低维结构实现√T的遗憾界，并配有下界分析与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统和医疗等实际场景中，完整获取动作特征成本高昂或不可行，仅能观察到随机子集坐标，导致传统线性赌博机方法失效。本文旨在克服部分观测带来的信息论下界，实现次线性遗憾。

Method: 提出TOFU-POV算法：利用带掩码的动作估计潜在动作子空间，使用epoch-wise冻结表示对当前动作进行补全，然后在低维坐标上运行OFUL。同时设计了无需已知内在维度的秩自适应版本。

Result: 理论上证明TOFU-POV达到√T遗憾界，依赖内在动作子空间维度而非环境维度，并量化了缺失率、决策集大小和子空间条件数的影响。构造了基于乘积结构的下界，分离了奖励学习不确定性与缺失相关的固有代价。实验验证了方法优于基线。

Conclusion: 部分观测的线性赌博机在低维结构假设下是可处理的，TOFU-POV有效克服了信息论障碍，为实际应用中昂贵特征获取问题提供了理论保证与实用算法。

Abstract: The stochastic linear bandit, where actions are represented as vectors and rewards are linear, is a central paradigm for sequential decision making. We study a partially observed variant of this problem in which the learning agent only sees a random subset of coordinates for each action. Such partial observability arises naturally in settings like recommendation and healthcare, where full action descriptions can be expensive or even impossible to obtain. In general, this makes sublinear regret information-theoretically impossible. However, we show that this barrier can be overcome when the action vectors have low intrinsic dimension. We propose an algorithm, TOFU-POV, that estimates the latent action subspace using the masked actions, imputes current actions using an epoch-wise frozen representation, and runs OFUL in the resulting low-dimensional coordinates. Our theory shows that TOFU-POV enjoys a $\sqrt{T}$ regret that scales with the intrinsic action subspace dimension as opposed to the ambient dimension and quantifies the interaction between these quantities and the missingness, decision set size, and subspace conditioning. We also devise a rank-adaptive algorithm that does not require the knowledge of the intrinsic dimension. We complement these guarantees with a lower bound based on a novel product construction that separates usual reward-learning uncertainty from a missingness-dependent cost intrinsic to partial observation. Synthetic and real data experiments support our theory and show that TOFU-POV can substantially improve upon natural baselines in this challenging problem.

</details>


### [145] [Federated Low-Rank Koopman Learning for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection in IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08978)
*Tung-Anh Nguyen,Van-Phuc Bui,Anh Tuyen Le,Kim Hue Ta,Minh Thuy Le,J. Andrew Zhang,Xiaojing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedKAD，一种面向分布式IoT多元时间序列的资源高效联邦Koopman异常检测框架。与传统联邦深度学习异常检测器不同，FedKAD通过轻量级滑动窗口Koopman表示学习正常时间动态，将联邦训练建模为低秩共识问题，仅在设备与服务器间交换紧凑子空间变量，并开发了联邦Stiefel-ADMM算法进行优化。实验表明，FedKAD在检测性能相当或更优的同时，训练速度提升高达2100倍，通信量降低80倍，推理延迟降低79倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式IoT系统产生多元时间序列流，异常检测对故障诊断、预测性维护和安全至关重要，但实际部署面临数据分散且非独立同分布、带宽有限、边缘设备计算和内存受限等挑战。现有基于深度学习的联邦异常检测方法需要训练和通信大型神经网络模型，不适合资源受限的IoT场景。

Method: 1) 使用轻量级滑动窗口Koopman表示学习正常时间动态，避免深度神经网络；2) 将联邦训练建模为低秩共识问题，原始传感器流和局部约化动态保留在设备端，仅交换紧凑子空间变量；3) 提出联邦Stiefel-ADMM算法，在正交性约束下优化共享表示，并提供部分客户端参与下的收敛性和驻点分析；4) 推理时通过测量观测未来轨迹与学习到的Koopman动态之间的预测残差进行局部异常检测。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的多元时间序列异常检测基准上，FedKAD相比联邦深度学习基线保持或提升了检测性能。在IoT部署方面，训练速度提升高达2100倍，通信量降低80倍，推理延迟降低79倍。

Conclusion: FedKAD是一种适用于资源受限边缘设备的联邦异常检测框架，通过Koopman算子理论有效解决了分布式IoT中数据异构、通信和计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Distributed IoT systems generate multivariate time-series streams for monitoring physical assets, servers, and embedded sensing platforms. Detecting abnormal temporal behavior is critical for fault diagnosis, predictive maintenance, and security. However, practical IoT anomaly detection is hindered by decentralized and non-IID data, limited bandwidth, and the constrained computation and memory of edge devices.
