To quantify generalisation performance, we introduce a new continuity bound for the InfoNCE loss, obtained via Gâteaux differentiation. The bound preserves the structure of averaging over negative samples present in the loss function and features an ``inverse temperature'' parameter which can be tuned to account for the algorithmic temperature. For embedding functions which are Lipschitz in a parameter, this yields a simple demonstration that the averaging effect of $k$ negative samples in the InfoNCE loss carries over to stabilisation of the generalisation error as $k$ grows.

</details>


### [183] [Action-Factored Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Scalable Quantum Device Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09422)
*Edwin De Nicolo,Rahul Marchand,Cornelius Carlsson,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Natalia Ares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QADAPT框架，利用在线学习的因子化动作空间表示解耦多智能体，实现量子点阵列的零样本泛化与快速校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同多智能体强化学习在参数空间大且具有局部可开发结构的问题（如静电定义量子点阵列调谐）中表现良好，但参数串扰导致的非平稳环境会破坏学习稳定性，这一问题同样困扰手动调谐过程。

Method: 提出QADAPT框架，采用在线学习的动作空间因子化表示来解耦智能体并最小化干扰，基于局部测量和奖励高效学习共享策略。

Result: 实现了对未见量子设备尺寸的零样本泛化，且收敛步数保持近似恒定，能够到达目标区间。

Conclusion: 该模块化策略为大规模量子处理器的快速校准提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning is well suited to problems with large parameter spaces and exploitable local structure, such as the tuning of electrostatically-defined quantum-dot arrays. However, if parameter cross-talk is strong, a non-stationary environment from the perspective of any individual agent can destabilize learning - the same effect that plagues manual tuning of such systems. We propose using a factored representation of the action space, learned online, to decouple agents and minimize their interference. Our framework, QADAPT, uses this factorization to efficiently learn shared policies based on local measurements and rewards. With this modular strategy, we achieve zero-shot generalization to unseen quantum device sizes and maintain an approximately constant number of convergence steps to reach target regimes. This work provides a scalable route toward the rapid calibration of large-scale quantum processors.

</details>


### [184] [Active rejection enables reliable generalization of universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09456)
*Mingxiang Luo,Xinnan Mao,Lu Wang,Lei Bai,Feng Ding,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师路由（ATR）方法，利用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练uMLIP教师，通过结构描述符、教师身份和教师间分歧评估每个结构-教师对的可靠性，选择高置信度预测生成伪标签，仅用0.2%的真实标签即可生成289万个可追溯的r²SCAN级伪标签，用于预训练轻量级CHGNet，在多个基准和分子动力学模拟中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIP）虽能桥接量子力学精度和大规模分子动力学，但高精度计算（如r²SCAN）成本高昂，导致训练数据集相对于开放材料空间仍然较小；同时，平均基准性能好并不能保证每个结构的能量-力预测都可靠。因此需要一种能高效构建高保真数据且保证结构级预测可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出自适应多教师路由（ATR）：(1) 使用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练uMLIP教师；(2) 结合结构描述符、教师身份和教师间分歧度，评估每个结构-教师对的可靠性；(3) 选择高置信度预测生成伪标签，拒绝无足够可靠教师的结构；(4) 将生成的289万可追溯伪标签用于预训练轻量级CHGNet。

Result: 仅用0.2%候选结构的真实r²SCAN标签，ATR生成了289万个可追溯的r²SCAN级伪标签。在留出r²SCAN结构和MP-r²SCAN基准上，ATR训练出的CHGNet一致优于基线和未路由的对照组。有限温度分子动力学显示，ATR在多个材料系统中提升了动力学鲁棒性，维持了稳定轨迹，而基线模拟发生灾难性结构崩塌。

Conclusion: 主动拒绝机制是将多个预训练uMLIP转化为可扩展且可靠的高保真数据构建系统的有效方法，为训练下一代高保真uMLIP提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) bridge quantum-mechanical accuracy and large-scale molecular dynamics, but the cost of high-accuracy calculations such as r$^2$SCAN limits training to datasets that remain small relative to the open materials space. Strong average benchmark performance also does not guarantee reliable energy--force predictions for every structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-Teacher Routing (ATR), which reformulates high-fidelity data construction as a structure-wise decision problem under uncertainty. Using a small set of real r$^2$SCAN labels, ATR calibrates multiple pretrained uMLIP teachers and combines structural descriptors, teacher identity, and inter-teacher disagreement to estimate the reliability of each structure--teacher pair. It selects high-confidence predictions for pseudo-label generation and rejects structures for which no teacher is sufficiently reliable. With real r$^2$SCAN labels for only 0.2\% of candidate structures, ATR distils 2.89 million traceable r$^2$SCAN-level pseudo-labels for pretraining. On held-out r$^2$SCAN structures and the MP-r$^2$SCAN benchmark, a lightweight CHGNet trained on the ATR-generated dataset consistently outperforms the baseline and non-routed controls. Finite-temperature molecular dynamics further shows that ATR improves dynamical robustness across multiple material systems, maintaining stable trajectories where baseline simulations undergo catastrophic structural collapse. These results establish active rejection as an effective mechanism for converting multiple pretrained uMLIPs into a scalable and reliable data-construction system for high-fidelity uMLIPs.

</details>


### [185] [All Explanations are Wrong, But Many Are Useful: Exploring the Rashomon Explanation Set with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09502)
*Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rashomon解释范式，通过构建一组忠实且指导预测的解释而非单一解释，证明解释与预测并非相互制约而是互补的；并设计了RashomonLLM智能体工作流，在客户流失分类、临床生存回归和工业点击率预测三个任务上显著优于现有方法，同时提升了准确率和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI（XAI）方法普遍存在一个假设：准确率与可解释性之间存在固有的权衡（accuracy-explainability trade-off）。本文认为该权衡并非本质性的，而是由于将解释和预测视为分离目标导致的伪问题。作者旨在证明当两者适当耦合时，解释不仅不会降低模型性能，反而能提升准确率。

Method: 1) 提出Rashomon解释范式，构建一组忠实且能指导预测的解释集合，而非传统单一解释；2) 理论上证明该集合非空，且解释的忠实度界定了其所指导模型的性能上界；3) 提出RashomonLLM工作流，采用"解释-预测-反思"（Explanation-Prediction-Reflection）智能体循环，通过迭代对齐解释与预测来生成自然语言解释，并证明其收敛且能恢复完整解释集合。

Result: 在客户流失分类、临床生存回归和工业级直播平台点击率预测三个任务上，RashomonLLM在准确率和解释质量两方面均显著优于最先进的预测和XAI基线方法；性能提升由解释忠实度驱动，且在分布偏移、时间分割和随机种子下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 本文所提框架打破了准确率与可解释性之间的伪权衡，在提升商业性能的同时为消费者信任奠定了基础，表明解释与预测可以互为增强而非互为代价。

Abstract: Explaining machine-learning models is increasingly important for decision-making and consumer trust, yet it is widely believed to come at a cost: existing Explainable AI (XAI) methods suffer from a persistent accuracy-explainability trade-off. We argue that this trade-off is not fundamental, but an artifact of treating explanation and prediction as separate objectives; when properly coupled, they become complementary, so that equipping a model to explain itself improves, rather than degrades, its accuracy. We introduce the Rashomon Explanation paradigm, which builds a set of faithful, prediction-guiding explanations rather than a single one, and prove that this set is generally non-empty and that explanation fidelity bounds the performance of the models it guides. To explore this set, we propose RashomonLLM, an Explanation-Prediction-Reflection agentic workflow that generates explanations in natural language by iteratively aligning them with predictions, and we prove it converges and recovers the full set. Across customer-churn classification, clinical survival regression, and industrial click-through prediction on large-scale live-streaming logs, RashomonLLM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prediction and XAI baselines on both accuracy and explanation quality, with gains driven by explanation fidelity and robust to distribution shifts, temporal splits, and seeds. Our framework thus advances business performance while laying the groundwork for consumer trust.

</details>


### [186] [TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09528)
*Refat Ishrak Hemel,Ehsan Hallaji,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TSAI-MetaFraud，一个面向元宇宙虚拟经济的多模态多任务基准数据集，整合行为、交易和图结构信息，支持交易欺诈检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链接预测和弱监督欺诈检测等任务，并提供了机器学习和图神经网络的基线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集通常孤立地关注用户行为、身份认证或金融交易中的一个方面，缺乏多模态融合的欺诈检测基准，限制了可复现的评估和多模态方法的发展。

Method: 构建了整合行为、交易和图结构信息的多模态数据集，包含欺诈和自动机器人场景，定义了四项基准任务，并采用机器学习和图神经网络模型进行基线评估。

Result: 提供了多模态多任务基准数据集TSAI-MetaFraud，并给出了各项任务上使用机器学习和图神经网络的基线评估结果。

Conclusion: 该数据集为元宇宙虚拟经济中的多模态学习、图挖掘、欺诈分析和可信AI研究提供了一个统一的基准平台。

Abstract: The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

</details>


### [187] [Statistically Undetectable Backdoors in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09532)
*Andrej Bogdanov,Alon Rosen,Neekon Vafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在深度前馈神经网络中，对抗性模型训练者可以植入在统计学上不可检测的后门（白盒设置下总变差距离极小），该后门为每个输入提供基于不变性的对抗样本，但无后门时在标准密码学假设下无法在多项式时间内生成此类样本，揭示了模型训练者与用户之间的根本性权力不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示模型训练者与模型用户在对抗样本生成能力上存在的根本性权力不对称，证明训练者可以植入统计上不可检测的后门，从而获得生成对抗样本的显著优势。

Method: 通过在后门植入的模型中引入不变性结构，使得每个输入都存在对应的对抗样本（映射到异常接近的输出）；利用总变差距离度量后门模型与诚实模型之间的统计不可区分性；基于标准密码学假设证明无后门时无法在多项式时间内生成此类对抗样本。

Result: 后门在白盒设置下统计不可检测（与诚实模型在总变差距离上接近）；后门为每个输入提供基于不变性的对抗样本；无后门时在标准密码学假设下证明不可能在多项式时间内生成此类对抗样本。

Conclusion: 模型训练者可以植入统计上不可检测的后门，从而获得对抗样本生成的能力优势，而模型用户在没有后门的情况下无法获得同等能力，这揭示了深度神经网络安全中训练者与用户之间根本性的权力不对称。

Abstract: We show how an adversarial model trainer can plant backdoors in a large class of deep, feedforward neural networks. These backdoors are statistically undetectable in the white-box setting, meaning that the backdoored and honestly trained models are close in total variation distance, even given the full descriptions of the models (e.g., all of the weights). The backdoor provides access to invariance-based adversarial examples for every input, mapping distant inputs to unusually close outputs. However, without the backdoor, it is provably impossible (under standard cryptographic assumptions) to generate any such adversarial examples in polynomial time. Our theoretical and preliminary empirical findings demonstrate a fundamental power asymmetry between model trainers and model users.

</details>


### [188] [GatedLinear: Adaptive Routing of Complementary Linear Bases for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09537)
*Qitai Tan,Ruiwen Gu,Yilin Su,Mo Li,Xu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GatedLinear 是一个轻量级时间序列预测框架，通过自适应路由三种互补的线性基（全局趋势-季节性基、差分增量基和相位对齐循环基）来处理多样化的时间动态，并引入三因子融合门控机制实现细粒度的软路由决策，在保持高精度的同时具有更小的参数量和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型通常采用单一计算主干（如自注意力或谱滤波）处理时间序列，难以同时捕捉平滑趋势延续、非平稳漂移和严格相位对齐循环等多样化且互斥的时间动态，需要一种能够自适应组合不同预测机制的轻量级框架。

Method: 提出 GatedLinear 框架，包含：(1) 三种互补线性基：全局趋势-季节性基、差分增量基和相位对齐循环基；(2) 三因子融合门控（Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate），将路由决策分解为通道特定偏好、水平感知偏移和基于已知未来时间标记的相位索引偏置，实现逐点软路由。

Result: 在标准基准测试上达到或超越现有复杂基础模型的 SOTA 精度，具有显著更小的参数量，并且路由模式具有明确的可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的线性基池与结构化门控机制的组合，能够在不同预测场景下自适应选择合适的时间动态建模方式，兼顾了预测精度、模型效率和可解释性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting requires models to capture diverse, often mutually exclusive, temporal dynamics, from smooth trend continuation to nonstationary drift and strict phase-aligned recurrence. While recent deep learning models have improved accuracy, they typically force these diverse patterns through a single computational backbone governed by fixed algorithmic inductive biases (e.g., self-attention or spectral filtering). This single-mechanism approach often struggles with the profound heterogeneity of real-world series, where different variables and forecast horizons necessitate fundamentally different predictive treatments. To address this, we propose GatedLinear: a lightweight framework that frames forecasting as the adaptive routing of complementary linear bases. GatedLinear leverages a pool of three specialized mechanisms: a global trend-seasonal basis for smooth projection, a difference-based incremental basis for nonstationary drift, and a phase-aligned recurrence basis for explicit cyclic reuse. To dynamically orchestrate these distinct behaviors, we introduce a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate that disentangles routing decisions into channel-specific preferences, horizon-aware offsets, and phase-indexed biases derived from known future time marks. This design allows the model to perform highly granular, point-wise soft routing across different predictive regimes without stacking computationally heavy neural modules. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy against recent complex foundational models, while offering explicitly interpretable routing patterns and operating with a substantially smaller parameter footprint.

</details>


### [189] [CoCoT-EEG: Contrastive-Pretrained Multiscale Convolutional Transformer for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09543)
*Gabriel Mahuas,Victoria Shevchenko,Ugo Tanielian,Yassir Bendou,Richard Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比预训练的多尺度时间卷积与Transformer编码器结合的EEG模型（CoCoT），在基准解码任务上匹配或超越现有重建预训练模型，且从头训练即可达到优异性能，展示了对比学习在EEG基础模型构建中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码重建预训练的EEG基础模型在噪声幅度高、信息集中在窄频带等有限维度的EEG数据上表现次优，因此需要探索更有效的预训练策略。

Method: 提出CoCoT模型，采用多尺度时间卷积输入层提取不同时间尺度的特征，结合Transformer编码器进行对比预训练，而非传统的掩码重建预训练。

Result: CoCoT在多个异构电极配置的基准解码任务上匹配或超越最先进的基于重建预训练的EEG模型；从头训练即超过以往单任务解码模型，甚至可以媲美预训练模型。

Conclusion: 对比学习在构建EEG基础模型方面具有可行性和数据效率优势，同时揭示了模型架构和预训练目标的设计考量，为替代性大规模预训练策略提供了方向。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretrained foundation models (FM) have shown early promise for non-invasive electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding applications. Many recent large-scale models converged on the approach of tokenizing raw EEG followed by masked reconstruction pretraining. However, this recipe has been shown to be suboptimal for data, like EEG, with high noise amplitude and information confined to limited dimensions such as narrow frequency bands. Building on this insight, we develop a novel contrastive-pretrained EEG model with multiscale temporal convolution input layers and Transformer encoder blocks (CoCoT). CoCoT matches or beats state-of-the-art reconstruction-pretrained EEG models on extensive benchmark decoding tasks with heterogeneous electrode configurations. Furthermore, CoCoT trained from scratch outperforms previous single-task decoding models and even rivals pretrained models, showcasing the architecture's flexibility and data efficiency. Through systematic ablations, including model architecture and pretraining objective, we demonstrate the viability of contrastive learning for building EEG FMs while suggesting key architectural design considerations, prompting further investigations in alternative large-scale pretraining strategies.

</details>


### [190] [Graph-Regularized Low-Rank Matrix Completion by Variable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09546)
*Benoît Loucheur,P. -A. Absil,Michel Journée*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GR-RTRMC方法，在图正则化框架下结合黎曼优化进行低秩矩阵补全，通过利用矩阵行/列间的内在关系提升准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTRMC方法虽利用低秩约束的几何结构进行优化，但未充分利用矩阵行与列之间的关联信息，导致在数据呈现强相关性时补全精度和鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将图正则化引入RTRMC框架，在Grassmann流形上将原问题重构成无约束优化问题，利用行与列的关系图约束来引导矩阵补全过程。

Result: 在图正则化的帮助下，GR-RTRMC在矩阵补全的准确性和鲁棒性上优于原始RTRMC，尤其适用于行或列之间存在强相关性的数据场景。

Conclusion: 图正则化可有效增强基于黎曼优化的矩阵补全方法，使其更好地利用数据内在结构，提升在复杂相关性场景下的补全性能。

Abstract: We address the low-rank matrix completion problem by incorporating graph regularization into the existing Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion (RTRMC) framework. The latter uses the geometry of the low-rank constraint to remodel the problem as an unconstrained optimization problem on a single Grassmann manifold. Our approach, named Graph-Regularized RTRMC (GR-RTRMC), exploits the inherent relationships between rows and columns of the matrix. By using these relationships, we aim to improve the accuracy and robustness of matrix completion, particularly in scenarios where the underlying data exhibits strong correlations between rows or columns.

</details>


### [191] [Semantic Pareto-DQN: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Financial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09641)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义帕累托-DQN，一种多目标强化学习框架，通过大语言模型将异构交易特征编码为自然语言叙事，构建鲁棒的状态表示，并利用向量奖励优化帕累托前沿，在不进行数据重采样的情况下解决金融异常检测中的极端类别不平衡和“欺诈崩溃”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融异常检测面临极端类别不平衡，传统单目标算法容易出现“欺诈崩溃”（即默认预测为多数类），无法平衡异常拦截与客户摩擦之间的矛盾，且现有数据重采样方法会引入分布失真。

Method: 1) 将异构交易特征通过大语言模型合成为连贯的自然语言叙事，生成尺度不变的状态表示；2) 构建多目标强化学习（Pareto-DQN），优化向量化奖励函数，显式解耦金融效能、操作摩擦和语义发现三个目标；3) 通过连续帕累托前沿映射，动态权衡漏检异常与误报之间的非对称成本。

Result: 在电子商务欺诈和UCI信用数据集上，语义帕累托-DQN成功打破了零召回陷阱，相比标量化基线方法取得了更优的少数类召回率，实现了以可控的操作摩擦换取金融异常发现能力的提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为无需数据重采样的金融异常检测提供了一种有效替代方案，通过多目标优化在异常拦截与客户摩擦之间实现了更灵活的帕累托最优权衡。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection suffers from extreme class imbalance, causing traditional single-objective algorithms to exhibit ``fraud collapse'', defaulting to the majority class and failing to balance anomaly interdiction with customer friction. To overcome this without distortive data resampling, we propose the Semantic Pareto-DQN, a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous transaction features into cohesive natural-language narratives, encoded by large language models, thereby producing a robust, scale-invariant state representation. The agent optimizes a vectorial reward that explicitly decouples financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. By mapping the continuous Pareto frontier, the system dynamically navigates the asymmetric costs of missed anomalies versus false positives. Empirical evaluations across E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets show that semantic Pareto-DQN successfully shatters the zero-recall trap. It achieves superior minority-class recall compared to scalarized baselines, providing an alternative to trade bounded operational friction for financial anomaly discovery.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [MemeBuddy: Dialog-Style Audio Representations for Engaging Non-Visual Meme Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08912)
*Chirag Bhansali,Vikas Ashok,Hae-Na Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MemeBuddy 是一个将图像梗图转化为多轮对话式音频的系统，利用多模态大模型识别模板和文化参考，以角色对话形式呈现梗图内容。对14名盲人用户的实验表明，对话式表示相比传统描述字幕在参与度和满意度上更优，同时保持可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有为盲人用户提供的梗图无障碍方案主要依赖自动生成的描述性字幕，虽然改善了一定可理解性，有时也融入韵律或情感线索，但未能捕捉梗图中的幽默、叙事结构和语境细微差异，导致体验缺乏吸引力。

Method: MemeBuddy 将梗图建模为对话，利用角色化发言人生成结构化的多轮音频表示。系统通过多模态大语言模型隐式推断上下文知识（如识别常见梗图模板和文化参考），将梗图重新诠释为两个发言人之间的对话，融合提取的梗图文本来传达意图、时机和隐含意义。

Result: 对14名盲人参与者的用户研究表明，对话式梗图表示在参与度和用户满意度方面 consistently 优于传统的字幕式描述，同时保持了相当的可理解性水平。

Conclusion: 对话式多轮音频表示能有效提升盲人用户对梗图的可访问性和体验质量，为无障碍信息呈现提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Image memes are a pervasive form of online communication, widely used to convey humor, opinions, and cultural references. Prior work has explored making memes accessible to blind users, primarily through auto-generated descriptive captions. While these approaches improve comprehensibility and sometimes incorporate prosodic or emotional cues, they often fail to capture the humor, narrative structure, and contextual nuances that make memes engaging. We present MemeBuddy, a system that models memes as dialog, generating structured, multi-turn audio representations using role-based speakers. MemeBuddy reinterprets a meme as a conversation between two speakers, integrating extracted meme text with contextual knowledge implicitly inferred by a multimodal LLM (e.g., recognition of common meme templates and cultural references) to convey intent, timing, and implicit meaning through conversational interaction. We evaluate MemeBuddy in a user study with 14 blind participants. Results show that dialog-style meme representations consistently improve engagement and user satisfaction compared to caption-style descriptions, while maintaining comparable comprehension.

</details>


### [193] [Micro-level AI Feedback Features and Student Responses in Consecutive LLM Tutoring Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08952)
*Shayla Sharmin,Mohammad Fahim Abrar,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于16,851条用户-AI对话数据，分析发现AI反馈中的具体阐述（如类比、示例）能显著提升学生后续交互中的理解并降低再次困惑，而共情语言无显著效果，较长回复反而与理解下降相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从会话或任务层面分析AI反馈的微观特征与学习结果的关系，缺乏对连续交互中反馈如何影响学生即时认知状态（困惑与理解）的细粒度考察。

Method: 使用StudyChat数据集中16,851条学生与LLM助教的自然对话记录，筛选出1,718个学生表达困惑后进入下一轮交互的案例，采用卡方检验和广义估计方程（GEE）分析具体阐述、情感语言和回复长度三个微观特征与后续学生理解/再困惑的关联。

Result: 具体阐述（类比、对比解释、示例）与学生后续交互中的更高理解水平和更低再困惑率显著相关；共情语言无显著关联；较长回复独立地与更低理解水平相关。

Conclusion: 研究强调了跨连续用户-AI交互分析反馈价值的必要性，并指出具体阐述在促进即时学生理解方面可能发挥重要作用。

Abstract: AI-assisted feedback research has shown that micro-level feedback features, such as concrete elaboration, affective language, and response length, are associated with learning outcomes. Existing studies have primarily examined these features using session- or task-level measures. We examine how feedback provided in one user-AI interaction is associated with student confusion and understanding in the immediately following interaction in a naturalistic tutoring setting. We focus on three micro-level features of AI feedback: concrete elaboration (analogies, comparison-based explanations, or worked examples), affective language (encouragement, empathy, or apology), and response length. We analyzed 16,851 conversational user-AI interactions from the StudyChat dataset, a naturalistic record of student interactions with an LLM tutor in an undergraduate AI course, and identified 1,718 cases in which students expressed confusion and continued to a subsequent interaction. Using chi-square tests and Generalized Estimating Equations (GEE), we found that concrete elaboration was associated with higher understanding and lower re-confusion in the student's next interaction. Empathetic language showed no significant association with either outcome, while longer responses were independently associated with lower understanding. These findings highlight the value of examining feedback across consecutive user-AI interactions and suggest that concrete elaboration may play an important role in supporting immediate student understanding.

</details>


### [194] [Living Inside the Black Box: Behavioral Probing and Adaptation in Mandatory Wearable Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09009)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Ruiqi Chen,Xiaolan Ding,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对中国社区矫正系统中24名强制电子监控佩戴者的定性研究，发现他们在不确定性、行为试探和适应过程中发展出一种"受限下的传感器素养"——一种实用的风险导向知识，揭示了佩戴者在系统行为可映射时获得有限灵活性，在不确定性代价高昂时自我收缩行为，甚至产生脱机后的计算习惯残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感系统在高风险制度环境中将行为数据转化为 consequential 的判断，但佩戴者对这些判断如何做出几乎没有了解，现有研究缺乏从佩戴者视角理解这一过程的工作。

Method: 对24名中国社区矫正系统中经历强制电子监控的个体进行定性研究（访谈），分析他们如何理解和应对传感器系统的运作。

Result: 参与者发展出"受限下的传感器素养"；在规则域中呈现两种取向：映射系统行为时获得有限灵活性，不确定性高昂时收缩行为；部分前佩戴者在设备移除后仍残留计算习惯。

Conclusion: 提出使机构化传感对佩戴者可理解的设计启示，包括传感器不确定性、可用文档和佩戴后评估。

Abstract: Wearable sensing systems in high-stakes institutional contexts translate behavioral data into consequential judgments, yet wearers have little access to how those judgments are made. We present a qualitative study of 24 individuals who experienced mandatory electronic monitoring in China's community corrections system. We show that participants built what we term sensor literacy under constraint, a practical form of risk-oriented knowledge developed through uncertainty, behavioral probing, and adaptation. We identify two orientations across rule domains. Where participants had mapped system behavior, they sometimes regained limited flexibility. Where uncertainty remained costly, they contracted movement and discretionary activity beyond formal rules. Some former wearers described residual habits of calculation after device removal. We discuss design implications for making institutional sensing intelligible to wearers, including sensor uncertainty, usable documentation, and evaluation after device wearing.

</details>


### [195] [Central Tendency Bias in Human Selection of AI-Generated Design Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09018)
*Huiyang Chen,Keqing Jiao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文研究了图像生成AI中多选项展示界面如何导致用户产生中心倾向偏差（倾向于选择设计集中心附近的选项），并通过实验证明高方差设计集会增加中心选项的选择概率，揭示了AI生成多样性与人类选择多样性之间的潜在矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究图像生成AI在呈现多个设计变体供用户选择时，多选项并排的界面设计是否系统性地影响人类决策，特别是是否诱发中心倾向偏差，从而制约选择的多样性。

Method: 基于集合感知理论，通过控制实验操纵设计集的方差（高vs低），测量参与者在审美偏好和代表性任务中的选择行为。

Result: 高方差设计集显著增加了用户选择中心附近选项的概率，且该效应在审美偏好和代表性两种任务中均稳定存在。

Conclusion: 多变异界面可能限制用户的选择多样性，AI生成输出的多样性与人类最终选择结果的多样性之间存在潜在张力。

Abstract: Image-generation AI systems increasingly support creative work by producing multiple design variations for users to evaluate and select. In such human-AI co-creation workflows, selection becomes a critical stage where human judgment guides AI-generated possibilities toward final outcomes. While presenting multiple alternatives is intended to encourage exploration, the simultaneous multi-option presentation may introduce systematic biases in human decision making. Drawing on ensemble perception theory, we investigate whether these interfaces induce central tendency bias-the tendency to favor options closer to the center of a design set. We conducted a controlled experiment manipulating the variance of design sets (high vs. low) and measured participants' selections in both aesthetic preference and representativeness tasks. Results show that higher variance increases the selection of center-proximal designs across both tasks. These findings suggest that multi-variation interfaces in image-generation AI systems may constrain selection diversity, revealing a potential tension between diversity in generated outputs and diversity in human selection outcomes.

</details>


### [196] [Privacy Detective: A Narrative Game that Cultivates Student Developers' Privacy Awareness by Harnessing Legal Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09022)
*Shao-Yu Chu,Jennifer Forsyth,Xu Wang,Haojian Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款名为Privacy Detective的叙事调查游戏，利用真实法律文件训练开发者的隐私意识。实验表明，相比直接阅读FTC法律文件，玩游戏能帮助开发者识别更多真实隐私违规、更少误报，并提供更完整的违规理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者往往在不知情的情况下做出有问题的隐私设计决策，需要一种有效的方法来训练其隐私意识。

Method: 设计了一款叙事调查游戏Privacy Detective，玩家从真实法律文档中寻找隐私违规证据，并使用模板组织成违规报告。采用组间实验设计，将游戏条件与直接阅读FTC法律文件的基线条件进行对比。

Result: 游戏组识别出更多真实违规、更少误报非问题项，并且为其报告的违规提供了更完整的理由。

Conclusion: 基于法律文件的叙事调查游戏能够有效提升开发者的隐私违规识别能力，优于传统阅读法律文件的方法。

Abstract: Developers' choices about what data a system collects, how it is used and shared, and what defaults govern user choices directly shape users' privacy experiences. Yet, developers often make problematic privacy-related design decisions without realizing the potential consequences. We introduce Privacy Detective, a narrative investigation game that leverages real-world legal documents to train developers' privacy awareness. In the game, players search for privacy violation evidence derived from legal documents and organize this evidence into privacy violation reports using curated templates. We evaluated Privacy Detective in a between-subjects study with student developers, comparing it against a baseline in which participants read raw FTC legal documents. Participants in the game condition identified more true violations than the baseline group, flagged fewer non-issues, and provided more complete justifications for the violations they reported.

</details>


### [197] [Configurable AI Coding Assistants: Designing For Developers Who Like to Be in Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09215)
*Ekaterina Koshchenko,Jovana Stankovic,Ilya Zakharov,Agnia Sergeyuk*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究调查了专业开发者对AI编码助手的配置需求，通过综合产品文档和前期研究整理出33个配置选项，并对56名专业开发者进行问卷调查和7场设计研讨会。结果显示开发者对可配置性有强烈兴趣（72.6%的评分积极），但仅有约三分之一的配置选项为参与者所知。任务相关控制（如置信度阈值、建议质量可见性、响应长度）需求最高，而个性相关配置被认为不必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI编码助手虽然被广泛使用，但只提供有限的配置方式来让开发者控制助手行为，研究者希望了解经验丰富的开发者需要哪些配置、如何优先排序不同配置需求以及他们偏好的界面机制。

Method: 首先综合产品文档和关于信任与个性化的前期研究，整理出33个配置选项并分为四类（代码建议、系统与策略、人机交互、用户及其个人背景）。随后对56名专业开发者进行问卷调查，并组织7场设计研讨会，让参与者安排配置到理想的控制面板并深入讨论需求和体验。

Result: 72.6%的有用性评分为积极，但仅约三分之一的配置选项为参与者所知；任务相关控制（置信度阈值、建议质量可见性、响应长度）需求最高，而个性相关配置被认为不必要。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了为未来编码助手设计更统一和可发现的配置界面的启示。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are now widely used in professional development, yet they offer only limited ways for developers to control how they behave. In this paper, we investigate what kinds of configurations experienced developers want in coding assistants, how they prioritize different types of configuration needs, and which interface mechanisms they prefer. We first synthesize product documentation and prior research on trust and personalization to compile a list of 33 configuration options, grouped into four categories: Code suggestions, System & policies, Human-assistant interaction, and Users & their personal context. We then conduct a survey with 56 professional developers and 7 design sessions in which participants arrange configurations into their perfect control board and talk about their needs and experiences in more depth.
  Developers report strong interest in configurability: 72.6% of usefulness ratings are positive, while only around a third indicate that the corresponding configuration is known to participants in their tools. Demand is particularly high for task-related controls such as minimum confidence thresholds, visibility of suggestion quality, and response length, whereas many persona-related configurations are seen as unnecessary. In this paper, we discuss the implications for designing more unified and discoverable configuration surfaces for future coding assistants

</details>


### [198] [LLMs for health: Perceived benefits, risks, intention to use AI chatbots, and willingness to self-disclose across sensitive health topics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09253)
*Gwenn Beets,Anniek Jansen,Saar Hommes,Ruben D. Vromans,Leonie Westerbeek,Supraja Sankaran,Julia C. M. van Weert,Emiel J. Krahmer,Nadine Bol*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过在线实验（荷兰样本，N=1,388）考察了话题类型（身体vs心理）和话题敏感性（低vs高）以及个人特征对AI健康聊天机器人的感知收益、风险、使用意愿及健康信息披露意愿的影响。结果发现，感知收益正向预测使用意愿和信息披露意愿，感知风险则负向预测；低敏感性话题的使用意愿更高；感知、意愿和披露意愿主要受感知收益、风险和个人特征影响，而非话题类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI聊天机器人在健康咨询场景中，话题类型（身体/心理）和话题敏感性（低/高）以及个人特征如何影响用户的感知收益、感知风险、使用意愿和健康信息披露意愿。

Method: 采用2（话题类型：身体vs心理，组间）×2（话题敏感性：低vs高，组内）混合设计的在线实验，在荷兰代表性样本（N=1,388）中实施。

Result: 1）感知收益正向影响使用意愿和披露意愿，感知风险负向影响；2）低敏感性话题的使用意愿高于高敏感性话题；3）感知、意愿和披露意愿随个人特征差异而变化；4）使用意愿和披露意愿主要关联于感知收益、感知风险和个人特征，而非话题类型。

Conclusion: 用户对AI健康聊天机器人的使用意愿和健康信息披露意愿主要由感知收益、感知风险和个人特征决定，而非话题类型本身。

Abstract: AI chatbots are increasingly used for answering health-related questions. This study examines the role of topic type discussed with an AI chatbot and individual characteristics on perceived benefits and risks, intention to use an AI chatbot, and willingness to self-disclose health information. We conducted an online experiment with a 2 (topic type: physical versus psychological, between-subjects) x 2 (topic sensitivity: low versus high, within-subjects) mixed design among a Dutch representative sample (N = 1,388). Results showed that perceived benefits were positively associated with intention and willingness to self-disclose, while perceived risks were negatively associated. Moreover, participants reported higher usage intentions for low-sensitive topics compared to high-sensitive topics. Furthermore, perceptions, intention, and willingness to self-disclose varied by individual characteristics. Overall, our findings suggest that intentions to use AI chatbots and self-disclosure of health-related information are primarily related to perceived benefits and risks and to personal characteristics rather than to topic type.

</details>


### [199] [Learning When to Intervene on Habitual Behaviors: A Case Study in Oral Health Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09518)
*Bhanu Teja Gullapalli,Vivek Shetty,Anna L. Trella,Asim H. Gazi,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应干预时机决策框架，通过在线学习持续调整数字健康干预的推送时间，以解决固定干预时机与用户日常习惯逐渐错位的问题。在口腔健康干预案例中，自适应策略相比固定策略显著提高了干预与刷牙行为的覆盖匹配度，并已在正在进行的随机对照试验中得到初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字健康干预中，固定干预时机随用户习惯演变会逐渐与行为错位，导致干预过早或过晚，影响效果。现有方法未能动态适配个体行为模式的变化，亟需一种可在线自适应调整干预时间的决策框架。

Method: 提出一个在线决策框架，将干预时机视为动态可调参数，通过持续学习用户行为模式的变化来更新干预时间，并与"是否推送干预"的决策过程联合优化。使用覆盖率指标评估干预与后续行为的接近程度。

Result: 在已部署的口腔健康干预试验数据及模拟场景中，自适应干预时机的覆盖率始终优于基于用户初始录入的固定干预时间。正在进行的随机对照试验的初步结果与前期评估一致，进一步支持了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应干预时机框架能有效解决习惯性行为的时间错位问题，提升数字健康干预的时效性和有效性，具有在动态行为环境中持续优化的潜力。

Abstract: A central challenge for digital health interventions aimed at improving habitual behaviors is deciding when to deliver an intervention prompt. For many daily habits, such as tooth brushing or eating, individuals tend to act around a usual time of day, but this timing is not fixed and can shift as routines evolve. When intervention timing is selected in advance and held constant throughout a study, it can gradually become misaligned with behavior, causing interventions to potentially arrive after the behavior has already occurred or too early to be effective. In this work, we address this habitual timing misalignment in digital health interventions by proposing an online decision-making framework that continuously adapts intervention timing as individual behavior patterns change. Rather than treating intervention timing as a static design choice, our framework adapts it over time and integrates it into a sequential process that determines both when and whether to deliver an intervention. Using data from a deployed oral health intervention trial as a case study, we evaluate our approach using both observed data and simulated settings to assess how well different intervention timing strategies align with the timing of brushing events. Across these evaluations, we measure performance using a coverage-based metric that captures whether an intervention is delivered sufficiently close to a subsequent brushing event. We find that adaptive intervention timing consistently improves coverage compared to fixed intervention times based on user-provided input. The proposed framework is currently deployed in an ongoing randomized controlled trial of a digital oral health intervention, with preliminary results that are consistent with and further support our prior evaluations.

</details>


### [200] [KnitID: Machine-Knitted RFID Antennas for Battery-Free Authentication, Localization and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09584)
*Weiye Xu,Yue Xu,Devin Murphy,Sen Zhang,Te-yen Wu,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: KnitID是一种通过机器编织技术将磁线集成到线圈结构中制成的紧凑型RFID纺织天线，尺寸仅为60mm×8mm，比传统环形天线缩小约90%，同时比类似尺寸的标准偶极子设计在人体上感应范围提升30%。该系统支持无电池的身份认证、空间定位和交互检测，并通过交互式袖套原型验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无电池RFID系统在可穿戴交互中的应用受限于天线尺寸过大或感应范围不足，难以在人体上紧凑嵌入多个标签以实现丰富的交互功能。

Method: 采用机器编织技术将磁线集成到独特的圈套圈（loop-over-loop）结构中，设计出紧凑型纺织环形天线（60mm×8mm），并构建嵌入多个KnitID标签的交互式袖套原型。

Result: 与传统环形天线相比尺寸缩小约90%；与类似尺寸的偶极子设计相比，人体上感应范围提升30%；成功实现身份认证、空间定位和交互检测功能。

Conclusion: KnitID证明了紧凑型编织RFID天线在无电池可穿戴交互中的可行性，为在针织用户界面上实现多样化交互提供了新途径。

Abstract: Battery-free RFID systems offer a scalable and maintenance-free approach to interaction. We present KnitID, a machine-knitted textile RFID antenna design that enables on-body authentication, localization, and interaction. Unlike prior antenna designs, KnitID achieves a compact antenna form factor (60mm by 8mm) by integrating magnet wire into the unique loop-over-loop structure of machine knitting. This structure reduces the size of conventional loop antennas by around 90\%, while also providing 30\% longer sensing ranges than standard dipole designs with similar size on the human body. The compact form factor creates new opportunities to embed multiple RFID tags across the human body, enriching backscatter signals and supporting a broader range of battery-free on-body interactions. To demonstrate this capability, we build an interactive sleeve to support wearer authentication, spatial localization, and interaction detection. Through technical evaluations, we show the feasibility of KnitID to provide diverse and battery-free interactions on knitted user interfaces.

</details>


### [201] [Indirect and Direct AI Scaffolding for Computational Problem Posing: A Pilot Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09628)
*Shayla Sharmin,Mohammad Fahim Abrar,Mohammad Al-Ratrout,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文设计了两种基于LLM的支架系统（间接引导式提问 vs 直接示范式示例）支持计算教育中的问题提出活动，通过20名研究生的被试内实验发现：直接支架带来更快的即时改进，而间接支架促进更深层次的反思，建议先使用间接支架再过渡到直接支架的教学顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题提出是计算教育中促进主动建构与反思的重要学习活动，但缺乏有效的技术支撑工具，特别是如何利用LLM设计不同模态的支架系统来支持这一过程尚待探索。

Method: 设计两种基于LLM的支架系统（基于布鲁姆分类学评估学生生成问题），差异仅在于输出模态：间接支架提供引导性问题，直接支架提供工作示例。采用被试内、平衡顺序设计的实验，20名研究生参与，收集问题质量评分、用户体验问卷和访谈数据。

Result: 两种支架系统以互补方式支持问题改进：直接支架产生更显著的即时改进效果，间接支架则被参与者认为促进了更深层次的对自身问题设计的反思。

Conclusion: 建议在实际教学中先使用间接支架促进反思，当学习者陷入困境时再切换到直接支架，为将LLM支架整合到计算教育课堂提供了初步的实践策略。

Abstract: Problem posing is a valuable learning activity in computing education, encouraging learners to actively construct, refine, and reflect on problems rather than simply solving them. This experience report presents the design and pilot deployment of two LLM-powered scaffolding systems for supporting problem posing across two computational scenarios with different levels of task openness. Both systems assessed student-generated problems using Bloom's Taxonomy-based criteria and applied the same assessment framework, differing only in output modality: one provided guiding questions (Indirect scaffolding), while the other offered worked examples (Direct scaffolding). We conducted a within-subjects, counterbalanced pilot study with 20 graduate students and collected problem-quality ratings, user-experience surveys, and post-session interviews. Our deployment showed that both systems supported problem refinement in complementary ways, each offering distinct benefits. Direct scaffolding produced greater immediate improvements, while interviews showed that participants valued Indirect scaffolding for promoting deeper reflection on their own problem design. Based on these findings, we suggest sequencing the two modalities by beginning with Indirect scaffolding to promote reflection, then shifting to Direct scaffolding when learners become stuck. These lessons offer an initial practical strategy for integrating LLM-based scaffolding into computing classrooms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Interval Certifications for Multilayered Perceptrons via Lattice Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08773)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个严格的AI安全理论框架，将对抗鲁棒性问题归约为格遍历问题，通过音证（sound certification）和完备证（complete certification）两个互补概念，结合格遍历算子与精炼-验证迭代方案，实现了形式化MLP验证器的最大音证性与最小完备性保证。同时揭示了优化问题中的不对称性：完备认证可在多项式时间内求解，而音证认证则具有强难解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒性是AI安全的基础性问题，现有研究主要关注音证认证（即已知的对抗鲁棒性），而完备认证尚未被系统研究。作者旨在建立统一的理论框架，将两类认证问题纳入格遍历的数学结构中，并揭示其计算复杂性的本质差异。

Method: 提出格遍历算子，构建"精炼与验证"（refine & verify）迭代方案；利用形式化MLP验证器保证音证的最大性（sound maximality）和完备的最小性（complete minimality）；对对称区间（ℓ∞球）设计对数时间复杂度算法。

Result: 完备认证的最小解可在多项式预言机调用次数内求得；音证认证被证明具有强难解性；对称区间上实现了对数级别算法；通过ParallelepipedoNN系统进行了经验评估。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了对抗鲁棒性与格遍历问题之间的等价关系，系统区分了音证与完备两类认证的计算复杂度，为AI安全性提供了新的理论工具和算法框架。

Abstract: In this work we present a rigorous theoretical framework to a foundational problem of AI safety, namely adversarial robustness. In particular, we show that the adversarial robustness problem can be reduced to a lattice traversal problem. Each element of this lattice corresponds to an interval, i.e., an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle, containing an input point $\mathbf{x}$. Consider a multilayered perceptron classifier (MLP). An interval $I$ constitutes a sound certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and $\mathbf{x}$ can be freely perturbed in $I$ without changing the MLP's prediction. Complementarily, an interval $I$ constitutes a complete certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and when $\mathbf{x}$ moves outside of $I$ the MLP's prediction is guaranteed to change. While the sound certification problem corresponds to the well-studied adversarial robustness, complete certifications have not been examined in the literature. We develop lattice traversal operators, which we apply in a refine & verify iterative scheme. Using formal MLP verifiers, sound maximality and complete minimality are guaranteed. Moreover, we examine objective optimization problems. There we discover some interesting asymmetries. For complete certifications, the minimum solution is obtained in polynomial oracle calls. This does not hold for sound certifications, where we prove strong intractability results. Additionally, we examine optimization problems in symmetric intervals (i.e., $\ell_\infty$-spheres), where we provide logarithmic algorithms. Finally, we present an empirical evaluation, using the novel ParallelepipedoNN system.

</details>


### [203] [CogniConsole: Externalizing Inference-Time Control as a Formal Abstraction for Reliable LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08774)
*Vanessa Figueiredo,Wilter Franceschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出推理时控制（inference-time control）是影响LLM系统可靠性的关键因素，并通过CogniConsole架构和489个可控性探针实验证明，增加结构脚手架可系统性地降低输出方差和失败率，许多失败模式源于控制不足而非能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将LLM系统可靠性仅归因于模型能力的传统观点，论证推理时控制（任务框架和上下文选择的计算层）同样显著影响可靠性。

Method: 提出CogniConsole架构，将推理时控制外化为结合程序化协调与有限提示推理的结构化接口；通过489个可控性探针在多步交互环境中进行实验，比较从非结构化到完全结构化的不同脚手架水平对输出方差和失败率的影响。

Result: 在固定模型架构下，增加结构脚手架系统性降低了输出方差和失败率；许多失败模式（如上下文漂移、不一致的约束遵循）源于控制不足而非能力不足。

Conclusion: 推理时控制应被视为一等抽象，为超越单纯规模扩展的LLM系统设计与评估提供了新的方向和经验基础。

Abstract: Reliability in large language model (LLM) systems is typically framed as a function of model capability. We challenge this by demonstrating that reliability is significantly influenced by \emph{inference-time control} -- the computational layer governing task framing and context selection. We introduce \emph{CogniConsole}, an architectural instantiation that externalizes this control into a structured interface combining programmatic coordination with bounded prompt-based reasoning. Through \emph{controllability-oriented probes} ($N=489$) in a multi-step interactive environment, we show that increasing structural scaffolding -- from unstructured to fully scaffolded -- \textbf{systematically reduces output variance and failure rates under a fixed model architecture}. Our results indicate that many observed failure modes, such as context drift and inconsistent constraint adherence, arise from under-specified control rather than insufficient capability. This work provides an empirical basis for treating inference-time control as a first-class abstraction, opening new directions for designing and evaluating LLM systems beyond scaling alone.

</details>


### [204] [GATS: Graph-Augmented Tree Search with Layered World Models for Efficient Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08894)
*Maureese Williams,Dymitr Nowicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GATS（图增强树搜索）是一个通过UCB1树搜索+三层世界模型实现零LLM调用的规划框架，在合成任务上100%成功率（LATS 92%，ReAct 64%），12个挑战场景中保持100%成功率，而LATS降至88.9%，ReAct降至23.9%，且零方差、零LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent规划方法（LATS、ReAct）在规划过程中过度依赖LLM推理，导致计算成本高、结果随机不稳定，亟需一种无需LLM推理即可高效规划的方案。

Method: 提出GATS框架，核心是结合基于UCB1的系统性树搜索与三层世界模型（L1精确符号动作匹配、L2执行日志统计学习、L3未知动作的LLM预测），在规划阶段完全消除LLM调用。

Result: 合成任务中GATS成功率100%（LATS 92%，ReAct 64%）；12个挑战场景中GATS 100%（LATS 88.9%，ReAct 23.9%）；GATS规划阶段零LLM调用（LATS每任务37次），且输出确定性计划零方差。

Conclusion: 系统性搜索结合学习到的世界模型在agent规划任务上能够大幅超越依赖LLM引导的探索方法，实现更优的性能、更低的成本和完全的确定性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise in multi-step planning tasks, but existing approaches like LATS (Language Agent Tree Search) and ReAct rely heavily on LLM inference during planning, leading to high computational costs and stochastic behavior. We present \textbf{GATS} (Graph-Augmented Tree Search), a planning framework that combines systematic UCB1-based tree search with a layered world model to eliminate LLM calls during inference while achieving superior planning performance. Our three-layer world model integrates: (L1) exact symbolic action matching, (L2) statistics learned from execution logs, and (L3) LLM-based prediction for unknown actions. On synthetic planning tasks with branching paths and dead-ends, GATS achieves \textbf{100\% success rate} compared to 92 % for LATS and 64\% for ReAct. On a comprehensive stress test spanning 12 challenging scenarios -- including coding workflows, web navigation, and long-horizon tasks -- GATS maintains \textbf{100\% success} while LATS drops to 88.9 % and ReAct to 23.9%. GATS requires \textbf{zero LLM calls per task} during planning (vs. 37 per task for LATS) and produces deterministic plans with zero variance across runs. Our results demonstrate that systematic search with learned world models can substantially outperform LLM-guided exploration for agent planning.

</details>


### [205] [Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964)
*Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Ruhan Wang,Junyao Yang,Zhichao Liu,Xiyang Wu,Anhao Li,Yue Yu,Ninghao Liu,Lichao Sun,Haotao Mi,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench（LHTB），一个包含46个长时域任务的终端基准测试，覆盖实验复现、软件工程、多模态分析、交互游戏和科学计算等9个类别。每个任务被分解为细粒度子任务以提供密集的中期奖励和部分评分，任务通常需要数百个episode和数分钟至数小时的执行时间。评测15个前沿模型发现，最强模型在部分奖励阈值0.95下仅达15.2% pass@1，在完美奖励阈值1.0下为10.9%，平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%，揭示了长时域终端智能体的巨大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准测试专注于几分钟内完成的简单任务，仅通过最终结果评估，忽略了中间进度和部分解决方案，导致奖励信号稀疏，无法全面反映智能体能力。因此，需要建立一个能够评估长时域、开放式工作流中智能体渐进式表现的新基准。

Method: 构建包含46个长时域任务的基准，任务分为9个类别；每个任务遵循Terminal-Bench风格设置，但进一步分解为细粒度分级子任务，从而提供密集的中期奖励和部分评分；任务设计强调长时域规划、长上下文管理和迭代调试而非一次性求解。

Result: 15个模型平均每任务消耗990万token、约231个episode和85.3分钟执行时间。最强模型在0.95部分奖励阈值下pass@1为15.2%，1.0完美阈值下为10.9%；所有模型平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%。

Conclusion: 目前最强模型在长时域终端任务上表现仍不理想，存在显著改进空间；通过分析失败模式和错误模式，并开源LHTB基准，支持未来长时域终端智能体的研究进展。

Abstract: AI agents have become capable of autonomously completing short, well-specified tasks. However, existing terminal benchmarks largely focus on simple problems that finish within minutes and are evaluated only by their final outcome. This setup overlooks intermediate progress and partial solutions, yielding sparse reward signals and an incomplete picture of agent capability. We introduce Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench, a terminal benchmark of 46 long-horizon tasks spanning nine categories, including experiment reproduction, software engineering, multimodal analysis, interactive games, and scientific computing. Each task follows a Terminal-Bench-style setup with a reference solution or simulation engine, but is further decomposed into fine-grained graded subtasks. This design enables dense intermediate rewards and partial credit, allowing evaluation to capture not only whether an agent reaches the final goal, but also how far it progresses on open-ended workflows. Tasks in Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench typically require hundreds of episodes and minutes to hours of execution, stressing long-horizon planning, long-context management, and iterative debugging rather than one-shot problem solving. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that agents consume on average 9.9M tokens per task, with roughly 231 episodes and 85.3 minutes of execution time per run, making Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench more demanding than prior terminal-based benchmarks. Even the strongest tested model achieves 15.2% pass@1 at a partial-reward threshold of 0.95 and 10.9% at a perfect-reward threshold of 1.0, while the mean pass rate across models is 4.3% and 1.7% under the two thresholds, respectively. These results reveal headroom for improvement. We further analyze failure modes and error patterns, and release Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench to support future progress on long-horizon terminal agents.

</details>


### [206] [A Formalization of the Mean-Field Derivation of the Vlasov Equation: AI-Assisted Lean Formalization as a Strategy Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08986)
*Joseph K. Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让数学家指导AI系统，将非线性Vlasov方程的适定性证明形式化到Lean 4证明助手中，将其视为一个形式化游戏。结果包括一个完整、公理清洁的形式化开发，其中最优传输机制（Wasserstein-1度量与Kantorovich-Rubinstein对偶定理）作为独立层可被Mathlib库吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种方法论框架：由数学家主导定义范围、分解策略和填补库缺口，而由AI代理执行证明书写，将形式化验证活动游戏化，以降低形式化的门槛并提高效率。

Method: 采用形式化游戏框架，目标是将LaTeX文档转化为Lean 4代码。游戏获胜条件：编译通过、无sorry、机器检查证明目标定理仅依赖Lean基础公理。引入"可重用性"作为第二重检查标准，定义自包含的通用数学层。案例为非线性Vlasov方程的适定性（存在性、唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限及Lagrangian弱解的短时叠加原理）。

Result: 完整的形式化开发完成；最优传输机制（49个声明，约总开发量的1/6）构成自包含层，通过22个声明的接口可独立编译于Mathlib且无反向依赖；头条定理约一周完成，全部开发约一个月。

Conclusion: 该方法论框架不限定特定系统，旨在超越具体工具的生命周期；形式化验证了每个陈述的证明，但所写陈述是否为预期定理仍由数学家判断；定量结果作为单次游戏的观察报告，非一般性定律。

Abstract: We formalize a research result in the Lean 4 proof assistant by having a mathematician direct an AI system, and frame the activity as a formalization game. The objective is to turn a LaTeX document into Lean. The game is won when the development compiles, contains no sorry, and a machine check shows the target theorems rest on Lean's foundational axioms alone. Reuse is a second check, by a definition we introduce: whether the development yields a self-contained layer of general mathematics the wider library could absorb. The case study is a complete, axiom-clean formalization of well-posedness for the nonlinear Vlasov equation via Dobrushin's mean-field route -- existence, uniqueness, the stability estimate and mean-field limit, and a short-window superposition principle (weak solutions are Lagrangian). The human's role was to direct, not to write proofs: to scope the definitions, steer the decompositions, and triage the library's gaps; the AI agent executed. The formalization certifies the proof of each statement as written; whether the written statement is the intended theorem stays the mathematician's judgment. The optimal-transport machinery that fell out of the build (in particular, properties of the Wasserstein-1 metric and the Kantorovich-Rubinstein duality theorem) separates into a self-contained layer that compiles against Mathlib alone: about a sixth of the development (49 of 299 declarations), behind a 22-declaration interface with no reverse dependency. The headline theorems ran in about a week, the full development in about a month. We report the quantitative claims as observations of one game, not as general laws. The game's rules name no particular system, so the methodological framing is meant to outlast the tools of any one run.

</details>


### [207] [ARCANA: A Reflective Multi-Agent Program Synthesis Framework for ARC-AGI-2 Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09059)
*Kunbo Zhang,Lei Fu,Zeyu Wang,Zijing Liu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCANA是一个协作式多智能体框架，通过感知、假设生成、符号执行和反思改进四个循环阶段，在严格的时间和硬件约束下解决ARC AGI 2抽象推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的测试时间和硬件资源限制下，现有方法难以高效解决ARC AGI 2中的抽象变换任务，需要一种结合结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正的协同框架来提升推理效率和解决方案质量。

Method: 框架包含四个核心智能体：感知基础智能体从原始网格构建以对象为中心的场景图；潜在程序策略智能体生成多样化的DSL程序；符号执行器在演示样本上验证候选程序；反思智能体综合失败驱动的反馈以指导下一轮改进。智能体通过可微黑板共享信息，并由学习到的元控制器调度。

Result: 在挑战性抽象变换任务上，该设计提升了推理效率和解决方案质量。

Conclusion: 结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正相结合的方法，能有效提升多智能体系统在资源受限条件下求解抽象推理问题的能力。

Abstract: We present ARCANA, a collaborative multi agent framework for solving ARC AGI 2 tasks under strict test time and hardware constraints. ARCANA decomposes each task into iterative perception, hypothesis generation, symbolic execution, and reflective refinement. A perceptual grounding agent builds object centric scene graphs from raw grids, a latent program policy proposes diverse DSL programs, a symbolic executor verifies candidates on demonstrations, and a reflective agent synthesizes failure driven feedback for the next turn. These agents communicate through a shared differentiable blackboard and are scheduled by a learned meta controller. The design combines structured program search with adaptive multi turn correction, improving reasoning efficiency and solution quality on challenging abstract transformation tasks.

</details>


### [208] [Neuro-Agentic Control: A Deep Learning-based LLM-Powered Agentic AI Framework for Controlling Security Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09076)
*Saroj Gopali,Bipin Chhetri,Deepika Giri,Sima Siami-Namini,Akbar Siami Namin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经-智能体控制框架，将LLM规划器（如Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite）与时间序列基础模型（TimesFM）结合，通过“反事实物理注入”机制在物理驱动下进行自主防御。在SWaT工业数据集上，该框架优于LSTM和TCN基线，实现了零物理无效动作执行，有效防止了33.3%的阈值违规事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网环境中，针对操作技术的网络攻击日益增多，导致昂贵的停机时间和物理损坏。传统基于规则的监控方法存在局限性，而LLM虽具备语义推理能力，但其幻觉特性在闭环控制中带来不可接受的安全隐患。

Method: 提出神经-智能体控制框架（Neuro-Agentic Control Framework），该架构将LLM规划器与预训练时间序列基础模型（TimesFM）耦合。核心机制为“反事实物理注入”（Counterfactual Physics Injection），即在执行前在基础模型的数值潜在空间中模拟LLM提议干预的物理影响，从而拒绝幻觉或不安全动作。

Result: 在安全水处理（SWaT）工业数据集上的随机攻击场景评估中，该框架优于LSTM和TCN基线。神经-智能体循环在阈值以下阻止了5次违规（33.3%），而LSTM为26.7%，TCN为13.3%。实现了零物理无效（幻觉）动作执行。

Conclusion: 证明了将基础模型作为确定性“哨兵”（Sentinels）来保护关键基础设施中智能体AI的有效性，为LLM在工业控制中的安全应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cyberattacks on operational technology are increasingly causing costly downtime and physical damage, exposing the limitations of traditional rule-based monitoring in industrial IoT environments. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have strong semantic reasoning abilities to assist in decision support, their hallucinatory nature presents unacceptable safety liabilities for closed-loop control. This paper introduces a neuro-agentic control framework, a novel architecture that couples an LLM-based planner (i.e., such as Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite) with a pre-trained Time-Series Foundation Model (TimesFM), to achieve physics-grounded autonomous defense. The paper introduces a ``Counterfactual Physics Injection'' mechanism that simulates the impact of LLM-proposed interventions within the numerical latent space of the foundation model before actuation, while allowing the system to reject hallucinatory or unsafe actions. Evaluated on an industrial dataset (e.g., the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT)) in the context of stochastic attack scenarios, the framework exhibited better performance compared to LSTM and TCN baselines. The Neuro-Agentic Loop prevented five breaches (33.3%) below the threshold versus LSTM (26.7%) and TCN (13.3%), with zero physically invalid (hallucinated) actions executed. These results demonstrate the efficacy of using foundation models as deterministic ``Sentinels'' to safeguard agentic AI in critical infrastructure.

</details>


### [209] [L-MAD: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Agent Debate Structures in Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09099)
*Tan-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Trung Nguyen,Huu-Dong Nguyen,Dinh-Truong Do,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出法律多智能体辩论框架(L-MAD)，通过赋予智能体不同专家角色进行辩论，在法律文本蕴含任务中相比强单智能体基线提升高达8%。研究发现：增加智能体数量可降低不一致性并提升准确率，但延长讨论轮次会导致"过度 deliberation漂移"，即智能体相互强化错误。这揭示了在高风险法律推理中部署协作式多智能体系统的边界与安全空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多智能体辩论框架在通用推理任务中展现出潜力，但其在高结构化、知识密集的法律领域中的有效性尚未被充分探索。作者旨在系统评估不同辩论结构和聚合方法在法律文本蕴含任务中的表现。

Method: 提出Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD)框架，为多个智能体分配不同的专家角色(personas)，在Legal Textual Entailment任务中系统比较不同的辩论结构和聚合方法。

Result: L-MAD相比强单智能体基线提升高达8%。增加智能体数量可降低不一致性并提升准确率；但延长讨论轮次会导致"过度 deliberation漂移"(over-deliberation drift)，即智能体相互强化错误。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在高风险法律推理环境中部署协作式多智能体系统的实践边界与安全限制，指出智能体数量与讨论轮次之间存在权衡关系。

Abstract: While multi-agent debate (MAD) frameworks have shown significant potential in general reasoning, their effectiveness in highly structured, knowledge-heavy legal domains remains under-explored. In this work, we introduce the Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD) framework to systematically evaluate different debate structures and aggregation methods within Legal Textual Entailment. By assigning distinct expert personas to multiple agents, L-MAD improves upon strong single-agent baselines by up to 8\%. Furthermore, analyzing how debate scales reveals a clear trade-off: increasing the agent population reduces inconsistency and improves accuracy, whereas extending discussion rounds induces a detrimental \textit{over-deliberation drift} where agents reinforce each other's mistakes. Ultimately, our findings outline the practical boundaries and safety margins of deploying collaborative multi-agent systems in high-stakes legal reasoning environments.

</details>


### [210] [MedRealMM: A Real-World Multimodal Benchmark for Chinese Online Medical Consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09142)
*Runhan Shi,Quan Zhou,Yuqian Xu,Shuai Yang,Xin Wu,Zitong Zhou,Hui Liu,Bin Cha,Zheming Wang,Liya Li,Wei Wei,Haoyuan Hu,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedRealMM是一个基于真实医患对话的大规模多模态在线医疗咨询基准，包含5,620个真实病例（覆盖64个科室），通过MCCP框架提取临床关键节点并转化为下一回复生成任务，配备医生制定的案例级评分标准。评估19个模型发现：图像信息对临床性能至关重要，前沿模型在正面标准上可媲美甚至超越医生，但在安全性错误规避方面仍存在显著瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型医疗评估基准存在三大缺陷：（1）依赖合成对话或患者模拟器，脱离真实临床场景；（2）忽略患者上传的医学图像这一多模态信息；（3）使用多项选择或词法重叠等指标评估开放式临床回复，无法反映真实的临床质量。因此需要构建一个更贴近真实临床实践的基准。

Method: （1）从中国全国性互联网医院收集脱敏的真实医患对话；（2）提出多模态临床关键点（MCCP）提取框架，识别对话中临床要求高的时刻，并将其标准化为"下一回复生成"任务，保留前文文本-图像上下文；（3）为每个实例配备由医生制定的案例特定评分标准，奖励临床合理行为，惩罚不安全、无依据或矛盾的回复；（4）在5,620个多模态病例（64个科室）上评估19个通用和医学专用LLM（含纯文本和多模态系统）。

Result: （1）图像信息对可靠临床性能至关重要；（2）当前前沿模型在整体表现上仍低于在线医生回复水平；（3）部分前沿模型在满足正面临床标准数量上可与医生媲美甚至超越，但触发的负面标准更多，说明安全性错误避免仍是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: MedRealMM为真实世界在线医疗咨询中的多模态医学推理评估提供了一个现实且可复现的基准，揭示了当前模型在临床安全性和错误避免方面的主要局限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in online medical consultation, yet existing benchmarks remain poorly aligned with real clinical practice. Many rely on synthetic conversations or patient simulators, omit patient-uploaded medical images, or evaluate open-ended clinical responses using multiple-choice or lexical-overlap metrics that poorly reflect clinical quality. We introduce \textbf{MedRealMM}, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal online medical consultation built from de-identified patient-doctor interactions collected from a nationwide Chinese internet hospital. MedRealMM uses a Multimodal Clinical Challenge Point (MCCP) extraction framework to identify clinically demanding moments in authentic consultation trajectories and converts each into a standardized next-response generation task while preserving the preceding text-image context. Each instance is paired with a case-specific rubric refined by physicians that rewards clinically desirable behaviors and penalizes unsafe, unsupported, or contradictory responses. The current release contains 5,620 real-world multimodal cases spanning 64 clinical departments. We evaluate 19 general-purpose and medical-specialized LLMs, including text-only and multimodal systems. Our results show that image information is critical for reliable clinical performance and that current frontier models remain below the online physician response. Although some frontier models satisfy as many or more positive clinical criteria than physicians, they trigger more negative criteria, indicating that safety-sensitive error avoidance remains a central bottleneck. MedRealMM offers a realistic and reproducible benchmark for evaluating multimodal medical reasoning in real-world online consultation. The dataset will be publicly available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jdh-algo/MedRealMM.

</details>


### [211] [KV-PRM: Efficient Process Reward Modeling via KV-Cache Transfer for Multi-Agent Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09153)
*Peng Kuang,Haibo Jin,Xiaoyu Han,Yanli Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Ye Yu,Kaidi Xu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-PRM，一种高效的过程奖励模型，通过直接利用LLM生成阶段产生的KV缓存替代文本重编码，将评分成本从O(L²)降至O(L)，在MATH、GSM8K和AIME基准上匹配或超越文本PRM性能，同时实现高达5000倍FLOPs减少、37倍延迟降低和34倍内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的PRM在对长序列轨迹评分时，需从头重新编码整个轨迹文本，计算成本随序列长度L呈二次增长(O(L²))，在长上下文多智能体场景中造成严重计算瓶颈，限制了PRM的实际应用。

Method: KV-PRM直接读取LLM生成阶段自然产生的KV缓存，通过处理单个"验证令牌"与已有KV缓存的交互进行奖励建模，理论上证明了KV缓存包含比文本更丰富的信息容量，将评分复杂度从O(L²)降低至O(L)。

Result: 在MATH、GSM8K和AIME基准上，KV-PRM在Beam Search、MCTS和Weighted Voting等多种测试时缩放方法下匹配或严格优于文本PRM；实现高达5000倍评分FLOPs减少、37倍延迟降低和34倍每序列内存占用减少。

Conclusion: KV-PRM通过利用KV缓存替代文本编码，在保持甚至提升过程奖励模型性能的同时，大幅提升了计算效率，为长上下文场景下PRM的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) have been proven to be highly effective in guiding test-time scaling (TTS) methods, which significantly boost the capabilities of LLM-based multi-agent systems. However, existing PRMs are text-based: they re-encode the entire trajectory text from scratch. In long multi-agent rollouts, the scoring cost, growing quadratically with respect to sequence length L, creates a severe computational bottleneck, severely limiting PRMs' application in long-context scenarios. To resolve this, we introduce KV-PRM, a highly efficient process reward model that eliminates the heavy text re-encoding by directly reading the KV cache produced naturally during the LLM's generation phase. By processing a single "verify token" against the pre-existing KV cache, KV-PRM reduces the scoring cost from O(L^2) to O(L). We formally prove that the KV cache contains strictly greater information capacity than text, and is more efficient for downstream reward modeling. Empirically, across the MATH, GSM8K, and AIME benchmarks, KV-PRM matches or strictly outperforms text-PRMs under various TTS methods such as Beam Search, MCTS, and Weighted Voting, with up to a 5,000x reduction in scoring FLOPs, a 37x reduction in latency, and a 34x reduction in per-sequence memory footprint compared to text-based PRMs.

</details>


### [212] [Scoped Verification for Reliable Long-Horizon Agentic Context Evolution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09175)
*Dan C. Hsu,Luke Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GRACE方法，将LLM agent的系统指令维护为类型化语义图，通过局部邻域验证实现图更新，再重建为文本指令。在电信agent任务中，pass^3从0.091提升至0.673，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期演化中，扁平文本形式的系统级指令维护会导致指令积累、交互复杂，使得验证越来越困难。

Method: GRACE：将持久指令组件维护为类型化语义图，在修改节点的局部类型化邻域内验证提议的更新，接受的图更新通过增量编辑重建为部署时使用的文本指令检查点。

Result: 在固定电信agent框架下，五轮独立重复实验中，GRACE将pass^3从Gemini 2.5 Flash零样本的0.091提升至最终检查点的0.673±0.136，超过Gemini 3.1 Pro零样本的0.242，而扁平文本HCE基线仅为0.191±0.051。

Conclusion: 可靠的长周期上下文演化需要两个条件：使验证局部化的结构基板，以及保持积累指令内容可用性的整合机制。

Abstract: Deployed LLM agents rely on agentic context, the model-external textual control content assembled by an operational harness. In this work, the mutable component of that context is a persistent system-level instruction that is updated from operational experience while the model, tools, and harness remain fixed. Over long evolution horizons, flat-text maintenance makes verification increasingly difficult as accumulated instructions grow and interact. We propose Graph-Regularized Agentic Context Evolution (GRACE), which maintains the persistent instruction component as a typed semantic graph and validates proposed updates within the local typed neighborhoods of modified nodes. Accepted graph updates are reconstructed as incremental edits to the textual instruction checkpoint used at deployment. We evaluate GRACE within a fixed telecom agent harness derived from $τ^2$-bench under a controlled distribution-shift protocol. Across five independent replications, GRACE improves strict reliability, measured by pass^3, from the Gemini 2.5 Flash zero-shot value of 0.091 to 0.673$\pm$0.136 at the final checkpoint. This exceeds a Gemini 3.1 Pro zero-shot reference of 0.242 on the same held-out set, while the flat-text HCE baseline finishes at 0.191$\pm$0.051. These results identify two requirements for reliable long-horizon context evolution, a structural substrate that makes verification local and a consolidation mechanism that keeps accumulated instruction content usable.

</details>


### [213] [Toward Auditable AI Scientists: A Hypothesis Evolution Protocol for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09195)
*Izumi Takahara,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出假设演化协议（HEP），一种将假设生成、评估和演化作为显式、可审计操作的智能体框架，使LLM智能体在材料科学研究中能够执行完整的假设-测试-证据-信念循环，并支持人类研究者审计推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在科学发现中虽能提出假设、进行测试并更新信念，但这些过程隐藏在非结构化日志中，缺乏让智能体或人类研究者审计该过程的机制，影响了AI科学发现的可信度和可复现性。

Method: 提出假设演化协议（HEP），将假设生成、评估和演化为显式、可审计的操作，构建假设-测试-证据-信念的完整循环，并将其集成到LLM智能体中。

Result: 在材料科学研究任务上，配备HEP的智能体具备规划型智能体所缺乏的完整科学推理循环，能够跨研究问题泛化，并且随着基础LLM能力增强，对协议利用更充分。

Conclusion: HEP标志着向可审计AI科学家的迈进，其科学推理过程可被检查、验证和在此基础上构建。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to play a central role in AI-driven scientific discovery. Equipped with broad knowledge, flexible reasoning, and tool use, they have the potential to autonomously explore and solve scientific problems by repeatedly proposing hypotheses, testing them, and revising their beliefs in the light of the evidence. In current agents, however, these hypotheses, tests, and belief updates are buried in unstructured logs, and no mechanism lets the agent or the human researcher audit that process. Here we propose the Hypothesis Evolution Protocol (HEP), an agent harness that provides hypothesis generation, evaluation, and evolution as explicit, auditable operations. On materials-science research tasks, a HEP-equipped agent operates the hypothesis--test--evidence--belief cycle that planning-style agents lack, generalizes across research questions, and exploits the protocol more fully as the base LLM becomes more capable. These results mark a step toward auditable AI scientists, whose scientific reasoning can be inspected, verified, and built upon.

</details>


### [214] [OpenProver: Agentic and Interactive Theorem Proving with Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09217)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OpenProver，一个开源的、基于大语言模型的自动化定理证明系统，采用Planner-Worker-Verifier架构并集成Lean 4形式化验证，支持人类交互式证明搜索和可复现的定量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动LLM驱动的自动化定理证明系统的开源生态发展，通过集成形式化验证实现可复现评估，并支持人类-AI协同的交互式证明搜索。

Method: 采用Planner-Worker-Verifier架构：Planner代理维护紧凑的Whiteboard暂存区和无界Repository存储中间发现，将数学工作分解为并行的Worker任务；集成Lean 4实现自动形式化验证；提供交互式终端界面支持人工引导证明搜索。

Result: 在ProofNet基准上进行了定量消融实验评估，并与简单基线方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: OpenProver作为一个完全开源的系统，通过自动形式化验证实现了可复现的评估，并且交互模式支持人类-AI协同，为自动化定理证明领域的定量消融研究提供了平台。

Abstract: In this system paper, we present OpenProver, an open-source system for LLM-driven automated theorem proving (ATP) with integrated Lean 4 formal verification. OpenProver integrates a Planner-Worker-Verifier architecture inspired by recent ATP agentic systems such as Aletheia. A Planner agent maintains a compact Whiteboard scratchpad and an unbounded Repository of intermediate findings, and decomposes mathematical work into parallel Workers.
  OpenProver is fully open-source, offers reproducible evaluation through automatic formal verification of generated proofs, and provides an interactive terminal interface for human-guided proof search. In interactive mode, OpenProver allows the human operator to monitor and steer the proof search process, motivated by the established human-AI synergy in interactive code generation.
  To showcase the potential for quantitative ablation experiments enabled by automatic formal verification, we evaluate OpenProver on ProofNet and compare it with a simple baseline. OpenProver is publicly available at https://github.com/kripner/OpenProver.

</details>


### [215] [LongMedBench: Benchmarking Medical Agents for Long-Horizon Clinical Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09322)
*Yanzhen Chen,Zihan Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Zhiting Fan,Weiqi Zhai,Hongxia Xu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongMedBench是一个基于真实EHR数据的长期临床决策基准，包含335名患者，平均每名患者19.72次住院、每次住院44.91个医疗事件，通过可复现的MIMIC-IV数据流水线构建。提出包含事实QA、时间推理、长期决策三个维度的评估体系，实验发现LLM能利用显式时间戳但难以处理隐式时间推理，RAG和记忆系统提升检索任务但决策任务高度依赖模型即时上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗智能体评估多聚焦于短上下文知识问答和工具使用，而实际临床医疗本质上是纵向的，医生需要跨多次就诊、检查、治疗变化进行证据聚合，因此需要长期交互能力评估的基准。

Method: 通过可复现流水线将MIMIC-IV住院记录和临床笔记集成到时间序列事件流和长上下文记忆数据集中，构建包含335名患者的LongMedBench基准；提出涵盖事实QA、时间推理、长期决策三个维度的评估分类体系。

Result: 近期LLM能有效利用显式时间戳，但在隐式时间推理上存在困难；RAG和智能体记忆系统能提升信息检索任务性能，但决策任务性能高度依赖于模型的即时上下文。

Conclusion: 长期临床决策评估需要专门的基准，现有LLM在时间推理能力上仍有不足，不同任务类型对记忆和上下文依赖程度不同，为未来医疗智能体的评估和发展提供了方向。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce LongMedBench, a real-world EHR-based benchmark for long-horizon clinical decision-making. Prior evaluations of LLM-based medical agents have largely emphasized short-context knowledge QA and tool use. However, real-world medical care is inherently longitudinal, and clinicians must aggregate evidence across repeated visits, tests, and evolving treatments. Therefore, long-horizon interaction is essential for realistic assessment. LongMedBench is constructed via a reproducible pipeline that integrates MIMIC-IV admission records and clinical notes into time-series event streams and long-context memory datasets, enabling long-horizon, multi-session interactions between agents and a clinical environment. It comprises 335 patients, with 19.72 inpatient visits per patient on average and 44.91 medical events per visit. Guided by the long-horizon decision process, we propose an evaluation taxonomy with three suites: fact-based QA, temporal reasoning, and long-horizon decision-making. This taxonomy measures how agents understand and leverage historical patient information over extended horizons. Our experiments show that while recent LLMs can make good use of explicit timestamps, they have challenges in implicit time inference; The RAG and agent memory system can improve the performance of information retrieval tasks, but the performance of decision-making tasks is highly dependent on the model's immediate context.

</details>


### [216] [Communication-Efficient Digital-Twin Coordination for Heterogeneous LLM Embodied Agents over Computing Power Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09330)
*Nuocheng Yang,Sihua Wang,Zihan Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek,Changchuan Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LDT-Coord框架，基于轻量级数字孪生实现异构LLM智能体团队的高效协调，通过结构化动作约束和规则协调器替代自然语言多轮对话，结合C-POMDP公式化与PPO-Lagrangian算法优化通信开销，在保持任务成功率的同时将通信开销降低70倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构LLM智能体协调框架依赖多轮自然语言对话，导致三个耦合挑战：1）智能体间对话通信开销随团队规模快速增长；2）协调质量受限于智能体LLM的异构能力；3）反复协商导致动作延迟。

Method: 1）每个智能体独立选择意图动作，向DT服务器报告动作决策和结构化时间约束，将协调性能与自然语言推理能力解耦；2）DT执行无训练、基于规则的协调器算法解决跨智能体冲突；3）将智能体报告控制建模为约束部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(C-POMDP)，用PPO-Lagrangian算法求解。

Result: LDT-Coord在任务成功率上与常规协调方法相当，同时将通信开销降低70倍以上，并在LLM异构性下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于轻量级数字孪生和结构化约束的协调框架能有效解决异构LLM智能体团队在有限网络资源下的协调问题，显著降低通信开销且不影响任务性能。

Abstract: Embodied agent teams powered by heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) are being widely deployed in physical artificial intelligence such as smart factories, warehouses, and service robotics. To enable collaboration among such an agent team, efficient coordination mechanisms that operate reliably under limited network resources are required. However, existing heterogeneous LLM-agent coordination frameworks that rely on multi-round natural-language-based conversations introduce three coupled challenges. First, inter-agent dialogue incurs communication overhead that grows rapidly with team size. Second, the quality of coordination is constrained by the heterogeneous capabilities of the agent team's LLMs. Third, agents may suffer from action delays due to iterative negotiation. To address these challenges, we propose LDT-Coord, a networked coordination framework built upon a lightweight digital twin (DT). Specifically, each agent independently selects its intended action and reports both the action decision and a structured temporal constraint over shared resources to the DT server, thereby decoupling coordination performance from natural-language reasoning ability. Then, DT executes a training-free, rule-based orchestrator algorithm to resolve cross-agent conflicts and returns coordination instructions to prevent such conflicts. To further reduce communication overhead, we formulate agent reporting control as a constrained partially observable Markov decision process (C-POMDP) and solve it with the PPO-Lagrangian algorithm. Simulation results show that LDT-Coord achieves a task success rate comparable to conventional coordination methods while reducing communication overhead by more than 70x and maintaining robustness under LLM heterogeneity.

</details>


### [217] [Fictional Worldbuilding: Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration with Hierarchical Context Compression and Iterative Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09403)
*Jingbo Chen,He Wang,Wei Yuan,Yuqiao Lai,Zhenyan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoWorldBuilder是一个多智能体协作系统，通过结构化概念网络、DAG调度、四层上下文压缩（~90% token缩减）、迭代审核机制（提案通过率从42%提升至85%+）和可扩展技能驱动架构，解决了LLM应用于世界构建时的上下文爆炸、创意多样性与一致性矛盾、缺乏自动化质量保证三大挑战。在两个LLM后端、20个任务中达到95%成功率，每个世界生成56-103个自洽概念，零冲突交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动世界构建中面临三个核心挑战：1) 构建过程中上下文线性增长导致的上下文爆炸；2) 创意多样性与内容一致性之间的张力；3) 缺乏自动化质量保证机制。

Method: 提出AutoWorldBuilder多智能体协作系统，包含五个组件：1) 带冲突检测的结构化概念网络；2) 基于语义局部性的DAG混合批量调度器；3) 实现约90% token缩减的四层上下文压缩机制；4) 含专门Auditor智能体的迭代审核系统（提案通过率42%→85%+）；5) 支持零代码扩展和差异化温度配置的技能驱动智能体架构。

Result: 在GPT-OSS 120B和DeepSeek v3.2两个LLM后端上，20个多样化世界构建任务中实现95.0%成功率。每个世界生成56-103个自洽概念，耗时18-31分钟，零冲突交付。

Conclusion: 验证了三种可迁移的架构模式：layer-as-budget压缩、semantic-locality调度、生成与审核分离，这些模式适用于更广泛的知识密集型多智能体LLM应用。

Abstract: Worldbuilding, the construction of coherent fictional worlds, is a foundational task in game design and literary creation. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for automated content generation, but their application to worldbuilding faces three challenges: context explosion that grows linearly with the building process, the tension between creative diversity and content consistency, and the absence of automated quality assurance. This paper presents AutoWorldBuilder, a multi-agent collaborative system that addresses these challenges through five integrated components: a structured concept network with conflict detection; a DAG-based hybrid batch scheduler that groups tasks by semantic locality; a four-layer context compression mechanism achieving approximately 90% token reduction; an iterative review system with specialized Auditor agents that improves proposal pass rates from 42% to over 85%; and a skill-driven agent architecture supporting zero-code extension with differentiated temperature configuration. Two experiments across 20 diverse worldbuilding tasks, using GPT-OSS 120B and DeepSeek v3.2 as LLM backends, demonstrate a 95.0% success rate. The system generated 56-103 self-consistent concepts per world in 18-31 minutes with zero-conflict delivery. The architectural patterns validated here, including layer-as-budget compression, semantic-locality scheduling, and separation of generation and review, transfer to the broader class of knowledge-intensive, multi-agent LLM applications.

</details>


### [218] [How Does Bayesian Causal Discovery Fail? Characterising Structural Consequences in Linear Gaussian Networks under Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09449)
*Debargha Ghosh,Silja Renooij,Anna Kononova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性高斯因果模型中潜在混杂因素对贝叶斯因果发现后验分布的影响，推导出关键相关性阈值，表明该阈值随样本量增加而降低，并识别出两种后验失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅指出潜在混杂会破坏因果可识别性，但缺乏对后验分布如何受混杂影响的系统性刻画。

Method: 针对线性高斯因果模型，分析恰好两个观测变量间的加性潜在混杂，推导评分函数偏好含虚假边图结构的相关性阈值，并通过精确后验计算验证。

Result: 推导出关键相关性阈值，发现该阈值随样本量增加而降低；超过阈值后，根据混淆变量局部结构的不同出现两种不同的后验失效模式。

Conclusion: 潜在混杂会导致贝叶斯因果发现的后验分布出现系统性偏差，且更多数据反而加剧问题，而非缓解。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery is widely used for its ability to quantify epistemic uncertainty over directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) through posterior inference. However, its behaviour under latent confounding remains poorly understood, as existing work typically notes that confounding breaks identifiability without characterising how the posterior distribution over DAGs responds. In this work, we analyse posterior behaviour under latent confounding in linear Gaussian causal models, focusing on additive latent confounding between exactly two observed variables. We derive a critical correlation threshold above which the score function favours graphs with a spurious edge between the confounded variables, and show that this threshold decreases with sample size -- more data lowers the correlation required for the spurious edge to be favoured. Beyond this threshold, we characterize two distinct posterior failure regimes determined by the local structure around the confounded variables. Our findings are supported by exact posterior computations on multiple graph structures, demonstrating both the predicted failure regimes.

</details>


### [219] [ProofCouncil: An LLM Agent for Solving Open Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09474)
*Johannes Schmitt,Tim Gehrunger,Jasper Dekoninck,Gergely Bérczi,Uri Kreitner,Liam Price,David Holmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProofCouncil是一个基于作者-评论者架构的数学智能体，能够在开放数学问题中自主生成可验证的证明，在FirstProof挑战中表现最佳（10个问题中6个仅需少量修订），并在30个开放问题上取得显著成果（5个完全正确，2个待验证，8个提供有用进展）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在解决开放数学问题方面展现出潜力，但通过适配真实数学实践的智能体工作流可以进一步提升其性能。作者旨在开发一个能够自主解决真实世界开放数学问题的智能体系统。

Method: 采用作者-评论者架构（author-critic architecture），构建数学智能体ProofCouncil。该智能体能够自主生成数学证明并接受批评性评审，形成迭代改进的闭环。同时，作者开发并开源了用于构建该智能体的Agent库。

Result: 在FirstProof挑战第二批次10个真实数学问题中，ProofCouncil有6个提交被评审认定为仅需少量修订即可正确，表现优于所有参赛团队。在30个来自数学研究者的开放问题中，21个获得人类反馈：其中5个完全正确，2个被认为有前景但需最终验证，8个包含有用的部分进展。

Conclusion: ProofCouncil展示了LLM驱动的智能体在解决真实开放数学问题上的有效性和潜力。作者将构建该智能体的库开源，以促进社区的进一步研究和应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in solving open problems in mathematics. However, their performance can be further improved through agentic workflows tailored to real-world mathematical practice. To this end, we introduce ProofCouncil, a mathematical agent that is designed to tackle open problems using an author-critic architecture. ProofCouncil served as a submission to the second batch of FirstProof, a challenge consisting of 10 real-world mathematical problems that agents must solve autonomously. Its submissions for 6 of the 10 problems were judged by the referees to be correct up to at most minor revisions, showing the best performance among participating teams. We also evaluate ProofCouncil on 30 open problems collected from mathematical researchers. Among the 21 solutions that received human feedback, 5 were judged completely correct, 2 more were judged promising pending final verification, and a further 8 contained useful partial progress. In this short paper, we describe the development of ProofCouncil and the agent-building library used to create it, which we release as open source to the community.

</details>


### [220] [Ceci n'est pas une pipe: AI systems as semantic abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09489)
*Jade Alglave,Patrick Cousot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语义框架，用于描述AI系统的输出并非事实或世界状态，而是工程化的表征。通过区分领域知识、参考来源和系统可用信息，精确定义了外推、被反驳/无支持的断言、来源与知识不匹配、过时/被反驳的来源、额外假设、无依据使用等常见失败模式，为依赖可靠声明和明确权威（而非表面流畅性）的AI系统提供规范的验证词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
